## Reconciled R1 Splits & Active Property IDs
- `P01` -> `P01a, P01b, P01c`: R1 found P01 mixes two confirmed physical invariants with a missing config guard and a transmission-restore contract; split per recommended_refinement
- `P02` -> `P02a, P02b`: R1 recommended splitting cross-boundary state-validity invariant from bounded-termination contract
- `P03` -> `P03a, P03b, P03c`: R1 recommended three enforceable contracts plus one tracked product decision
- `P04` -> `P04a, P04b, P04c`: R1 recommended three sharper properties aligned with confirmed requirements
- `P05` -> `P05a, P05b, P05c, P05d`: R1 recommended four implementation-accurate invariants covering restore/clamp defects
- `P06` -> `P06a, P06b, P06c`: R1 recommended three orthogonal properties aligned with product scope
- `P07` -> `P07a, P07b`: R1 found P07 violated and recommended splitting probe non-interference from brake-parity staging
- `P08` -> `P08a, P08b, P08c, P08d`: R1 recommended four scope-accurate sub-invariants covering the Stepper boundary gaps
- `P10` -> `P10a, P10b, P10c, P10d`: R1 recommended four sub-invariant contracts covering deserialization/rollback gaps
- `P12` -> `P12a, P12b, P12c`: R1 recommended three independently checkable properties since the mechanisms share no code
- `P14` -> `P14a, P14b, P14c, P14d`: R1 recommended four separately-verdicted properties: two holding contracts, two real violations, one dead-code clause
- `P17` -> `P17a, P17b, P17c`: R1 recommended splitting per subclause and per frontend (bike-ride exit-130 gap)
- `P20` -> `P20a, P20b, P20c`: R1 recommended independently checkable properties; found letter-level violations in (b),(c),(d)
- `P21` -> `P21a, P21b, P21c, P21d`: R1 recommended splitting the four-component bundle into per-component properties
- `P24` -> `P24a, P24b`: R1 recommended splitting into separately verifiable claims and tightening verdict semantics
- Active for R2: `P01a` (R1): Across live CPU (`Transmission`, `crank_clutch`, `DrivetrainWriter`, MJCF joints) and GPU (`drivetrain_resistance_kernel.hpp`) drivetrains, `loss_j` carries zero modeled viscous/frictional drivetrain dissipation (soft tendon-limit `solreflimit` work is isolated in `closure_*_j`); and for `motor_clutch == true` under coherent state, `crank_clutch` never applies negative/retarding torque (`f[drive_shaft_spin] >= 0`), remains open (`torque == 0`) once unloaded during stationary/backward crank motion, and permits `AssistController` `release_hold_s` run-on to drive `drive_shaft_spin -> ideal_hub_ -> rear_wheel_spin`.
- Active for R2: `P01b` (R1): `config.drive.pedaling.reposition_on_stall == true` is rejected unless `config.drive.motor_clutch == true`, at both `bike::configuration::validate` and the wire config-validation boundary, matching `NativeRideRuntime::request_crank_reposition()` semantics and simulation-decisions ruling #44.
- Active for R2: `P01c` (R1): Every advancing `reset()`, `prepare()`, and ratio-changing `set_ratio()` establishes `model_->tendon_range[1] == *state_.boundary` (ideal mode) or `*state_.boundary - prepared->phi + dot(prepared->jacobian, prepared->qpos)` (geometric mode).
- Active for R2: `P02a` (R1): In both `decode_policies` and `PedalingPolicy::set_state`: (a') `!r.stall_elapsed_s.has_value() => (r.crank_rad == 0.0 && r.wheel_rad == 0.0)` (and `stall_update` zeroes both accumulators when arming); (b') `r.goal_rad.has_value() <=> (r.reason == 1 || r.reason == 2)`, `!(r.goal_rad.has_value() && r.cooldown_s > 0.0)`, and `r.goal_rad.has_value() => (!s.coasting && s.effort == 0.0)`; (c') `s.coasting => s.target_phase_rad.has_value()` with `deceleration_rad_s2 >= 0` and `effort >= 0`.
- Active for R2: `P02b` (R1): Within cumulative active-manoeuvre steps of duration `<= reposition_timeout_s + 0.5 * ulp(reposition_timeout_s)` (or earlier on `braking == true` or `phase - *goal_rad <= reposition_phase_tolerance_rad`), `goal_rad` disengages (`reason == 0`), `cooldown_s` is set to `reposition_cooldown_s` (disengaging `state_.reposition` iff `reposition_cooldown_s == 0 && !reposition_on_stall && armed`), and on transition to `PedalMode::pedaling` (when `!braking && effort > 0 && !excessive_cadence`) effort ramps from `previous == 0 N*m` by at most `effort_slew_nm_s * dt` whenever `effort_slew_nm_s > 0` (or equals `target` when `effort_slew_nm_s == 0`).
- Active for R2: `P03a` (R1): During active pedaling `|effort_k - effort_{k-1}| <= effort_slew_nm_s * dt` for `effort_slew_nm_s > 0`; any `PedalMode::coasting` or `PedalMode::disabled` return drops effort to `0 N*m` immediately; and `PedalingPolicy::set_state` rejects `(s.coasting || (s.reposition && s.reposition->goal_rad)) && s.effort != 0.`.
- Active for R2: `P03b` (R1): Current cadence-hysteresis coasting behavior is specified strictly as Python-oracle parity (an engineering contract), not as a physical requirement, pending the product decision on splitting `PedalingSnapshot::coasting` into cadence-cutoff latching vs coast-profile activation or retiring `stop_time_s`.
- Active for R2: `P03c` (R1): `braking == true` zeroes all `AssistSnapshot` fields; an active hold (`release_hold_left_s > 0`) caps output by `omega > 0 ? min({hold_power_w / omega, hold_torque_nm, ceiling, cap}) : 0.`; non-underflowing holds (`hold_power_w > 0`) do not re-arm after expiry while `target < state_.torque` (`DrivetrainWriter::settle()` updates `state_.torque` to solved actuator torque); and `AssistController::set_state` rejects `release_hold_left_s > 0. && (hold_power_w <= 0. || hold_torque_nm <= 0.)` and `hold_power_w > 0. && hold_torque_nm <= 0.`.
- Active for R2: `P04a` (R1): (shift_count > 0) <=> (direction != "none"); `shift_count == 0 => (cooldown_s == 0 && cut_remaining_s == 0)`; `0 <= cut_remaining_s <= cooldown_s`; `enabled => rear_teeth, from_teeth in cassette`; `shift_count == 0 => rear_teeth == from_teeth` on reset/committed trajectories; and `CadenceShifter::update` auto-band admission requires `next.cut_remaining_s <= 1e-12`.
- Active for R2: `P04b` (R1): A single `shift_cut_active(const ShiftingSnapshot&)` predicate defines the torque-cut active window at every call site (shifting update/writer paths, drivetrain writer, PhysicalStep), eliminating the `(0.0, 1e-12]` split window between call sites.
- Active for R2: `P04c` (R1): `shift_request` and `stationary_rear_teeth` are mutually exclusive; single-step upshift bridging holds across `Perception` delay (or `commands.shift_request` clears after the first physics sub-step); `shift_up == false` on exit from `accelerate`; the upshift latch fully resets on `done`/`arm`/`reset`; and `LaunchPlan::restore` enforces `request_count == 0 && request_teeth == 0 && blocked_teeth == 0 && retry_tick == -1` for `LaunchPhase::release` as it already does for `hold`.
- Active for R2: `P05a` (R1): `!config_.plan_enabled => state_.phase == LaunchPhase::idle` across construction, `arm_start()`, `update()`, and `restore()` (restore rejects a non-idle phase while the plan is disabled).
- Active for R2: `P05b` (R1): `rise_ticks` uses the `-1` sentinel (`-1` in `idle`/`done`, `< phase_ticks` in `hold`, `< delivered` in `RiderIntent::restore` when `>= 0`); `handover`/`blend` bounds enforce `launch_ticks <= max(release_ticks_, max_launch_ticks_)` and `blend_ticks <= delivered`; `SeatedPostureProgram::restore` validates `duration_s > 0`, `[0,1]` load shares, finite fields; `SeatedClimbPolicy::restore` validates `time_s >= 0`, `effort_nm >= 0`, `[0, surge_budget_j]`; and `FrontLiftReaction::restore` stages the candidate state before committing.
- Active for R2: `P05c` (R1): Effort composition claims are qualified by `config_.enabled && active && control.rider_enabled && !control.human_torque_nm.has_value()`; `torso_lean_rad` claims are qualified by `!control.posture.has_value()`; `SeatedPostureProgram::update` enforces `min(config_.max_forward_lean_rad, *lean_limit_rad)`; and `start_lean_rad`/`lean_rad_` respects `forward_limit` (or slew-rate decay from a higher initial lean is explicitly allowed).
- Active for R2: `P05d` (R1): `LaunchPlan` reaches neutral post-launch `LaunchPhase::done` (`weight == 0`, `brake == 0`, `!owns_shifting`) in at most `release_ticks_ + max(0, max_launch_ticks_ - release_ticks_) + cut_clear_ticks + handover_ticks_` intention ticks (or resets to `idle` in 1 tick when `!in.eligible`); `FrontLiftReaction` strictly progresses to `ReactionPhase::idle` under its stated assumptions.
- Active for R2: `P06a` (R1): `model->dof_damping[i] == 0.0` for all DOFs across all MJCF modes/variants; physical enforcement (`flat` pad `normal_contact >= 0`) is separated from closed-form `spindle` controller targets (`kPedalPressMarginN = 25.0 N` when `!steady_state && mean_crank_torque_nm > 0`, `>= 0 N` during coast/reposition, `coast_hold_nm_` anti-windup freeze when `raw_task + max(0, coast_hold_nm_) >= crank_press_ceiling_nm`, `|coast_hold_nm| <= weld_servo_limit_nm` clamped at decode/validate); and the `-6 N` rider-support rule is wired into `PeriodAccounting`/`ModelStatus` or scoped to ride-level acceptance gating.
- Active for R2: `P06b` (R1): `PedalRecoveryState::stage` is a closed enum (`None`, `Release`, `Escape`, `Raise`, `Return`), and `direction` is a closed sign enum `{-1, +1}` whenever `stage != None` (enforced in both `decode_pedal_recovery` and `validate_state`).
- Active for R2: `P06c` (R1): When cohesive pair release applies, reachability and trial spring forces are computed for both hands (`side = 0, 1`) before deciding pair release; on release, `qfrc`, `grip_xi_local`, and `elastic_energy_j` zero for both hands in the same step with `old[0] + old[1]` elastic energy dumped to `release_loss_j`; and `!enabled[kGripIndex] => grip_xi_local == {0, 0}` at decode/assign boundaries.
- Active for R2: `P07a` (R1): All persistent controller and step mutations are gated on `advance == true` (`commit_hold = advance`, staged/restored `command_enabled_`, `last_terms_`, `pd_split_`, `saturated_ik_`, `ik_reach_limited_`, `joint_torques_nm_`, `joint_capacity_nm_`, `allocation_memory_` scratch copy, no committed `wheel_x_m_`/`rider_state_`/`CruiseWriter` overwrites); and `probe_step_inputs` snapshots and restores `mjModel::dof_frictionloss` and `Stepper::posvel_fresh_` (or explicitly restricts model-buffer purity to `NativeRideRuntime` post-restore).
- Active for R2: `P07b` (R1): A non-mutating `peek_rollback_brake_demand(speed_mps, control)` evaluates the hysteresis guard against `rollback_hold_` without mutating it; in `apply_forces_body` with `resolved == nullptr`, `.rollback_demand = active ? peek_rollback_brake_demand(...) : 0.` so `probe_step_inputs` (active) matches `advance_physics` while equilibrium settle (inactive) is unaffected.
- Active for R2: `P08a` (R1): Calls routed through `engine::invoke` contain MuJoCo C errors on the thread-local interceptor stack without leaking across the boundary, under the trivial-frame `noexcept` `Operation` precondition and the enforced `mj_*` entry-point set.
- Active for R2: `P08b` (R1): `Stepper` fails closed: `EngineFailure` poisons subsequent `try_step`/`try_forward`/`step`/`forward`/`mutate`/`reset`/`set_state` entry points (with mutating `rider_contacts_*` helpers wrapped or documented as caller-`mutate`-responsible seams), while `ErrorKind::unsupported_callback` never poisons.
- Active for R2: `P08c` (R1): `Stepper::try_step` and `Stepper::try_forward` satisfy `BIKE_NONBLOCKING` across normal stepping and prevalidated error paths (`poisoned_`, unvetted `mjcb_*`/`mju_user_*`, `nplugin != 0`, out-of-range `opt.solver`) once `mjcb_time` is warmed.
- Active for R2: `P08d` (R1): `ControlClock` rejects `held()` while `Empty` (`std::runtime_error`) and enforces `steps = nearbyint(period_s / timestep_s) >= 1` within `|steps * timestep_s - period_s| <= 1e-9`.
- Active for R2: `P09` (R0): (a) `NativeRideRuntime` rejects concurrent/reentrant entry immediately via `AdvancementGuard` without blocking or latching `closed_` on a rejected `close()`, and mid-advance exceptions preserve `committed_step_`, `committed_integration_state_`, and `committed_presentation_` at the last completed step/period; (b) `CrashDetector` latches the first terminal physical crash cause irreversibly per episode; (c) `grace_left_s` engages on the first riding sample (`started_s` latched only while riding) and then burns in wall-clock — non-braked `!riding` windows pause the dwell timer but do NOT pause or re-arm grace; a full braked stop (`BalanceStop::braked`) re-arms grace by clearing `started_s`; the production braked-stop classification additionally requires launch-hold phase or already-spent grace, so a mid-grace braked stop cannot extend the current grace window; and the first balance-loss event latches permanently per episode while `current_event` clears on speed recovery, in-grace, or non-riding samples; and (d) `brake_latch` serializes both optional anchors through a strict codec (exact keys, null-or-finite, reject-before-commit) and `StaticBrake::restore()` applies them atomically; `PhysicalStep::restore()` deliberately does NOT apply them — it resets latches so every fresh episode re-bites — the wire preserves the field while the apply path drops it by design.
- Active for R2: `P10a` (R1): `numerically_valid` transitions `"not_evaluated" -> {true, false}`, `true -> {true, false}`, `false -> false`, evaluated iff `include_energy && find(channels, "energy") != nullptr && !object(*find(channels, "energy")).empty()`; `ModelStatus::from_wire` requires `count >= 1`, unique `counts` keys, `counts.empty() == !result.first.has_value()`, `result.counts.empty()` when `result.last_interval == -1`; and `initialize_accounting` requires `!monitor.first_failure.has_value()`.
- Active for R2: `P10b` (R1): Single-warning emission and `first_failure` latching are scoped to a single episode/`ReferenceMonitor` lifecycle; the intentional `EngineFailure` cleanup exception (trailing `InvalidReferenceRun` suppressed/captured so primary engine failures propagate after committing partial-period diagnostics) is explicit.
- Active for R2: `P10c` (R1): `AsyncWorkerLost` (lost-worker transport) auto-degrades via `detach_async()` in `collect()`/`submit_period()`; `AsyncPublicationError` (worker evaluation failure) surfaces to the caller without automatic fallback and recovers only via explicit `PeriodAccounting::detach_async()`; explicit `detach_async()` replays unacknowledged periods in order without loss or duplication; and `restore()` strips synthesized keys or copies the `raw.channels` spine so aborted partial flushes leave `RawStep::channels` untouched.
- Active for R2: `P10d` (R1): `cursor == samples_attempted` unconditionally; `delivery_time` is monotonic across `read(now)` calls within an episode (resettable via `reset()`/`import_state()`); and startup non-duplication means `cursor == 1` at `t = 0` after `reset(initial)`/`startup_sensor_state()` import.
- Active for R2: `P11` (R0): (a) At every external boundary of `NativeResearchRuntime`, `delivery_pending <=> pending_transition.has_value()`, `delivery_pending => window.has_value()`, `0 <= completed_count <= requested_count <= completed_count + 1`, and retrying `advance_control` while `delivery_pending == true` returns the identical `ResearchTransition` without side effects; (b) episode terminal states are partitioned and coherent: `!(terminated && truncated)`; `done() => !stop_requested` (a stale `stop_requested` surviving `handle_failure` is a named defect to fix, not to bless); `consume_samples(false)` folds trailing committed samples with all gates active without changing the terminal-reason partition; and non-empty `error` text is attached iff `reason` is an error outcome (`simulation_error`, `invalid_controller`, or `policy_error`, with `RuntimeError: `/`InvalidReferenceRun: ` prefixes); (c) `advance_control` without wall/step budget yields terminates in at most `window->target_step - window->start_step` physics steps; and (d) at sealed episode boundaries, `sample_cursor` vs `committed_step_` alignment is well-defined across mid-period `finish`/`numerical_quality` stops.
- Active for R2: `P12a` (R1): `measure_run` terminates in at most `max_wall_clock_s + one compute_slice_s + flush/export`, and driver teardown closes exactly once when `factory()` returns non-null.
- Active for R2: `P12b` (R1): Any reference-monitor violation is also a model-status violation: the load-bearing merge (`accounting.cpp` -> `status.cpp`) poisons `model_valid` even in non-strict mode because attachment/effort violations feed `model_status.counts`.
- Active for R2: `P12c` (R1): Provider discovery fails closed: a correctly-hashed but unmapped file fails `loaded_provider_images`, no escaping-symlink or wrong-backend provider is silently rescued, and `collect_provider_image` is noexcept-tolerant (no `bad_alloc` through `dl_iterate_phdr`).
- Active for R2: `P13` (R0): (a) `SetupOwner` mutating transitions are single-shot and seal-confined — `settle()` completes once, `seal_bootstrap()` succeeds exactly once after a completed finalized settle — while the observational readers `capture_initial_state`, `capture_state_file`, `project_initial_stations`, `research_initial_state`, and `metadata()` are legal from `SettledFinalized` onward including after `Sealed`; (b) `crank_hold_torque` acts exclusively while `context.initializing == true && physical.step() == 0 && pedal_attachment == "spindle" && physical.rider_present()` and never during `prepare_research_startup` or runtime episodes; (c) `refine_equilibrium` restores `DriveSnapshot`, tire/rider state, `qvel = 0`, and `root_x` on every trial evaluation, and `restore_equilibrium_state` validates all arrays/states before mutating live `Stepper` state; and (d) `settle_context`, `fit_sag`, and `write_state_file` terminate within explicit finite step/evaluation/retry bounds, no post-`seal_bootstrap()` operation writes to the equilibrium cache, and `load_equilibrium_cache` verifies the stored `fingerprint_sha256` member and re-measures residual on cache hits like the explicit path.
- Active for R2: `P14a` (R1): At most one `act` is delivered per accepted control window across budget/target yields and staged `begin_control` retries (including the staged-retry leg).
- Active for R2: `P14b` (R1): A throwing `apply` during the post-acknowledge drain consumes the command and releases the completed transition so `pending()` becomes false (failed apply journaled as a distinct kind such as `operator_apply_failed` with `applied=false`, or an explicit fault state clearing `completed` while retaining diagnostics); whether deterministic apply failures also terminate the episode is an explicit decision.
- Active for R2: `P14c` (R1): A reset that successfully creates the successor episode is journaled `operator_applied`/`applied=true` in the new episode; `applied` reflects the action's own success (post-terminal stop may legitimately record `applied=false`).
- Active for R2: `P14d` (R1): `PlaybackClock` either paces a production research-viewer path (replacing or unifying with `viewer::Pacer`) or is explicitly marked parity-dead code and dropped from evidence expectations; its seven-test suite remains the behavioral contract either way.
- Active for R2: `P15` (R0): (a) `ResearchRun` seals outgoing episodes before every plant reset, rejects resets after `finalization_attempted_` (except already-queued resets drained during `draining_`), and makes `finish()` idempotent once succeeded across both persistent and non-persistent runs while keeping failed publications retryable; (b) `PluginPolicy` passes every non-null opaque instance created by `BikePolicyV1::create` to `destroy` at most once and strictly before `dlclose`, even on creation/hash-verification failure or `destroy` throw; (c) `examples/policies/traction_control.cpp` and `builtin:traction_control` produce bit-identical `BikeControlV1` trajectories on default parameters; and (d) `PhysicalLiveCsv` locks its telemetry column schema from the first scalar `write()` row — with `write_marker()` either enforcing write-before-marker ordering (`std::invalid_argument`), deferring the schema until the first `write()`, or the ordering documented as a class contract — never silently dropping telemetry.
- Active for R2: `P16` (R0): (a) Any crash or failure prior to `platform::link_new(candidate, destination / "manifest.json")` leaves zero readable sealed recording, and no publication/export overwrites an existing path or symlink; (b) the manifest-covered payloads (`run.duckdb`, `summary.md`, `manifest.json`) are hash-protected and never rewritten after the manifest link; documented analyst sidecars (`preview.csv`, `*.png`, `ride.html`) may be created exclusively inside the published directory post-seal and are intentionally NOT manifest-covered; (c) `ResearchRecording::publish` either re-stages `capture_` on every `publish()` call while unpublished (keeping no-duplicates because the staged `unique_ptr` is discarded wholesale on failure so retries honor updated events including a late `operator_stop_deferred`), or ratifies the latch by documenting that retries must pass the identical event set while `finish()` stops appending `operator_stop_deferred` once `finalization_attempted_` is set; and (d) `RunCapture`/caller episode sealing prevents orphan `episode_started` events from poisoning earlier completed episodes in a multi-episode viewer session.
- Active for R2: `P17a` (R1): Deferred-`SIGINT` shielding holds for stack-allocated main-thread scopes, with the explicit caveats that LIFO is convention-enforced only and that >=17 nested scopes silently degrade to flag-holders.
- Active for R2: `P17b` (R1): Every CLI frontend exits 130 whenever `SIGINT` is deferred during seal/publication/artifact-writing (`interrupt.deferred()` or folded into `reason` ahead of the exit decision), matching `research.cpp`, `settle.cpp`, `replay.cpp`, `batch.cpp` behavior — including the plain `bike-ride` physical path.
- Active for R2: `P17c` (R1): A `SIGINT` deferred before the publication commit point is journaled as `operator_stop_deferred`; deferred inside `publish` is honored via `OperatorInterrupted` and not journaled; and the physical frontend emits `operator_stop_deferred` when `interrupt.deferred()` is observed while the `EpisodeJournal` is still open (matching `RECORDING.md` and Python `_journal_deferred`), or `RECORDING.md` scopes the kind to research journals only.
- Active for R2: `P18` (R0): (a) `ReplaySession::report()` produces `passed == true` iff `check_execution` passed at construction AND at `finish()`, every recorded `requested`, `applied`, optional `rider_commands`, `transitions`, and `observations` entry matched within `max(1e-9, 1e-9 * max(|a|, |e|))`, final `mjSTATE_INTEGRATION` matched within `1e-9 + 1e-9 * |expected|`, and `executable_sha256` remained unchanged — i.e., passed attests that the complete recorded-evidence battery ran and matched, including configuration, initial state+observation, outcome/event-metrics/research fields/model_status/episode metrics, applied and rider_commands completeness, and step/time alignment; (b) once any mismatch or failure occurs, `ReplaySession` halts permanently for that attempt (`FailedHalted`), never advancing `environment` again unless `restart()` is called, and rolls `frame()` back to the last verified `RenderFrame` (the validated `t = 0` `environment->render_frame()` when failure occurs on `command == 0`); and (c) `cli::replay_command` halts viewer stepping on any `session.advance()` exception and on `advance()` failure prints the session diagnostics JSON to `stdout` and exits `1` (`130` on interrupt, `2` on user-close without failure), with `REPLAY ERROR:` on `stderr` reserved for exceptions escaping the inner handling.
- Active for R2: `P19` (R0): (a) `RenderOwner::apply` is evaluation-kinematic-only — `mj_setState`+`mj_kinematics`+`mj_comPos`+`mj_camlight`+`mj_tendon` with no `mj_forward`/`mj_step`/sensor/`qfrc` evaluation — writes only the six declared mutable presentation arrays of its own replica model, enforces monotone non-decreasing lexicographic `(generation, step)` admission plus `model_sha256` identity, with all rejection staged before replica mutation; (b) `RenderOwner::close()` frees OpenGL/MuJoCo render contexts before `GlfwContext` destroys the `GLFWwindow`/`glfwTerminate()` on both normal return and exception unwind (reverse declaration order plus explicit `renderer->close()`); (c) `viewer::Pacer` preserves fixed physics `dt` across `{1, 2, 4, 8}` scales, caps `offered <= max_offer = 1e6` physics steps per poll (an offer bound, not a debt bound — debt is honest and uncapped by design), and discards stale wall debt on pause/scale/reset, while `terminal_drawn` ensures one full render pass before `close_on_terminal` exits; and (d) interactive viewer HUD legend latching and physical reset hooks preserve intended operator telemetry/journal semantics.
- Active for R2: `P20a` (R1): A reaped child can never re-enter `~ChildOwner` (`released` set at reap before any post-reap exception path, or an `armed` flag cleared at reap); `platform::run` enforces grace->SIGKILL ordering for children ignoring `SIGINT`; and the macOS `add_chdir` branch guards on `__MAC_OS_X_VERSION_MIN_REQUIRED >= 260000` (deployment target) or a runtime availability check.
- Active for R2: `P20b` (R1): `sweep` calls `cli::validate_research_arguments` after `parse_arguments` (matching `cli/batch.cpp`) or documents its reliance on per-child validation; `--jobs 0/-N` exits non-zero before output creation (same rejection `bike-batch` uses, not a `std::max(1,jobs)` clamp); and `sweep.json` records an honest worker count.

# Stage 3 (`R1`) Independent Research Report — `P01`: Drivetrain 100% Mechanical Efficiency, Unilateral Clutch Decoupling & Tendon-Boundary Restore Coherence

- **Inspected Commit:** `0502660dd14bd1021b3da90a61e20dcb1d5805a1`
- **Compliance Verdict (`R1` Agent):** `partially_violated`
- **Product Justification Verdict (`R1` Agent):** `mixed_subclauses`

## 1. Producers, Mutators, Consumers & Potential Breakers
- **Topology & State Producers:**
  - `native/src/model/drivetrain.cpp:15-58` & `native/src/model/physical_topology.cpp:12-14,47-53,79-91,113-143`: emits `crank_spin`, `pedal_{front,rear}_spin`, `rear_wheel_spin`, conditional `drive_shaft_spin` (`motor_clutch == true`), `motor_rotor` inertia, `mid_drive` actuator, and fixed tendons `ideal_mid_drive_freehub`, `crank_clutch`, `geometric_mid_drive_freehub` (`limited="true"`, `range="-1e12 0"`, `margin="0"`, `solreflimit="<closure_tau> 1"`, zero joint `damping`/`frictionloss`).
  - `native/src/drivetrain/motor_clutch.cpp:8-16` (`motor_topology`): selects `"drive_shaft_spin"` + `"crank_clutch"` when `motor_clutch == true`, else `"crank_spin"`.
  - `native/src/drivetrain/transmission.hpp:29-289`, `native/src/drivetrain/transmission.cpp:68-147`: `Transmission` owns `state_` (`boundary`, `range`, `coefficients`, `prepared`, shift work fields) and mutates `model_->tendon_range` / `model_->wrap_prm`.
  - `native/src/writers/drivetrain.hpp:442-533`, `native/src/writers/drivetrain.cpp:124-198`: `DrivetrainWriter` owns `policies_`, `ideal_hub_`, and optional `clutch_`.
  - `native/src/drivetrain/freehub.hpp:7-29`, `native/src/drivetrain/freehub.cpp:7-25`: standalone spring-damper pawl `Freehub` (unused in CPU runtime per `native/src/config_types.hpp:105-110`); `native/src/gpu/drivetrain_resistance_kernel.hpp:187-242`: GPU lossless freehub/drive kernel.
- **Advancing Mutators & Non-Advancing Probes:**
  - `Transmission::stage_reset_into` (`native/src/drivetrain/transmission.cpp:303-320`), `stage_prepare_into` (`328-344`), `stage_ratio_into` (`365-402`), `linearize_candidate` (`181-208`), `commit` (`416-456`), `stage_solved_into` (`458-506`).
  - `DrivetrainWriter::reset` (`native/src/writers/drivetrain.cpp:301-340`), `prepare` (`430-452`), `stage_components` (`706-961`; skips policy/ratchet commits when `!tick.advance` at `801-807`), `settle` (`1104-1211`).
  - `PedalingPolicy::update` (`native/src/drivetrain/pedaling.cpp:42-206`), `stall_update` (`native/src/drivetrain/stall_detector.hpp:19-31`), `AssistController::step` (`native/src/drivetrain/motor.cpp:54-112`).
- **Restore / Codec Deserializers:**
  - `decode_transmission` & `decode_drive_snapshot` (`native/src/drivetrain/state_codec.cpp:341-376,554-614`), `drivetrain::validate` (`native/src/writers/drivetrain.cpp:25-85`), `DrivetrainWriter::restore` (`1213-1261`), `Transmission::validate_state` & `restore` (`native/src/drivetrain/transmission.cpp:610-657`).
- **Validators & Consumers:**
  - `bike::configuration::validate` (`native/src/configuration/validate.cpp:693-745,1330-1350`), `runtime::implemented_capabilities` (`native/src/runtime/capabilities.cpp:7-66`), `NativeRideRuntime::request_crank_reposition` (`native/src/runtime/runtime.cpp:848-856`).
  - MuJoCo constraint solver (`model->tendon_range`), `SpindleController::compute` (`native/src/rider/spindle_controller.cpp:1711-1725`), `PhysicalStep` (`native/src/runtime/step.cpp:921-1021,1486-1493,2711-2787`), `accounting::raw_work` (`native/src/runtime/accounting.cpp:83-138`).
- **Concrete Breakers:**
  1. `Transmission::restore` (`native/src/drivetrain/transmission.cpp:642-657`) + `drivetrain::validate` (`native/src/writers/drivetrain.cpp:45-85`) write unvalidated `s.range` verbatim to `model_->tendon_range` without checking `s.range[1]` against `s.boundary` / `s.prepared` (exercised by `native/tests/reference/test_native_drivetrain.cpp:335-341`).
  2. `validate.cpp:1330-1331` fails to require `config.drive.motor_clutch == true` when `config.drive.pedaling.reposition_on_stall == true`.
  3. `Freehub::Freehub` / `Freehub::update` (`native/src/drivetrain/freehub.cpp:10,22`) permits `damping_nms_rad > 0` and computes viscous dissipation `c_ * (wc - ww)`.

## 2. Sub-Clause Evidence Analysis (Supporting & Opposing)
- **Sub-clause (a) [Axis 1: Partially Violated (holds in live CPU/GPU runtime; violated in standalone `drivetrain::Freehub`) | Axis 2: Confirmed Requirement]:**
  - *Compliance:* Live CPU drivetrains use `Transmission` (`ideal_hub_`, `clutch_`) over frictionless/undamped MJCF hinges (`native/src/model/drivetrain.cpp:16,28,55`, `native/src/model/physical_topology.cpp:49`), emit no bearing drag (`native/src/writers/drivetrain.cpp:898-950`), and add zero drivetrain loss in `PhysicalStep::loss_increment` (`native/src/runtime/step.cpp:2743-2745`), while routing `mjCNSTR_LIMIT_TENDON` power to `closure` (`native/src/runtime/accounting.cpp:87-95`). GPU `gpu_compute_drivetrain_and_resistance` (`native/src/gpu/drivetrain_resistance_kernel.hpp:187-242`) is likewise lossless (`c = 0`, exact `1/ratio` transfer). **Violation:** standalone `drivetrain::Freehub` (`native/src/drivetrain/freehub.cpp:10,22`) accepts `damping_nms_rad > 0` (`nonnegative`) and adds viscous damper torque `c_ * (wc - ww)` during engagement (dead in CPU runtime, `native/src/config_types.hpp:105-110`).
  - *Justification:* Directly mandated by `AGENTS.md:206,210-218` and `docs/agents/simulation-decisions.md:210-219,246-253,283-284,489` (#20, #26, #32, #43); separate closure tracking is documented at `simulation-decisions.md:524-530`.
- **Sub-clause (b) [Axis 1: Holds (subject to coherent snapshot & post-unloading of prior soft-limit compression) | Axis 2: Confirmed Requirement]:**
  - *Compliance:* With `motor_clutch == true`, `crank_clutch` (`native/src/model/physical_topology.cpp:119-125`) constrains `L = q_crank - q_shaft <= tendon_range[1]` (`J_efc = [-1_crank, +1_shaft]`, `efc_force >= 0`), so `f[drive_shaft_spin] = +efc_force >= 0` can never back-drive `drive_shaft_spin` or retard `rear_wheel_spin`. When cranks stand (`omega_crank == 0 < omega_shaft`) or back-pedal (`crank_reposition`: `PedalMode::coasting`, `effort_nm = 0`, `target_rate < 0` at `native/src/drivetrain/pedaling.cpp:116-122`), `phi` decreases so `stage_prepare_into` (`native/src/drivetrain/transmission.cpp:337-341`) ratchets `boundary = min(boundary, phi) = phi` and `clutch_` stays open (`torque == 0`, `engaged == false` at `native/src/writers/drivetrain.cpp:1123-1126`). Meanwhile `AssistController::step` (`native/src/drivetrain/motor.cpp:84-103`, called with `assist_sensor = 0` and `shaft_rpm` from `drive_shaft_spin` at `writers/drivetrain.cpp:749-751,844-865`) sustains `release_hold_s` power on `mid_drive` -> `drive_shaft_spin` -> `ideal_hub_` -> `rear_wheel_spin`.
  - *Justification:* Directly mandated by `AGENTS.md:204`, `docs/agents/simulation-decisions.md:179-190,471-520` (#16, #43, #44), and `docs/RIDE.md:178-186`.
- **Sub-clause (c) [Axis 1: Violated on `restore()` (and unengaged before first `reset`/`prepare` or when `geometric` `prepared == nullopt`) | Axis 2: Inferred Engineering Contract Conflicting with Raw-Mirror Snapshot Tests]:**
  - *Compliance:* Advancing `stage_reset_into` (`native/src/drivetrain/transmission.cpp:303-320`), `stage_prepare_into` (`328-344`), and ratio-changing `stage_ratio_into` (`365-402`) maintain `tendon_range[1] == *state_.boundary` (ideal) or `*state_.boundary - phi + dot(jacobian, q)` (geometric, `195-200`). **Violation:** `Transmission::validate_state` (`610-640`), `Transmission::restore` (`642-657`), and `drivetrain::validate` (`native/src/writers/drivetrain.cpp:45-85`) check only finiteness of `s.range` and `s.boundary`, never relational equality; `restore()` writes `s.range[1]` directly to `model_->tendon_range[1]` (`transmission.cpp:423,651`). Also, `state_.boundary` is `nullopt` after construction before `reset`/`prepare` (`68-147`; preserved if `set_ratio` is called with unchanged ratio at `368`), and `geometric` snapshots allow `prepared == nullopt` with `boundary.has_value()` (`626`).
  - *Justification:* Inferred cross-layer coherence invariant on normal commits, but `docs/TESTING.md:425-427,497-505` and Python-oracle golden test `test_injected_valid_state_and_replay` (`native/tests/reference/test_native_drivetrain.cpp:335-341`) explicitly treat `boundary` and `range` as independently restorable raw fields.
- **Sub-clause (d) [Axis 1: Violated | Axis 2: Relevant Engineering Contract (Confirmed for Manual Reposition Only)]:**
  - *Compliance:* **Violated.** `bike::configuration::validate` (`native/src/configuration/validate.cpp:1330-1331`) only checks `reposition_on_stall => pedal_attachment == "spindle"`. Neither `validate.cpp`, `schema.cpp`, `writer_config.hpp`, `capabilities.cpp:7-75`, nor `DrivetrainWriter` requires `drive.motor_clutch == true` when `drive.pedaling.reposition_on_stall == true`. Only manual `NativeRideRuntime::request_crank_reposition()` checks `motor_clutch` (`native/src/runtime/runtime.cpp:853-855`).
  - *Justification:* `docs/agents/simulation-decisions.md:498-500` (#44) and `docs/RIDE.md:188` confirm the `motor_clutch == true` requirement for *manual* reposition (`request_crank_reposition`), while autonomous stall handling remains open under «Решено — не сделано» #3 (`simulation-decisions.md:546-553`; shipped profiles set `reposition_on_stall = false`, `examples/research/viewer_physics_welded.toml:92`). Extending the guard to `reposition_on_stall` is a sound engineering symmetry contract, not an existing specification rule.

## 3. Exceptions, Cross-Module Dependencies & Failure Consequences
- **Unilateral solver closure damping vs. modeled dissipation in (a):** `ideal_mid_drive_freehub`, `geometric_mid_drive_freehub`, and `crank_clutch` use soft MuJoCo tendon limits with `solreflimit="<closure_time_constant_s> 1"` (`native/src/model/physical_topology.cpp:12-14,117-124,134`). During pawl/clutch take-up (`L > tendon_range[1]`), MuJoCo's critical impedance damper absorbs impact kinetic energy in constraint space; `PhysicalStep` (`native/src/runtime/step.cpp:950-951,1003-1021`) and `accounting::raw_work` (`native/src/runtime/accounting.cpp:87-95`) route this power to `closure_signed_j` / `closure_absolute_j` and elastic penetration to `stored_energy["closures"]`, keeping `loss_j` strictly zero for drivetrain elements (`step.cpp:2743-2745`).
- **Soft-limit elastic unloading transient in (b):** Because `solreflimit` permits small elastic penetration `phi > boundary` under load, if the crank halts or reverses immediately after heavy forward pedaling while `drive_shaft_spin` has not yet rotated forward past `q_crank - boundary`, `L > boundary` persists for the brief (~`closure_time_constant_s`) unloading of `phi - boundary`. Throughout that transient, the upper-limit Jacobian `J_efc = [-1_crank, +1_shaft]` and `efc_force >= 0` guarantee `f[drive_shaft_spin] >= 0` (forward-only, never back-driving or retarding); once `phi <= boundary`, `stage_prepare_into` (`native/src/drivetrain/transmission.cpp:337-341`) ratchets `boundary = phi` (`L == tendon_range[1]`, `margin == 0`), so no `mjCNSTR_LIMIT_TENDON` row is instantiated (`torque == 0`).
- **Cross-clause dependency (c) -> (b):** `NativeRideRuntime::apply_bootstrap` (`native/src/runtime/runtime.cpp:527,551`) calls `stepper_->drive().restore(bootstrap_.drive)` followed by `engine::forward` before any advancing `prepare`. If a restored snapshot carries `clutch.range[1] < clutch.boundary` via (c)'s unvalidated `restore`, `mj_forward` and non-advancing `probe_step_inputs` (`runtime.cpp:693-741`) evaluate spurious `crank_clutch` limit forces until the next advancing `stage_components` overwrites `tendon_range[1]`.
- **Exact consequence of violating (d) (`reposition_on_stall == true && motor_clutch == false`):** Rear freehub `ideal_hub_` (`native/src/writers/drivetrain.cpp:150-156`) still ratchets open, so the rear wheel is not dragged backward; however, `crank_spin` rigidly carries `motor_rotor` (`rotor_inertia_kgm2`, `0.2 kg*m^2` in shipped profiles, `native/src/model/physical_topology.cpp:79-91`) and `mid_drive` (`52,139`). On stall, `PedalingPolicy` triggers `CoastReason::reposition_stall` (`native/src/drivetrain/pedaling.cpp:95-122`) and `SpindleController` (`native/src/rider/spindle_controller.cpp:1711-1725`) commands active back-pedal leg torque that must accelerate the motor rotor backward and directly fight any active `release_hold_s` motor run-on torque on `crank_spin`.

## 4. Located Test Coverage vs. Unexercised Gaps
- **Sub-clause (a) Exercised:**
  - `native/tests/reference/test_native_drivetrain.cpp:517-534` (`test_ideal_drive_applies_no_bearing_drag`): asserts no `"drive_bearings"` component and zero drive force at nonzero spin with zero effort.
  - `native/tests/gpu/test_gpu_drivetrain_resistance.cpp:764-818` (`test_gpu_drive_is_lossless`): asserts zero coasting drag and full `nominal_crank_torque_nm / ratio` transfer on GPU.
  - `native/tests/reference/test_welded_profile_config.cpp:294-300` & `native/tests/config/test_schema.cpp:805-816,903-921`: assert removal of `elastic_chain`, `bearing_c_nms_rad`, `freehub_c_nms_rad`, and `joint_passive_damping_nms_rad`.
  - *Gap:* `native/tests/gpu/test_gpu_drivetrain_resistance.cpp:255-320` only instantiates `drivetrain::Freehub` with `damping_nms_rad = 0.0`; no test rejects `damping_nms_rad > 0` on `Freehub`.
- **Sub-clause (b) Exercised & Gaps:**
  - `native/tests/reference/test_crank_reposition.cpp:290-466` (`test_reposition_backpedal_respects_the_clutch_topology`): asserts `crank_clutch_engaged == false`, `crank_clutch_torque_nm >= -1e-6`, and rear wheel backward drift `<= 0.02 rad` during manual `request_crank_reposition()` back-pedaling (`qvel[crank] < -0.5`) with `motor_torque_nm = 0`.
  - `native/tests/reference/test_assist_controller.cpp:56-98`: unit-tests `AssistController` `release_hold_s` run-on with stopped pedals (`cadence = 0`, `shaft_rpm = 60`).
  - `native/tests/reference/test_native_drivetrain.cpp:496-513` (`test_genuine_wheel_and_rotor_overrun`): asserts `freehub_torque_nm == 0` during wheel overrun on `clutch` topology.
  - *Gap:* No full-plant integration test exercises simultaneous `AssistController` `release_hold_s` motor run-on driving `drive_shaft_spin` + `rear_wheel_spin` *while* `crank_reposition` actively back-pedals `crank_spin` (`test_crank_reposition.cpp:292` sets `motor_torque_nm = 0.` and starts from standstill).
- **Sub-clause (c) Exercised & Counter-Example in Suite:**
  - `native/tests/reference/test_native_drivetrain.cpp:89-113` (`test_transmission_core`) & `native/tests/drivetrain/test_transmission.cpp:32-102`: exercise `reset`, `prepare`, `set_ratio`, non-advancing probes, and valid round-trip `restore`.
  - `native/tests/reference/test_native_state_restore.cpp:612-842` & `native/tests/reference/test_native_state_fuzz.cpp:270-299`: assert atomic rejection of `ratio`, `rear_teeth`, `coefficients`, `prepared.jacobian`, and `clutch` coefficient drift on `restore`, **without** checking `range[1]` vs. `boundary`.
  - *Active counter-example:* `native/tests/reference/test_native_drivetrain.cpp:334-341` (`test_injected_valid_state_and_replay`) mutates `injected->ideal_hub->boundary.value() += .02` without updating `range[1]` and asserts `restore(*injected)` succeeds; `583-586` (`test_geometric_prepared_sentinel_owns_reused_storage`) restores `geometric` `ideal_hub` with `prepared.reset()` while `boundary` remains engaged.
- **Sub-clause (d) Exercised & Gap:**
  - `native/tests/reference/test_crank_reposition.cpp:540-581`: asserts *manual* `request_crank_reposition()` rejects `motor_clutch = false` (incidentally proving `reposition_env` builds a valid runtime with `motor_clutch = false` and `pedal_attachment = "spindle"`).
  - `native/tests/config/test_schema.cpp:1013-1026`: tests `reposition_on_stall = true` rejection ONLY for `pedal_attachment = "flat"`, never for `motor_clutch = false`.
  - *Gap:* Missing config/capability rejection test (and implementation guard) for `reposition_on_stall == true && motor_clutch == false`.

## 5. Recommended Contract / FSM Refinement
Split `P01` into two confirmed physical safety invariants and two explicit contract-alignment items:
1. **P01-A (Confirmed Lossless Drivetrain & Unilateral Clutch Invariant — keep & enforce):**
   - Scope to live CPU (`Transmission`, `crank_clutch`, `DrivetrainWriter`, MJCF joints) and GPU (`drivetrain_resistance_kernel.hpp`) drivetrains: zero modeled viscous/frictional drivetrain dissipation in `loss_j` (with soft tendon-limit `solreflimit` work isolated in `closure_*_j`), and for `motor_clutch == true` under coherent state, `crank_clutch` never applies negative/retarding torque (`f[drive_shaft_spin] >= 0`), remains open (`torque == 0`) once unloaded during stationary/backward crank motion, and permits `AssistController` `release_hold_s` run-on to drive `drive_shaft_spin -> ideal_hub_ -> rear_wheel_spin`.
   - **Code hygiene for standalone `Freehub`:** Either pin `damping_nms_rad == 0` in `Freehub::Freehub` (`native/src/drivetrain/freehub.cpp:10`, matching Ban #39 in `validate.cpp:297-299`) or remove the `c_` parameter from `Freehub`.
2. **P01-B (Autonomous Stall Reposition Clutch Guard — missing config check):**
   - In `bike::configuration::validate` (`native/src/configuration/validate.cpp:1330-1331`) and wire config validation, require `config.drive.motor_clutch == true` whenever `config.drive.pedaling.reposition_on_stall == true`, matching `NativeRideRuntime::request_crank_reposition()` (`native/src/runtime/runtime.cpp:853-855`) and `simulation-decisions.md:498-500` (#44).
3. **P01-C (`Transmission` Tendon-Range Coherence — scope to advancing commits OR tighten `restore`):**
   - **As-implemented invariant:** State that every advancing `reset()`, `prepare()`, and ratio-changing `set_ratio()` establishes `model_->tendon_range[1] == *state_.boundary` (ideal) or `*state_.boundary - prepared->phi + dot(prepared->jacobian, prepared->qpos)` (geometric).
   - **If tightening `restore()`:** Validate `s.range[0] <= s.range[1]` and (whenever `s.boundary` and, for geometric, `s.prepared` are present) enforce exact `s.range[1]` equality in `Transmission::validate_state` (`native/src/drivetrain/transmission.cpp:610-640`), updating `test_injected_valid_state_and_replay` (`native/tests/reference/test_native_drivetrain.cpp:335-341`) and its golden fixture accordingly.

# Stage 3 (`R1`) Independent Research Report — `P02`: Crank-Reposition & Stall-Detector State-Space Validity and Bounded Manoeuvre Termination

- **Inspected Commit:** `0502660dd14bd1021b3da90a61e20dcb1d5805a1`
- **Compliance Verdict (`R1` Agent):** `partially_violated`
- **Product Justification Verdict (`R1` Agent):** `mixed_subclauses`

## 1. Producers, Mutators, Consumers & Potential Breakers
- **State Owners & Value Records:**
  - `drivetrain::StallDetectorState` (`native/src/drivetrain/stall_detector.hpp:13-17`): ephemeral public aggregate `{crank_rad = 0., wheel_rad = 0., elapsed_s = nullopt}`; persistent fields live in `drivetrain::RepositionSnapshot` (`native/src/config_types.hpp:66-74`: `{crank_rad, wheel_rad, stall_elapsed_s, goal_rad, elapsed_s, cooldown_s, armed, reason}`), hosted optionally in `drivetrain::PedalingSnapshot::reposition` (`native/src/config_types.hpp:76-82`) inside `PedalingPolicy::state_` (`native/src/drivetrain/pedaling.hpp:126`), `PolicySnapshot` (`native/src/drivetrain/policy_set.hpp:14-18`), and `DriveSnapshot` (`native/src/writers/drivetrain.hpp:62-78`).
- **Config Validators (Environmental Bounds):**
  - `drivetrain::validate(const PedalingConfig&)` (`native/src/config_validation.hpp:117-142`) & `post_init` (`native/src/configuration/validate.cpp:577-611`): enforce `reposition_stall_window_s > 0`, `reposition_stall_progress_rad > 0`, `reposition_back_rate_rad_s > 0`, `reposition_timeout_s > 0`, `reposition_phase_tolerance_rad > 0`, `reposition_noop_rad >= reposition_phase_tolerance_rad`, and **non-negative** (`>= 0`) `effort_slew_nm_s`, `reposition_min_effort_nm`, `reposition_cooldown_s`, `reposition_damping_nms_rad`.
- **Producers & Runtime Mutators:**
  - `stall_update` (`native/src/drivetrain/stall_detector.hpp:19-31`): disarms `s = {}` on `!loaded` (`:21`) or `*s.elapsed_s >= l.window_s` (`:29`); re-anchors `s = {}; s.elapsed_s = 0.` on progress (`:25-26`); arms `*s.elapsed_s = 0.` on `!s.elapsed_s` **without** clearing `crank_rad`/`wheel_rad` (`:22`).
  - `PedalingPolicy::reset` (`native/src/drivetrain/pedaling.hpp:108`): `state_ = {}`.
  - `PedalingPolicy::update` (`native/src/drivetrain/pedaling.cpp:47-215`): validates `dt > 0` & finite angles (`:53-61`); `!enabled` resets and latches `armed = false` if `reposition_request` (`:64-78`); engages `state_.reposition` (`:82-84`); decays `cooldown_s` (`:87`); aborts on `braking` into `cooldown_s = reposition_cooldown_s` (`:88`); detects rising `request_edge`/`reflex_edge` (`:89-99`); starts manoeuvre (`goal_rad = phase - turn`, `reason = why` in `{1,2}`, `elapsed_s = 0.`, `state_.effort = 0.`, `state_.coasting = false`) unless within `reposition_noop_rad` (`:100-109`); advances `elapsed_s += dt` and terminates on `remaining <= reposition_phase_tolerance_rad || elapsed_s > reposition_timeout_s` (`:110-122`); auto-clears `state_.reposition` when idle & `!reposition_on_stall && cooldown_s <= 0. && armed` (`:124`); runs ordinary pedaling (`:142-197`) or coasting (`:199-214`).
  - Request producers: `ViewerAction::reposition_crank` (`native/src/viewer/input.cpp:26,54-58`, `native/src/viewer/ride.cpp:85,179-185`) -> `NativeRideRuntime::request_crank_reposition` (`native/src/runtime/runtime.cpp:850-858`) -> `PhysicalStep::request_crank_reposition` (`native/src/runtime/step.cpp:1339-1343`), latching `pending_reposition_` consumed only on advancing control boundaries (`step.cpp:1367,1471-1474,1517-1518`) and cleared on `reset`/`restore` (`runtime.cpp:969`, `step.cpp:3010`).
- **Restore, Wire Codec & Non-Advancing Probes:**
  - Wire decode/encode: `decode_policies` (`native/src/drivetrain/state_codec.cpp:268-322`), `reposition_value`/`policy_value` (`:439-496`), `decode_policy_snapshot` (`:531-535`), `decode_drive_snapshot` (`:544-577`).
  - State restore: `PedalingPolicy::set_state` (`pedaling.cpp:20-44`), `PolicySet::restore`/`validate_state` (`native/src/drivetrain/policy_set.hpp:35-57`), `drivetrain::validate(DriveSnapshot)` & `DrivetrainWriter::restore` (`native/src/writers/drivetrain.cpp:25-85,1203-1261`), `PhysicalStep::restore` (`step.cpp:2952-3012`), `apply_bootstrap` (`runtime.cpp:508-531`), `equilibrium_cache.cpp:81-88,158-162`.
  - Non-advancing probes (`advance = false`): `DrivetrainWriter::stage_pedal_advance` copies `PedalingPolicy` and evaluates `copy.update(...)`, leaving `policies_.pedaling` untouched (`writers/drivetrain.cpp:599-620`); `PhysicalStep::probe_step_inputs` passes `.crank_reposition = false` (`step.cpp:3055`).
- **Consumers & Potential Breakers:**
  - `DrivetrainWriter::stage_components` (`writers/drivetrain.cpp:693-759`): zeroes `human_torque`, `sensed_rider`, and `motor_cmd` when `mode != PedalMode::pedaling`, records `rider_mode` and `coasting_reason`.
  - `PhysicalStep::apply_forces_body` (`step.cpp:1499-1516`): checks `is_reposition(pedal.reason)`, passes `reposition_active` and `reposition_rate_rad_s = pedal.target_rate_rad_s` to `SpindleController` (`native/src/rider/spindle_controller.cpp:357-364,1688-1748,1767-1809`), which applies `reposition_damping_nms_rad`, preserves `coast_hold_nm_`, and zeros pedal `f_rest_n` on upward motion (preventing platform-pedal pull while `crank_clutch` decouples the motor).
  - **Breakers:** `state_codec.cpp:287-322` and `PedalingPolicy::set_state` (`pedaling.cpp:20-44`) omit cross-field invariants on `RepositionSnapshot` and `PedalingSnapshot::effort`, while `state_codec.cpp:315-322` also omits `coasting => target_phase_rad`.

## 2. Sub-Clause Evidence Analysis (Supporting & Opposing)
### Sub-clause (a): `!elapsed_s.has_value() => (crank_rad == 0.0 && wheel_rad == 0.0)`
- **Axis 1 (Compliance): VIOLATED on `set_state` and wire deserialization; HOLDS on runtime-only trajectories from `reset()`.**
  - *Runtime:* `StallDetectorState{}` (`native/src/drivetrain/stall_detector.hpp:13-16`) and `RepositionSnapshot{}` (`native/src/config_types.hpp:66-73`) default-zero `crank_rad`/`wheel_rad` with `nullopt` elapsed time. In `stall_update` (`stall_detector.hpp:21,26,29`), every disarm (`!loaded`, `*s.elapsed_s >= l.window_s`) assigns `s = {}`, preserving `(a)`.
  - *Deserialization / `set_state` counterexample:* `decode_policies` (`native/src/drivetrain/state_codec.cpp:294-296,306-308`) and `PedalingPolicy::set_state` (`native/src/drivetrain/pedaling.cpp:30-42`) check only finiteness of `crank_rad`/`wheel_rad` and `stall_elapsed_s >= 0`, accepting `{crank_rad = 5.0, wheel_rad = -3.0, stall_elapsed_s = nullopt}`. Moreover, `StallDetectorState` is an unguarded aggregate (`stall_detector.hpp:13-17`).
- **Axis 2 (Justification): Inferred engineering contract.** Supports state-domain hygiene (`docs/agents/native-engineering.md:38`) and unbiased stall-window arming (`stall_detector.hpp:22`); the stall reflex itself is an algorithmic helper (`docs/agents/simulation-decisions.md:513-519`, off by default in research profiles per `docs/RIDE.md:196`).

### Sub-clause (b): `goal_rad.has_value() <=> reason in {1, 2}` and `!(goal_rad.has_value() && cooldown_s > 0.0)`
- **Axis 1 (Compliance): VIOLATED on `set_state` and wire deserialization; HOLDS on runtime-only trajectories from `reset()`.**
  - *Runtime:* Entry (`pedaling.cpp:101-108`) requires `!braking && edge && !r.goal_rad && r.cooldown_s <= 0.` and sets `r.goal_rad = phase - turn; r.reason = why` (`why` in `{1,2}`); exits (`pedaling.cpp:88,114`) execute `r.goal_rad.reset(); r.reason = 0; r.cooldown_s = config_.reposition_cooldown_s`.
  - *Deserialization / `set_state` counterexamples:* Neither `state_codec.cpp:287-314` nor `PedalingPolicy::set_state` (`pedaling.cpp:30-42`) nor `drivetrain::validate(DriveSnapshot)` (`native/src/writers/drivetrain.cpp:25-85`) checks relational invariants among `goal_rad`, `reason`, and `cooldown_s`. All three invalid states — `{goal_rad = 1.0, reason = 0}`, `{goal_rad = nullopt, reason = 1}`, and `{goal_rad = 1.0, reason = 1, cooldown_s = 0.5}` — are accepted by both `decode_*` and `set_state`/`restore`.
- **Axis 2 (Justification): Relevant engineering contract** backing confirmed crank-reposition behavior (`docs/agents/simulation-decisions.md:490-503` «Действует» §44, `docs/RIDE.md:166-204`, `docs/agents/native-engineering.md:38-41`).

### Sub-clause (c): `(coasting && !reposition_active) => target_phase_rad.has_value()` across wire decode and `set_state`
- **Axis 1 (Compliance): VIOLATED at standalone wire decode (`decode_policy_snapshot` / `decode_drive_snapshot`); HOLDS at `set_state` / `restore` and runtime.**
  - *`set_state` & runtime:* `PedalingPolicy::set_state` (`pedaling.cpp:26`) unconditionally throws `std::invalid_argument("coasting target_phase_rad")` if `s.coasting && !s.target_phase_rad` (and runtime reposition sets `state_.coasting = false` at `pedaling.cpp:107`; ordinary coasting sets `state_.target_phase_rad = phase` at `pedaling.cpp:201`).
  - *Wire decode counterexample:* `decode_policies` (`state_codec.cpp:315-322`) omits the `coasting && !target_phase_rad` gate, so `decode_policy_snapshot` (`state_codec.cpp:531-535`) and `decode_drive_snapshot` (`state_codec.cpp:544-577`) succeed on `{"coasting": true, "target_phase_rad": null, ...}` with `_reposition` absent.
- **Axis 2 (Justification): Relevant engineering contract.** Prevents `engaged(state_.target_phase_rad)` termination (`pedaling.cpp:209`) and enforces wire-boundary validation (`docs/agents/native-engineering.md:35-41`) for passive-leg coasting (`docs/agents/simulation-decisions.md:91-112` «Действует» §9).

### Sub-clause (d): Active manoeuvre terminates in `<= reposition_timeout_s` (or earlier on arrival / `braking`), enters `cooldown_s = reposition_cooldown_s`, and resumes pedaling effort from `0 N*m`
- **Axis 1 (Compliance): PARTIALLY VIOLATED across edge inputs and restored states.**
  - *Nominal path holds:* On trigger (`pedaling.cpp:106-107`), `r.elapsed_s = 0.; state_.effort = 0.`. Each enabled non-braking step increments `r.elapsed_s += dt` (`:112`) before checking `remaining <= reposition_phase_tolerance_rad || r.elapsed_s > reposition_timeout_s` (`:113`), clearing `goal_rad`/`reason` and setting `r.cooldown_s = config_.reposition_cooldown_s` (`:88,114`).
  - *Violating edge cases:* (1) `enabled == false` (`pedaling.cpp:64-72`) resets `state_`, terminating the manoeuvre with `cooldown_s == 0.` instead of `reposition_cooldown_s`; (2) tiny positive `dt <= 0.5 * ulp(r.elapsed_s)` (permitted by `dt > 0`) stagnates `r.elapsed_s += dt` in binary64 forever, and downward FP rounding at normal `dt` can admit one step whose real time sum exceeds `reposition_timeout_s` by 0.5 ULP; (3) post-termination enters `PedalMode::coasting` (`effort_nm = 0.`) rather than `pedaling` when `braking == true`, `effort == 0.`, or `cadence_ema >= coast_above_rpm` (`:137-141,199-214`); (4) valid `effort_slew_nm_s == 0.` (`:165`) jumps immediately to `target` effort; (5) restoring `s.effort > 0.` with active `goal_rad` (`:25-43`) resumes pedaling from `s.effort` instead of `0 N*m`; (6) `reposition_cooldown_s == 0.` with `!reposition_on_stall && armed` (`:124`) immediately resets `state_.reposition` to `nullopt`.
- **Axis 2 (Justification): Mixed confirmed requirement + engineering parameters + over-strong postcondition.** Back-pedaling to power phase without platform-pedal pull and motor decoupling via `crank_clutch` are confirmed in `AGENTS.md:175-186` and `docs/agents/simulation-decisions.md:490-520` («Действует» §44); timeout/cooldown/tolerance/slew are explicitly noted as algorithmic engineering knobs (`simulation-decisions.md:513-517`, `docs/RIDE.md:178-204`); requiring `cooldown_s = reposition_cooldown_s` on `enabled == false` or `0 N*m` ramp when `effort_slew_nm_s == 0` overstates intended behavior.

## 3. Exceptions, Cross-Module Dependencies & Failure Consequences
- **Stale progress anchor upon arming `stall_update` (from (a) gap):** Because `stall_update` arms via `if (!s.elapsed_s) { s.elapsed_s = 0.; return false; }` (`native/src/drivetrain/stall_detector.hpp:22`) without zeroing `s.crank_rad` or `s.wheel_rad`, restoring `stall_elapsed_s = nullopt` with nonzero `crank_rad`/`wheel_rad` corrupts the subsequent loaded tick (`stall_detector.hpp:23-26`): positive residue (`> crank_progress_rad`) prematurely resets the stall window, whereas large negative residue masks real forward progress and causes false-positive stall triggers at `window_s`.
- **Silent telemetry/control misclassification (from (b) gap):** If `goal_rad.has_value() && reason == 0` is injected via `decode_*` or `set_state`, `PedalingPolicy::update` (`native/src/drivetrain/pedaling.cpp:118-119`) evaluates `r.reason == 1 ? CoastReason::reposition_requested : CoastReason::reposition_stall`, silently reporting idle `0` as a stall-reflex manoeuvre (`reposition_stall`) to telemetry (`native/src/writers/drivetrain.cpp:756`) and `is_reposition` consumers. If `goal_rad.has_value() && cooldown_s > 0.`, cooldown decays concurrently with the active manoeuvre (`pedaling.cpp:87`).
- **Deferred validation & invariant-abort hazard (from (c) gap):** `decode_policy_snapshot` (`native/src/drivetrain/state_codec.cpp:531-535`) and `decode_drive_snapshot` (`state_codec.cpp:544-577`, called in `native/src/runtime/bootstrap.cpp:376`) return snapshots with `coasting == true && !target_phase_rad` (as well as negative `deceleration_rad_s2` or `_effort`); safety relies solely on callers subsequently invoking `set_state`/`restore` (`pedaling.cpp:24-26`) before `engaged(state_.target_phase_rad)` (`pedaling.cpp:209`).
- **Effort-jump, disable-reset, FP-absorption, and multi-step hold nuances (in (d)):**
  1. Restoring `s.effort > 0.` while `s.reposition->goal_rad.has_value()` passes `set_state` (`pedaling.cpp:25-43`), stays untouched while active (`pedaling.cpp:118`), and resumes pedaling from `previous = s.effort > 0.` (`pedaling.cpp:143`) rather than `0 N*m`.
  2. `enabled == false` (`pedaling.cpp:64-72`, e.g., `RideControl::rider_enabled == false` at `writers/drivetrain.cpp:679`) calls `reset()`, terminating an active manoeuvre with `cooldown_s == 0.` (or `reposition == nullopt`) instead of `reposition_cooldown_s`.
  3. Under `effort_slew_nm_s == 0.` (`native/src/config_validation.hpp:124`), `pedaling.cpp:165` jumps directly to `target` effort; under `reposition_cooldown_s == 0.` (`config_validation.hpp:133`) with `!reposition_on_stall && armed`, `pedaling.cpp:124` immediately resets `state_.reposition` to `nullopt`.
  4. In IEEE-754 binary64, arbitrary `dt > 0` with `dt <= 0.5 * ulp(r.elapsed_s)` causes `r.elapsed_s += dt` (`pedaling.cpp:112`) to stagnate below `reposition_timeout_s` indefinitely; at normal `dt`, downward rounding plus strict `>` (`pedaling.cpp:113`) can admit 1 extra tick (~0.5 ULP real-time overshoot), and `goal_rad` clears in `state()` only on the tick where `r.elapsed_s > timeout_s`. In `PhysicalStep` (`native/src/runtime/step.cpp:1471-1495`), `PedalingPolicy::update` runs on `ControlClock` boundaries (`control_dt`, e.g. `0.005 s`) while held across physics substeps (`0.0005 s`).

## 4. Located Test Coverage vs. Unexercised Gaps
- **Actively Asserted Paths:**
  - *Stall detector & policy unit tests (`native/tests/reference/test_pedaling_policy.cpp`):* `test_stall_detector` (`:78-115`) asserts arming, rocking/rollback non-progress, progress reset, `!loaded` reset (`StallDetectorState{}`), and post-fire re-arming; `test_reposition_stall_reflex` (`:117-159`) asserts stall trigger (`reason == "reposition_stall"`), arrival within `reposition_phase_tolerance_rad`, `cooldown_s == reposition_cooldown_s`, slew-limited effort recovery (`effort_nm <= effort_slew_nm_s * dt`), and `reposition_noop_rad` suppression; `test_reposition_lifecycle` (`:161-235`) asserts rising-edge request, `braking` abort into cooldown, timeout termination (`301` steps of `0.01 s` at `3.0 s` timeout), `enabled = false` held-request suppression, and engaged/disengaged `PedalingSnapshot::reposition` round-trip through `set_state`.
  - *Integrated physics & runtime tests (`native/tests/reference/test_crank_reposition.cpp`):* R3 (`:263-312`) asserts `SpindleController` zero upward pull (`f_rest_n == 0`), `coast_hold_nm_` retention, and `crank_clutch` zero slack/motor decoupling; R5 (`:318-357`) asserts full `PhysicalStep` hill-launch recovery via `request_crank_reposition()`; S3 (`:393-475`) asserts non-advancing `probe_step_inputs` preserves `pending_reposition_`, one-shot consumption on the advancing control boundary, and `reset()` clearing `pending_reposition_`.
  - *Codec & validation tests:* `native/tests/reference/test_native_drive_policies.cpp:178,430-467` asserts `PedalingPolicy::set_state` rejects `coasting && !target_phase_rad` and negative/out-of-range `_reposition` scalars (`reason == 3`, `reason == -1`, negative `elapsed_s`/`cooldown_s`/`stall_elapsed_s`) and round-trips engaged `_reposition` through `encode_policy_snapshot`/`decode_policy_snapshot`; `native/tests/reference/test_native_state_restore.cpp:367-370` asserts `DrivetrainWriter::restore` rejects `coasting && !target_phase_rad`.
- **Located Coverage Gaps (Unexercised / Unchecked):**
  1. **Cross-field `_reposition` & `_effort` invariants in `state_codec` and `set_state`:** No test exercises `{stall_elapsed_s: null, crank_rad != 0}`, `{goal_rad != null, reason: 0}`, `{goal_rad: null, reason: 1}`, `{goal_rad != null, cooldown_s > 0}`, or `{_effort > 0, _reposition.goal_rad != null}`.
  2. **Standalone `decode_policy_snapshot` / `decode_drive_snapshot` on `coasting && !target_phase_rad`:** Tests always follow decode with `restore`/`set_state` (`test_native_state_restore.cpp:367-370`), leaving standalone decode acceptance untested.
  3. **State-fuzz & contract blind spots on `_reposition`:** In `native/tests/reference/test_native_state_fuzz.cpp:330-355`, `drivetrain_reference::pair()` starts from `writer.reset()` where `pedaling.reposition == nullopt`, so `encode_drive_snapshot` omits `"_reposition"` (`state_codec.cpp:466-468`) and leaf-mutation fuzzing **never fuzzes `_reposition`**. Furthermore, `same(const PedalingSnapshot&, const PedalingSnapshot&)` in `native/tests/test_contracts.cpp:1689-1696` omits `a.reposition` / `b.reposition` from bitwise snapshot equality!
  4. **Manoeuvre termination edge cases:** No test exercises `effort_slew_nm_s == 0.` post-reposition, `reposition_cooldown_s == 0.` post-reposition, or tiny-`dt` FP absorption in `r.elapsed_s += dt`.

## 5. Recommended Contract / FSM Refinement
Split `P02` into a **Cross-Boundary State-Validity Invariant (`P02a`)** and a **Guarded Bounded-Termination Contract (`P02b`)**, and tighten `state_codec` / `set_state` / `stall_update` to match:

1. **`P02a` (State-Space Coherence across Runtime, `set_state`, and Wire Codec):**
   - Enforce in both `decode_policies` (`native/src/drivetrain/state_codec.cpp:287-322`) and `PedalingPolicy::set_state` (`native/src/drivetrain/pedaling.cpp:20-44`):
     - `(a')` `!r.stall_elapsed_s.has_value() => (r.crank_rad == 0.0 && r.wheel_rad == 0.0)` (and defensively zero `s.crank_rad = s.wheel_rad = 0.` when arming `stall_update` at `stall_detector.hpp:22`);
     - `(b')` `r.goal_rad.has_value() <=> (r.reason == 1 || r.reason == 2)` (equivalently `!r.goal_rad.has_value() <=> r.reason == 0`), `!(r.goal_rad.has_value() && r.cooldown_s > 0.0)`, and `r.goal_rad.has_value() => (!s.coasting && s.effort == 0.0)`;
     - `(c')` `s.coasting => s.target_phase_rad.has_value()` (plus `deceleration_rad_s2 >= 0` and `_effort >= 0`) at `state_codec.cpp:315-322`, not only in `set_state`.
2. **`P02b` (Bounded Crank-Reposition Termination & Post-State):**
   - Explicitly scope environmental preconditions to `enabled == true` throughout, `dt >= dt_min > 0` (e.g., `dt > 0.5 * ulp(reposition_timeout_s)`, preventing binary64 absorption), `reposition_timeout_s > 0`, `reposition_phase_tolerance_rad > 0`, and valid `P02a` initial state:
   - Within cumulative active-manoeuvre steps of duration `<= reposition_timeout_s + 0.5 * ulp(reposition_timeout_s)` (i.e., `<= floor(reposition_timeout_s / dt) + 1` policy evaluations, or earlier on `braking == true` or `phase - *goal_rad <= reposition_phase_tolerance_rad`), `goal_rad` disengages (`reason == 0`), `cooldown_s` is set to `reposition_cooldown_s` (disengaging `state_.reposition` iff `reposition_cooldown_s == 0 && !reposition_on_stall && armed`), and on transition to `PedalMode::pedaling` (when `!braking && effort > 0 && !excessive_cadence`) effort ramps from `previous == 0 N*m` by at most `effort_slew_nm_s * dt` whenever `effort_slew_nm_s > 0` (or equals `target` when `effort_slew_nm_s == 0`).
3. **Test Hardening:** Add `PedalingSnapshot::reposition` to `same()` in `native/tests/test_contracts.cpp:1689-1696`, seed an engaged `_reposition` case in `native/tests/reference/test_native_state_fuzz.cpp`, and add negative wire/`set_state` tests for `(a')`, `(b')`, and `(c')`.
