# Stage 3 (`R1`) Independent Research Report — `P03`: Pedaling/Coasting Hysteresis, Effort Slew Bound & Motor Assist Run-On Hold Coherence

- **Inspected Commit:** `0502660dd14bd1021b3da90a61e20dcb1d5805a1`
- **Compliance Verdict (`R1` Agent):** `partially_violated`
- **Product Justification Verdict (`R1` Agent):** `mixed_subclauses`

## 1. Producers, Mutators, Consumers & Potential Breakers
- **State Owners & Fields:**
  - `drivetrain::PedalingSnapshot` (`native/src/config_types.hpp:51-59`), owned by `PedalingPolicy::state_` (`native/src/drivetrain/pedaling.hpp:126`), `PolicySet::pedaling_` (`native/src/drivetrain/policy_set.hpp:61`), and `DrivetrainWriter::{pedaling_, pedaling_scratch_, live_.pedaling, banks_[i].pedaling}` (`native/src/writers/drivetrain.hpp:442, 468, 491, 495`): `coasting`, `target_phase_rad`, `target_rate_rad_s`, `deceleration_rad_s2`, `effort`, `cadence_ema`, `reposition` (`RepositionSnapshot`, `config_types.hpp:39-48`). Per-tick output `PedalingState` (`pedaling.hpp:94-100`) carries `mode` (`PedalMode`), `reason` (`CoastReason`), `effort_nm`, `required_cadence_rpm`, `target_phase_rad`, `target_rate_rad_s`.
  - `drivetrain::AssistSnapshot` (`native/src/config_types.hpp:97-105`), owned by `AssistController::state_` (`native/src/drivetrain/motor.hpp:99`), `PolicySet::assist_` (`policy_set.hpp:63`), and `DrivetrainWriter::{assist_, assist_scratch_, live_.assist, banks_[i].assist, settlement_banks_[i].assist}` (`writers/drivetrain.hpp:444, 470, 491, 497, 502`): `torque`, `last_gain`, `hold_power_w`, `hold_torque_nm`, `release_hold_left_s`, `pedaling`.
- **Producers & Mutators:**
  - `PedalingPolicy`: ctor `validate(config_)` (`pedaling.hpp:104-106`, `native/src/config_validation.hpp:118-137`); `reset()` (`pedaling.hpp:108`); `update(...)` (`native/src/drivetrain/pedaling.cpp:47-215`); `set_state(...)` (`pedaling.cpp:20-44`).
  - `AssistController`: ctor `validate(config_)` (`motor.hpp:64-75`, `config_validation.hpp:168-193`); `reset()` (`motor.hpp:77`); `step(...)` (`native/src/drivetrain/motor.cpp:50-113`); `set_state(...)` (`motor.cpp:28-35`); `publish(...)` (`motor.hpp:95`, noexcept commit path).
  - `DrivetrainWriter` (`native/src/writers/drivetrain.cpp`): `reset()` (`448-461`), `restart_clock()` (`464-471`), `stage_prepare()`/`stage_pedal_advance()` (`599-620, 661-760`), `stage_components()`/`stage_components_metrics()`/`stage_components_telemetry()` (`762-965`, notably `bank.assist.torque = m.delivered` at `894-895`), `commit(PreparedTick)` (`967-1022`), `stage_settle()`/`commit(PreparedSettlement)` (`1032-1186`, overwriting `assist.torque = pending_torque` at `1064-1067` and publishing via `assist_.publish` at `1174-1175`), `restore(const DriveSnapshot&)` (`1213-1261`).
  - Deserializers & Runtime Restorers: `decode_policy_snapshot` / `decode_drive_snapshot` / `decode_policies` (`native/src/drivetrain/state_codec.cpp:268-339, 583-613`); `PolicySet::set_state` (`policy_set.hpp:39-44`); `PhysicalStep::initialize_ride` (`native/src/runtime/step.cpp:960-995`, calls `restart_clock()`).
- **Callers & Consumers:**
  - `PhysicalStep::apply_forces_body` (`step.cpp:1363-1371` `drive().prepare(...)`; `1448-1470` `drive().stage_components(...)` + `commit(...)`; `1472-1528` packs `pedaling.effort_nm`, `target_phase_rad`, `target_rate_rad_s`, `is_reposition(pedaling.reason)` into `rider::RiderCommand`); `PhysicalStep::solve_tail` (`step.cpp:1846` `drive().settle()`).
  - `DrivetrainWriter::stage_components_metrics` (`writers/drivetrain.cpp:848-866`) consumes `ps.effort_nm` via `human_crank_torque` (`crank_effort` mode) and gates `m.assist_sensor = ps.mode == PedalMode::pedaling ? m.sensor : 0.` (`line 854`) before `assist_scratch_.step(...)`, writing `ctrl[human_actuator_]` and `ctrl[motor_actuator_]` (`890-893`) and telemetry (`915-955`).
  - `rider::SpindleController::compute` (`native/src/rider/spindle_controller.cpp:1633-1636, 1726-1735`) consumes `mean_crank_torque_nm` (`pedaling.effort_nm`), `crank_target_rate_rad_s`, and `crank_reposition`, while ignoring `crank_target_phase_rad` during ordinary active coasting (`!steady && !crank_reposition`). `LegacyController::compute` (`native/src/rider/legacy_controller.cpp:322-344`) reads `mean_crank_torque_nm` and `crank_target_phase_rad.has_value()` for velocity scratch prediction.
- **Confirmed Breakers / Boundary Gaps:**
  1. `AssistController::set_state` (`motor.cpp:28-35`), `decode_policies` (`state_codec.cpp:280-282, 333-339`), `PolicySet::validate_state` (`policy_set.hpp:46-57`), and `drivetrain::validate` (`writers/drivetrain.cpp:25-85`) accept `release_hold_left_s > 0 && hold_power_w == 0` without throwing.
  2. `AssistController::step` (`motor.cpp:82-86, 104-106`) can itself produce `release_hold_left_s > 0 && hold_power_w == 0` when positive sub-min inputs (`state_.torque > 0`, `omega > 0`) underflow `state_.torque * omega` to `0.0` in binary64.
  3. `PedalingPolicy::set_state` (`pedaling.cpp:20-44`) accepts `s.coasting == true` (with engaged `target_phase_rad`) or active `s.reposition->goal_rad` alongside `s.effort > 0.`, breaking the zero-effort-on-coasting invariant across `set_state`/`restore` and seeding the next pedaling tick's slew from `s.effort > 0.`.

## 2. Sub-Clause Evidence Analysis (Supporting & Opposing)
- **Sub-clause (a) — Active Pedaling Slew Bound & Immediate Zero Drop on Coasting:**
  - **Axis 1 (Implementation Compliance): Holds on reset-reachable runtime paths; unguarded on `set_state`/`restore` injection.** Under `effort_slew_nm_s > 0` (`300.0 N·m/s` in `examples/research/viewer_physics_welded.toml:80`) and `dt > 0`:
    - When `PedalingPolicy::update` enters `PedalMode::pedaling` (`reason == CoastReason::none`, `native/src/drivetrain/pedaling.cpp:142-170`), `target` incorporates the low-cadence mash ramp (`lines 151-152`), `ceiling_nm`, and shift-cut `torque_factor` (`lines 153-163`), and `effort` is clamped to `[previous - slew * dt, previous + slew * dt]` (`lines 167-169`). Thus rising demand, falling `ceiling_nm`, and shift-cut entry/exit are all rate-bounded by `effort_slew_nm_s * dt`.
    - Entering `PedalMode::coasting` via `braking`, `no_effort`, or `cadence` (`pedaling.cpp:199-214`) sets `state_.effort = 0.` (`line 210`) and returns `.effort_nm = 0.` (`line 212`); crank-reposition coasting (`pedaling.cpp:106-122`) sets `state_.effort = 0.` on trigger (`line 107`) and returns `.effort_nm = 0.` (`line 120`, though if a snapshot is restored mid-reposition with `goal_rad` engaged and `s.effort > 0.`, lines `110-122` return `.effort_nm = 0.` without clearing `state_.effort`). `PedalMode::disabled` calls `reset()` (`line 65`, zeroing `state_.effort`) and returns `.effort_nm = 0.` (`line 74`).
    - *Snapshot boundary gap:* `PedalingPolicy::set_state` (`pedaling.cpp:20-44`) checks `nonnegative(s.effort)` and `if (s.coasting && !s.target_phase_rad) throw`, but permits `s.coasting == true` (with `target_phase_rad` set) and `s.effort > 0.`, so the first `PedalMode::pedaling` tick after such a `set_state`/`restore` slews from `s.effort` instead of `0.`.
  - **Axis 2 (Product Justification): Confirmed Requirement.** Directly mandated by `docs/agents/simulation-decisions.md` «Действует» item 17 (`lines 198-202`: human crank effort always passes through configured `effort_slew_nm_s`, `300 N·m/s`, on initial demand, restart after coasting, shift cuts, and rider reductions), «Решено — не сделано» item 12 completed 2026-11-09/10 (`lines 631-639, 654-667`), «Действует» items 9 & 44 (`lines 91-95, 501-503`: zero driving crank torque in coasting), and `docs/RIDE.md:178-181`.
- **Sub-clause (b) — Coasting Entry (`braking` / `stop_time_s`) vs. `[resume_below_rpm, coast_above_rpm]` Schmitt Trigger:**
  - **Axis 1 (Implementation Compliance): Holds deterministically, exhibiting cross-reason hysteresis latching.** In `PedalingPolicy::update` (`pedaling.cpp:135-141, 199-205`):
    - Line `135` selects `threshold = state_.coasting ? config_.resume_below_rpm : config_.coast_above_rpm`, and line `136` computes `excessive = config_.enabled && engaged(state_.cadence_ema) >= threshold`.
    - Any ordinary coasting tick (`CoastReason::braking`, `CoastReason::no_effort`, or `CoastReason::cadence`) initializes the `stop_time_s` deceleration ramp on `!state_.coasting` (`lines 199-204`) and sets `state_.coasting = true` (`line 205`).
    - Consequently, if the rider is pedaling at `cadence_ema = 110 rpm` inside `[resume_below_rpm, coast_above_rpm)` (`[105, 120)` rpm in `viewer_physics_welded.toml:76-77`) and briefly applies caller brake (`braking = true`) or eases `effort = 0.` for one tick, `state_.coasting` flips to `true`. On the next tick when `braking = false` and `effort > 0.`, `threshold` is `105 rpm`, so `110 >= 105` evaluates `excessive = true` and **latches `CoastReason::cadence`** until `cadence_ema < 105 rpm`, even though `coast_above_rpm` (`120 rpm`) was never reached. Conversely, crank-reposition coasting explicitly sets `state_.coasting = false` (`line 107`), and rider-owned braking (`step.cpp:2411`) leaves `legs_coast = false`.
  - **Axis 2 (Product Justification): Incidental Python-Port Coupling / Unresolved Product Decision.** `viewer_physics_welded.toml:72-77` and `simulation-decisions.md:79-80, 113` define `[resume_below_rpm, coast_above_rpm]` strictly as high-cadence spin-out hysteresis. Reusing `state_.coasting` for both `stop_time_s` ramp initialization and Schmitt-trigger state is an unexamined carryover from `pedaling.py` preserved under bitwise port parity (`docs/agents/native-engineering.md:33-35`). Moreover, for the primary `SpindleController` (`spindle_controller.cpp:1726-1733`), `stop_time_s`'s `target_phase_rad`/`target_rate_rad_s` ramp is unread during ordinary coasting after coasting leg braking was removed (`simulation-decisions.md:96-99`).
- **Sub-clause (c) — `AssistController` Brake Reset, Power-Capped Run-On Hold, Single-Shot Guard & Snapshot Rejection:**
  - **Axis 1 (Implementation Compliance): Violated on snapshot rejection; runtime sub-clauses hold (modulo binary64 product underflow).**
    1. *Immediate reset on `braking == true`:* **Holds.** `AssistController::step` (`native/src/drivetrain/motor.cpp:59-62`) executes `if (braking) { reset(); return 0.; }`, zeroing `torque`, `release_hold_left_s`, `hold_power_w`, `hold_torque_nm`, `last_gain`, and `pedaling` before any gain/hold logic.
    2. *Run-on delivery cap:* **Holds.** While `state_.release_hold_left_s > 0.` (`motor.cpp:104-108`), `release_hold_left_s` decrements by `dt` (clamped at `0.`), `delivery = omega > 0. ? std::min({state_.hold_power_w / omega, state_.hold_torque_nm, limit}) : 0.`, and final output is `std::max(0., std::min({delivery, limit, cap}))` (`line 108`).
    3. *No re-arming after hold expiry until `target >= state_.torque`:* **Holds for normal magnitudes; tiny underflow exception.** When `target < state_.torque` and `state_.hold_power_w <= 0. && omega > 0.` (`motor.cpp:81-86`), `hold_power_w = state_.torque * omega` and `release_hold_left_s = config_.release_hold_s`. After `release_hold_left_s` hits `0.`, `hold_power_w > 0.` remains set as the spent marker while `target < state_.torque`, blocking re-entry into line `82` until `target >= state_.torque` clears `hold_power_w = 0.`, `hold_torque_nm = 0.`, `release_hold_left_s = 0.` (`lines 87-91`). *Edge exception:* if `state_.torque > 0.` and `omega > 0.` are sub-`1e-162` doubles whose product `state_.torque * omega` underflows to `0.0`, `step()` writes `hold_power_w = 0.0` alongside `release_hold_left_s = config_.release_hold_s` (and sets `state_.torque = 0.0` at `line 110`).
    4. *Rejecting `release_hold_left_s > 0 && hold_power_w == 0` snapshots:* **Violated.** `AssistController::set_state` (`motor.cpp:28-35`) checks only `nonnegative(...)` on each of the five scalar fields. `decode_policies` (`state_codec.cpp:280-282, 333-339`), `PolicySet::validate_state` (`policy_set.hpp:46-57`), and `drivetrain::validate(const DriveSnapshot&, ...)` (`writers/drivetrain.cpp:25-85, 1213-1220`) add **zero** cross-field checks on `AssistSnapshot`. Restoring `{.torque = 30., .hold_power_w = 0., .hold_torque_nm = 0., .release_hold_left_s = 0.05}` succeeds; if the next `step()` has `target < 30.` and `omega > 0.`, `state_.hold_power_w <= 0.` (`motor.cpp:82`) re-arms `release_hold_left_s = config_.release_hold_s` (`0.3 s`), overwriting the restored countdown.
  - **Axis 2 (Product Justification): Mixed.** Brake reset, `release_hold_s` (`0.3 s`) power-and-torque-capped run-on, and single-shot hold expiry are **Confirmed Requirements** (`simulation-decisions.md` «Действует» item 16, `lines 179-190`). Encoding the spent state via `hold_power_w > 0 && release_hold_left_s == 0` (`config_types.hpp:99-101`, `motor.cpp:77-91`) is an **Inferred Engineering Contract**. Rejecting `release_hold_left_s > 0 && hold_power_w == 0` snapshots is an **Unimplemented Relational Hypothesis** (absent from all docs and validators).

## 3. Exceptions, Cross-Module Dependencies & Failure Consequences
- **Transactional Staging & Atomicity:** `DrivetrainWriter` stages policy advances on scratch copies (`pedaling_scratch_`, `shifting_scratch_`, `assist_scratch_` in `native/src/writers/drivetrain.cpp:614-619, 652-659, 822-833, 862-867`) inside `TickBank` and validates via `validate_tick()` before memory-only publication in `commit(PreparedTick)` (`drivetrain.cpp:967-1022`). `PhysicalStep::advance_physics` wraps each step in `Stepper::mutate` (`native/src/runtime/step.cpp:1648-1665`, `native/src/stepper.hpp:518-535`), poisoning the stepper if an engine failure escapes after partial live-model mutation (`docs/NATIVE_POLICIES.md:31-51`).
- **Probe Isolation:** Non-advancing probes (`advance == false` in `stage_prepare` / `stage_components`, `drivetrain.cpp:604-619, 777-789, 969-987`; `PhysicalStep::probe_step_inputs`, `step.cpp:2045-2090`) detach `pending_actuation`/`last_time_s` and write telemetry only to `live_.probe_last` without mutating `pedaling_` or `assist_`.
- **Braking Channel Split (`BrakeResolution`):** `PhysicalStep::resolve_brakes` (`step.cpp:2394-2417`) sets `legs_coast = request.caller_braking` (`line 2411`) and `motor_braking = request.caller_braking || rider_braking` (`line 2412`). Thus caller brakes trigger BOTH `PedalingPolicy` coasting (`CoastReason::braking`) and `AssistController::reset()`, rider-owned launch/reaction brakes reset `AssistController` (`drivetrain.cpp:861`) WITHOUT coasting the rider's legs, and rollback hold (`rollback_brake_demand`) triggers neither.
- **Ceiling vs. Shift-Cut Ordering (`cut_after_ceiling`):** When `RiderIntent::output` marks `effort_limited = true` (front-lift reaction `effort_factor != 1.` or active launch plan, `native/src/rider/intent.cpp:580-594`), `PhysicalStep` passes `cut_after_ceiling = true` (`step.cpp:1363-1371`), capping `target` by `ceiling_nm` BEFORE multiplying `torque_factor` (`pedaling.cpp:153-159`) so shift cuts are not swallowed when `ceiling_nm < target * torque_factor`.
- **External Caps & Post-Solve Settlement Feedback on Assist Hold:** `DrivetrainWriter::stage_components_telemetry` (`drivetrain.cpp:868-875, 894-895`) clamps `m.request` by `control.motor_limit_nm` and overwrites `bank.assist.torque = m.delivered`. After `mj_step`, `stage_settle()` (`drivetrain.cpp:1053-1068, 1174-1175`) overwrites `assist_.torque` with solved `actuator_force[mid_drive]` (raising `ArithmeticError` if `< -1e-10` or `> pending->requested + 1e-8`). If `motor_limit_nm`, `request = 0.`, `omega <= 0.`, or actuator clamping drives `assist.torque` down to `<= next_target`, the next step's `if (target < state_.torque)` (`motor.cpp:81, 87-91`) evaluates false and clears `hold_power_w`, `hold_torque_nm`, and `release_hold_left_s` early.
- **Boundary Config Cases Outside P03's Stated Preconditions:** `validate(const PedalingConfig&)` (`native/src/config_validation.hpp:124`) allows `effort_slew_nm_s == 0.`, which disables rate limiting (`if (slew <= 0.) effort = target` at `pedaling.cpp:165`); `validate(const AssistConfig&)` (`config_validation.hpp:175`) allows `release_hold_s == 0.`, which arms `hold_power_w` at `motor.cpp:83-85` but skips `if (state_.release_hold_left_s > 0.)` at `motor.cpp:104`, falling back to first-order `tau`/`slew` decay. P03's stated assumptions (`effort_slew_nm_s > 0`, `release_hold_s >= 0`) properly bound both.

## 4. Located Test Coverage vs. Unexercised Gaps
- **Actively Asserted Paths:**
  - *Sub-clause (a) unit & full-ride integration:* `native/tests/reference/test_pedaling_policy.cpp` asserts `effort_slew_nm_s == 0.` instant tracking (`16-24`), rising/falling slew under `PedalMode::pedaling` (`26-38`), immediate `0.` drop on `no_effort` coasting + slewed resume (`40-50`), falling `ceiling_nm` slewing (`52-77`), and shift `torque_factor` slewing (`79-111`). `native/tests/reference/test_pedaling_cut_order.cpp:10-44` asserts `cut_after_ceiling` ordering (`ceiling_nm` before `torque_factor`) with slew disabled (`0.`). `native/tests/reference/test_seated_pedaling_cycle.cpp` asserts `300 N·m/s` (`0.375 N·m/step` at `dt = 0.00125 s`) end-to-end across first command, coasting drop to `0.`, and restart (`376-463`), shift-cut entry/exit (`468-548`), and falling rider `effort_ceiling_nm` (`553-623`). `native/tests/reference/test_crank_reposition.cpp:17-125` asserts reposition coasting zeroes effort and aborts on `braking`.
  - *Sub-clause (b) cadence hysteresis & coasting:* `test_pedaling_policy.cpp:113-155` asserts `[resume_below_rpm, coast_above_rpm]` (`[105, 118]` rpm) Schmitt trigger transitions driven purely by cadence, plus `cadence_ema` smoothing (`157-191`) and `coasting && !target_phase_rad` rejection in `set_state` (`281-294`). `native/tests/reference/test_rider_brake_channel.cpp:365-680` asserts `BrakeResolution` (`legs_coast` vs. `motor_braking` vs. `rollback`).
  - *Sub-clause (c) assist hold & braking:* `native/tests/reference/test_assist_controller.cpp` asserts immediate `0.` on `braking` (`35`), hold arming on stop-pedaling and mid-pedal torque drop (`155-182`), single-shot expiry without re-arming during post-hold filter decay (`170-173`, `181-182`), `hold_power_w / omega` attenuation on accelerating shaft (`185-204`), `hold_torque_nm` cap on slowing shaft and `0.` on stopped shaft (`206-226`), `braking` clearing `torque`, `hold_power_w`, and `release_hold_left_s` mid-hold (`228-239`), and re-arming after rider resumes (`241-256`).
- **Unexercised Paths & Contradictory Test Evidence (Gaps):**
  1. **Cross-reason hysteresis trap in (b) is untested as a specification:** No unit or integration test exercises `braking = true` (or `effort = 0.`) -> release inside `[resume_below_rpm, coast_above_rpm)` to assert whether `PedalMode::pedaling` or `CoastReason::cadence` is expected (`test_native_drive_policies.cpp:38-49` only replays Python parity vectors).
  2. **Snapshot relational invariant in (c) is NOT tested for rejection — existing fuzzers assert acceptance of its negation:** `test_assist_controller.cpp:258-281` tests roundtrip of a valid armed hold and negative-scalar rejection (`release_hold_left_s = -0.1`, `hold_power_w = -1.`), never `release_hold_left_s > 0 && hold_power_w == 0`. Worse, `native/tests/reference/test_native_drive_policies.cpp:354-367` explicitly constructs `{.torque = 18.5, .hold_power_w = 185., .hold_torque_nm = 18.5, .release_hold_left_s = 0.12}` alongside arbitrary combinations in `native/tests/reference/test_native_state_fuzz.cpp:94-101, 224-273`, where `valid_policy_state` / `mutate_valid` independently fuzz `hold_power_w`, `hold_torque_nm`, and `release_hold_left_s` across `{0., 0.12, 19.5}` and **require `set_state` / `decode_policy_snapshot` to succeed**!
  3. **`PedalingSnapshot` `coasting && effort > 0.` across `set_state`:** Untested for rejection; `test_native_state_fuzz.cpp:69-77` independently sets `coasting = true` (fixing `target_phase_rad = 0.5`) with nonzero `_effort` (`22.0`) and asserts `restore` succeeds.
  4. **Post-solve `settle()` / `motor_limit_nm` interaction with active `AssistController` hold:** No test asserts early hold cancellation on step `k+1` when `motor_limit_nm = 0` or actuator clamping sets `assist.torque = 0` on step `k`.

## 5. Recommended Contract / FSM Refinement
Split and refine `P03` into three explicit, enforceable contracts and one tracked product decision:
1. **`P03a` (Pedaling Effort Slew & Coasting Zero-Effort Contract — Enforce at Snapshot Boundary):** Keep the active-pedaling slew bound `|effort_k - effort_{k-1}| <= effort_slew_nm_s * dt` (for `effort_slew_nm_s > 0`, `dt > 0`) and immediate `0 N·m` drop on any `PedalMode::coasting` or `PedalMode::disabled` return (`pedaling.cpp:74, 118-120, 210-212`). Tighten `PedalingPolicy::set_state` (`pedaling.cpp:20-44`) to reject `(s.coasting || (s.reposition && s.reposition->goal_rad)) && s.effort != 0.` (or scope `P03a` explicitly to reset-reachable trajectories until snapshot fuzzers are updated).
2. **`P03b` (Cadence Hysteresis vs. Brake/Zero-Effort Coasting — Resolve Product Intent):** Escalate to the user (`simulation-decisions.md` «Не решено»): should `PedalingSnapshot::coasting` be split into (i) `cadence_cutoff_latched` (engaged only when `cadence_ema >= coast_above_rpm` and cleared when `cadence_ema < resume_below_rpm`) and (ii) `coast_profile_active` (for `stop_time_s`, or retire `stop_time_s` altogether for `SpindleController` per «Действует» 9)? Until decided, specify current behavior strictly as Python-oracle parity (`pedaling.cpp:135, 205`), not a physical requirement.
3. **`P03c` (Motor Assist Brake Reset, Power-Capped Run-On Hold & Snapshot Relational Invariant):**
   - Retain the runtime guarantees: `braking == true` zeroes all `AssistSnapshot` fields (`motor.cpp:59-62`); active hold (`release_hold_left_s > 0`) caps output by `omega > 0 ? min({hold_power_w / omega, hold_torque_nm, ceiling, cap}) : 0.` (`motor.cpp:104-108`); and non-underflowing holds (`hold_power_w > 0`) do not re-arm after expiry while `target < state_.torque` (`motor.cpp:81-91`), noting that `DrivetrainWriter::settle()` (`writers/drivetrain.cpp:1064-1067`) updates `state_.torque` to solved actuator torque.
   - To make the snapshot-rejection clause true, add relational checks in `AssistController::set_state` (`motor.cpp:28-35`): reject `release_hold_left_s > 0. && (hold_power_w <= 0. || hold_torque_nm <= 0.)` and `hold_power_w > 0. && hold_torque_nm <= 0.`, simultaneously guard underflow in `AssistController::step` (`motor.cpp:82-86`: only arm `release_hold_left_s` when `const double power = state_.torque * omega; power > 0.`), and update `test_native_state_fuzz.cpp` / `test_native_drive_policies.cpp` generators accordingly.

# Stage 3 (`R1`) Independent Research Report — `P04`: Gear-Shifting Snapshot Coherence, Cut-Window Threshold Agreement & Launch Upshift Sub-Machine

- **Inspected Commit:** `0502660dd14bd1021b3da90a61e20dcb1d5805a1`
- **Compliance Verdict (`R1` Agent):** `partially_violated`
- **Product Justification Verdict (`R1` Agent):** `mixed_subclauses`

## 1. Producers, Mutators, Consumers & Potential Breakers
- **State Owners & Constructors:**
  - `drivetrain::ShiftingSnapshot` (`native/src/config_types.hpp:84-93`): owned by `CadenceShifter::state_` (`native/src/drivetrain/shifting.hpp:179`; ctor `:118-135` validates config, sorts `cassette`, checks `rear_teeth` membership if `enabled`, calls `reset()` `:140-144`), `PolicySet::shifting` (`native/src/drivetrain/policy_set.hpp:63`, public member), and `DrivetrainWriter::shifting_` + `shifting_scratch_` (`native/src/writers/drivetrain.hpp:502,516`; `reset()` `native/src/writers/drivetrain.cpp:274-278,448`).
  - `rider::LaunchPlan::State` (`native/src/rider/launch_plan.hpp:40-53`): owned by `LaunchPlan::state_` (`:105`; `reset()` `native/src/rider/launch_plan.cpp:71`) inside `RiderIntent::Core::plan` (`native/src/rider/intent.cpp:382`).
- **Advancing Mutators & Non-Advancing Probes:**
  - `CadenceShifter::update` (`native/src/drivetrain/shifting.cpp:26-208`): decays `cooldown_s`/`cut_remaining_s` (`:58-59`), handles `stationary_target_teeth` (`:66-107`), `RiderShift::request` (`:108-137`), or auto-band scan (`:138-181`), and commits shifts (`:98-106,198-207`).
  - `DrivetrainWriter::prepare` -> `stage_pedal_advance` (`native/src/writers/drivetrain.cpp:477-501,661-760`) stages into `shifting_scratch_`; `DrivetrainWriter::commit` (`:967-1002`) copies scratch into `shifting_` iff `advance && active`. `PhysicalStep::probe_step_inputs` (`native/src/runtime/step.cpp:2952-3012`) calls `apply_forces(true, /*advance=*/false, ...)` leaving committed states untouched.
  - `LaunchPlan::arm_start`/`arm` (`native/src/rider/launch_plan.cpp:76-78,122-127`), `LaunchPlan::shifting` (`:138-153`), `LaunchPlan::update` (`:155-226`), driven by `RiderIntent::tick` (`native/src/rider/intent.cpp:521-572`) and `resolve_full` (`:598-630`).
- **Restore / Codec Boundaries & Potential Breakers:**
  - `CadenceShifter::set_state` (`native/src/drivetrain/shifting.cpp:8-23`): checks scalar domains only; skips all relational invariants.
  - `PolicySet::restore` / `validate_state` (`native/src/drivetrain/policy_set.hpp:34-57`) via `decode_policy_snapshot` (`native/src/drivetrain/state_codec.cpp:531-535`): omits `shift_count == 0 ==> rear_teeth == from_teeth` and `cut_remaining_s <= cooldown_s`.
  - `DrivetrainWriter::restore` / `drivetrain::validate(DriveSnapshot)` (`native/src/writers/drivetrain.cpp:25-85,1213-1261`) via `decode_drive_snapshot` (`state_codec.cpp:544-577`): omits those same two clauses.
  - `LaunchPlan::restore` (`native/src/rider/launch_plan.cpp:257-311`) via `RiderIntent::restore` (`intent.cpp:653-706`) and `decode_rider_intent_state` (`native/src/rider/state_codec.cpp:2559-2691`): permits nonzero `request_*`/`blocked_teeth`/`retry_tick` in `release` (`:289-296`), `handover`, and `blend` (`:297-308`).
- **Consumers:**
  - `drivetrain::shift_torque_factor` (`native/src/drivetrain/shifting.hpp:100-114`), `PedalingPolicy::update` (`native/src/drivetrain/pedaling.cpp:153-163`), `DrivetrainWriter::shifts` (`writers/drivetrain.cpp:191-223`), `DrivetrainWriter::stage_pedal_advance` ideal-hub sweep (`:726`), `PhysicalStep::apply_forces_body` (`native/src/runtime/step.cpp:1277-1333,1466-1470`), and `commands_of` (`native/src/rider/intent.cpp:405-419`).

## 2. Sub-Clause Evidence Analysis (Supporting & Opposing)
- **Sub-Clause (a) — `ShiftingSnapshot` Coherence:**
  - **Axis 1 (Compliance): PARTIALLY VIOLATED (holds on committed trajectories; violated on `restore`/`set_state`).**
    - *Committed states:* Holds under stated assumptions (`0 <= shift_cut_duration_s <= shift_cooldown_s`, sorted non-empty `cassette`, `dt > 0`). `CadenceShifter::reset()` (`shifting.hpp:140-144`) and `DrivetrainWriter::reset()` (`writers/drivetrain.cpp:274-276,448`) initialize `shift_count = 0`, `direction = "none"`, `cooldown_s = cut_remaining_s = 0.`, `rear_teeth = from_teeth = gearing.rear_teeth` (validated in `cassette` when `enabled`, `shifting.hpp:132-133`). `CadenceShifter::update` decays both timers monotonically via `std::max(0., x - dt)` (`shifting.cpp:58-59`), preserving `cut_remaining_s <= cooldown_s`. Stationary commits (`:98-106`) set `direction = "down"`, `++shift_count`, `cooldown_s = shift_cooldown_s`, `cut_remaining_s = 0.`; ordinary commits (`:198-207`) set `direction` in `{"up","down"}`, `++shift_count`, `cooldown_s = shift_cooldown_s`, `cut_remaining_s = shift_cut_duration_s <= shift_cooldown_s`, with `from_teeth, rear_teeth` in `cassette`.
    - *Restored states:* (1) `CadenceShifter::set_state` (`shifting.cpp:8-23`, reachable directly via public `PolicySet::shifting` at `policy_set.hpp:63`) enforces **none** of the four relational clauses. (2) Both `PolicySet::restore` (`policy_set.hpp:48-56`) and `DrivetrainWriter::restore` (`writers/drivetrain.cpp:38-47`) enforce `(shift_count > 0) <==> (direction != "none")`, `shift_count == 0 ==> (cooldown_s == 0. && cut_remaining_s == 0.)`, and `cassette` membership when `enabled`, **but omit** `shift_count == 0 ==> rear_teeth == from_teeth` and `cut_remaining_s <= cooldown_s`.
  - **Axis 2 (Product Justification): MIXED.** Fail-fast relational restore checks (`(shift_count > 0) <==> (direction != "none")`, zero timers at `shift_count == 0`, `cut_remaining_s <= cooldown_s`, cassette membership) are relevant engineering contracts under `docs/agents/native-engineering.md:27-33`. However, requiring `shift_count == 0 ==> rear_teeth == from_teeth` on restored `PolicySet` is incidental/overly-strong: `from_teeth` is only consulted when `cut_remaining_s > 0.` (`writers/drivetrain.cpp:70-72,726`), and `test_native_drive_policies.cpp:279-282` explicitly restores `shift_count = 0, rear_teeth = 51, from_teeth = 45` and asserts round-trip preservation.

- **Sub-Clause (b) — Cut-Window Threshold Agreement (`> 1e-12` vs `> 0.0`):**
  - **Axis 1 (Compliance): VIOLATED.**
    - *`> 1e-12` group:* `CadenceShifter::update` stationary gate (`shifting.cpp:87`) and rider-request gate (`:112`; `admitted` also uses `cooldown_s <= 1e-12` at `:64`); `PhysicalStep::apply_forces_body` (`runtime/step.cpp:1285`: `.cut_active = shifting.cut_remaining_s > 1e-12`), consumed by `LaunchPlan::shifting` (`launch_plan.cpp:146`) and `LaunchPlan::update` `LaunchPhase::handover` (`:212`).
    - *`> 0.0` group:* `shift_torque_factor` / `CadenceShifter::torque_factor` (`shifting.hpp:103,163`: `cut_remaining_s <= 0.`), consumed by `DrivetrainWriter::stage_pedal_advance` (`writers/drivetrain.cpp:680,751`) -> `PedalingPolicy::update` (`pedaling.cpp:158-160`) and `TelemetryField::shift_torque_factor` (`writers/drivetrain.cpp:221-222`); `TelemetryField::shift_active` (`:203`: `s.cut_remaining_s > 0.`); ideal-hub ratio interpolation (`:726`: `cut_remaining_s > 0.`); and `drivetrain::validate(DriveSnapshot)` (`:63`: `cut_remaining_s > 0.`). `CadenceShifter` even disagrees with itself between `update()` and `torque_factor()`.
    - *Reachability:* `cut_remaining_s in (0.0, 1e-12]` is reachable via `restore`/`set_state` AND via normal quantized stepping (e.g., exact decimal `shift_cut_duration_s = 0.27, dt = 0.03` yields `2^-55` after 9 steps; or `SimulationPhysicsConfig` `1e-9` slack at `configuration/validate.cpp:1244-1248`).
  - **Axis 2 (Product Justification): RELEVANT ENGINEERING CONTRACT.** `simulation-decisions.md:81-86,148-152` and `2026-11-11-rider-launch-plan-design.md:108,214-216` define a single 0.20 s shift-cut concept ("план ждёт конца среза"); split thresholds are an accidental porting artifact.

- **Sub-Clause (c) — `LaunchPlan` Upshift Sub-Machine (`FSM-010`):**
  - **Axis 1 (Compliance): PARTIALLY VIOLATED.**
    - *No concurrent `shift_request` & `stationary_rear_teeth`:* **Holds.** `commands_of` (`rider/intent.cpp:410-413`) emits `stationary_rear_teeth` only in `LaunchPhase::hold` and `shift_request` iff `c.shift_up`, which is true only in `LaunchPhase::accelerate` (`launch_plan.cpp:148,200,207,269`). Defense-in-depth guards reject simultaneous values in `rider::validate(RiderCommands)` (`intent.cpp:103-105`) and `CadenceShifter::update` (`shifting.cpp:39-40`).
    - *Bridges 0.15 s `Perception` cog lag without double-shifts:* **Holds when `shift_cooldown_s >= period_s` (shipped `period_s == dt_s`), conditional otherwise.** On acceptance (`fb.shift_count > n.request_count`, `launch_plan.cpp:140`), `LaunchPlan::shifting` clears `n.shift_up = false` and latches `n.blocked_teeth = n.request_teeth`, suppressing new requests (`p.senses.rear_teeth != n.blocked_teeth`, `:147`) until delayed `Perception` (`0.15 s`, `perception.cpp:69-112`) reports the new cog. Two edge gaps exist: (1) if `period_steps_ > 1` (`intent.cpp:612-629`) and `shift_cooldown_s < period_s`, held `commands.shift_request` double-shifts across physics sub-steps; (2) `LaunchPlan::restore` (`launch_plan.cpp:289-296`) fails to reject nonzero `blocked_teeth`/`request_*` in `LaunchPhase::release`.
    - *Clears retry/block latches on phase exit or reset:* **Violated on `accelerate -> handover`.** `reset()` (`:71`), `!eligible` (`:159`), `arm()` (`:123`), and entry to `done` (`:215,221`) clear `State{}` completely, **but** `accelerate -> handover` (`:205-208`) clears ONLY `n.shift_up = false`, leaving `request_count`, `request_teeth`, `blocked_teeth`, and `retry_tick` populated throughout `handover` and `blend`.
  - **Axis 2 (Product Justification): MIXED.** Stationary `hold` downshift, single-step upshift bridging across 0.15 s reaction delay, `0.25 s` `shift_retry_s`, and withdrawing pending `shift_up` on handover are confirmed requirements (`simulation-decisions.md:148-153`, `2026-11-11-rider-launch-plan-design.md:108-109`, `2026-11-15-stationary-launch-gear-and-restarts.md:5-8`). Requiring `blocked_teeth`/`retry_tick`/`request_*` to be cleared at `accelerate -> handover` (instead of `done`/`arm`/`reset`) is an incidental over-strengthening contradicted by `test_launch_plan.cpp:447-460`.

## 3. Exceptions, Cross-Module Dependencies & Failure Consequences
- **Config & Quantization Dependencies:**
  - `drivetrain::validate(ShiftingConfig)` (`native/src/config_validation.hpp:143-165`), `post_init(ShiftingConfig)` (`native/src/configuration/validate.cpp:896-918`), and `decode_drive_policies` (`native/src/writer_config.hpp:982-1013`) enforce unique `cassette` (`>=3`), `0 <= shift_cut_duration_s <= shift_cooldown_s`, and `rear_teeth` in `cassette` when `enabled`. Standalone `CadenceShifter`/`DrivetrainWriter` do NOT require `dt` quantization.
  - `post_init(SimulationPhysicsConfig)` (`native/src/configuration/validate.cpp:1244-1248`) enforces step quantization only within `1e-9` (`|round(v / timestep_s) * timestep_s - v| <= 1e-9`), wide enough to admit `shift_cut_duration_s = N * timestep_s + 5e-13`. Even exact decimal multiples in IEEE-754 binary64 (e.g., `shift_cut_duration_s = 0.27`, `dt = 0.03`) leave `cut_remaining_s = 2^-55` (`2.78e-17`) on the 9th step, entering `(0.0, 1e-12]`.
- **Consequences of Violated Clauses:**
  - **Mid-cut auto-shift after restore (`(a)`):** `CadenceShifter::update` checks `cut_remaining_s > 1e-12` in stationary/rider-request branches (`native/src/drivetrain/shifting.cpp:87,112`) but checks ONLY `admitted` (`cooldown_s <= 1e-12`) in the automatic band branch (`:64,138`). Because `PolicySet::restore`, `DrivetrainWriter::restore`, and `CadenceShifter::set_state` allow `cut_remaining_s > cooldown_s` (e.g., `cooldown_s = 0, cut_remaining_s = 0.2`), an automatic shift can fire mid-cut.
  - **Premature handover & split cut semantics (`(b)`):** When `cut_remaining_s` is in `(0.0, 1e-12]`, `PhysicalStep` (`step.cpp:1285`) reports `cut_active = false`, allowing `LaunchPlan::handover` (`launch_plan.cpp:212`) to exit to `blend` and `CadenceShifter` (`:87,112`) to admit a new shift on the same step that `DrivetrainWriter` still applies cut `torque_factor < 1` (`shifting.hpp:103`), sweeps `ideal_hub` ratio (`writers/drivetrain.cpp:726`), and publishes `shift_active = true` (`:203`).
  - **Decimated intention clock (`(c)`):** If `RiderIntent` runs with `period_s > dt_s` (`intent.cpp:612-629`) and `shift_cooldown_s < period_s`, held `shift_request = up` repeats across physics sub-steps before `LaunchPlan` sees `shift_count` advance. Shipped profiles set `period_s == timestep_s == 0.00125` and `shift_cooldown_s == 0.4`.
  - **Harmless post-`accelerate` latch retention (`(c)`):** Retaining `request_count`/`request_teeth`/`blocked_teeth`/`retry_tick` across `handover`/`blend` (`launch_plan.cpp:205-208`) has zero control effect because `shifting()` runs only in `accelerate` (`:200`) and `done`/`arm`/`reset` zero the full struct (`:71,123,215,221`). However, `LaunchPlan::restore` (`:289-296`) omitting latch-zero checks in `release` lets a crafted `release` snapshot carry a stale `blocked_teeth` into `accelerate`.

## 4. Located Test Coverage vs. Unexercised Gaps
- **Actively Exercised & Asserted:**
  - *`CadenceShifter` & torque-cut:* `native/tests/test_contracts.cpp:191-369` (ctor domains, unsorted cassette sorting, `80%`/`30%` cut factors, directionless snapshot); `native/tests/reference/test_cadence_shifter.cpp:82-510` (band hysteresis, cooldown, slip modes, `RiderShift` `accepted`/`deferred`/`rejected` during cut/cooldown/cassette edge, stationary `hold` jump, simultaneous `request` + `stationary_target_teeth` rejection at `:474-509`); `native/tests/reference/test_pedaling_cut_order.cpp:11-50` (`cut_after_ceiling` slew order).
  - *Policy / Writer / Runtime restore:* `native/tests/reference/test_native_drive_policies.cpp:122-160,276-305,421-462` (round-trip mid-cut restore, rejection of `direction`/`shift_count` mismatch, off-cassette teeth, and timers with `shift_count == 0`; **pins** `shift_count == 0` with `rear_teeth = 51 != from_teeth = 45` at `:279-282`); `native/tests/reference/test_native_drivetrain.cpp:449-515` (`DrivetrainWriter` mid-cut restore & `shift_time_s` invariant); `native/tests/reference/test_native_state_restore.cpp:355-396,577-686` & `test_native_state_fuzz.cpp:33-411`.
  - *`LaunchPlan` & `RiderIntent`:* `native/tests/reference/test_launch_plan.cpp:259-462` (0.15 s `Perception` lag bridging via `blocked_teeth` at `:259-294`, `deferred` persistence and `rejected` `0.25 s` `retry_tick` at `:296-325`, `handover` waiting on `cut_active` at `:327-357`, `restore` guards at `:425-460` — **pins** nonzero `blocked_teeth`/`retry_tick` in `handover` at `:447-460`); `native/tests/reference/test_launch_intent.cpp:365-433,521-571` (`hold` `stationary_rear_teeth` vs `accelerate` `shift_request`); slow acceptance `native/tests/reference/test_rider_launch_rides_normal.cpp:96-115`.
- **Unexercised Paths & Coverage Gaps:**
  - No test exercises `cut_remaining_s` in `(0.0, 1e-12]` across `DrivetrainWriter`, `PhysicalStep`, and `LaunchPlan::handover` (missing located test for threshold disagreement).
  - No test exercises restoring `cut_remaining_s > cooldown_s` into `PolicySet`/`DrivetrainWriter` followed by `CadenceShifter::update` in auto-band mode (mid-cut shift bug unexercised).
  - No test checks `LaunchPlan::restore` with nonzero `blocked_teeth`/`request_*`/`retry_tick` in `LaunchPhase::release`, nor `LaunchPlan` upshifts when `control_interval_s > timestep_s` and `shift_cooldown_s == 0`.

## 5. Recommended Contract / FSM Refinement
Split `P04` into three sharper properties aligned with confirmed requirements and engineering contracts:
1. **`P04a` (Shifting Snapshot & Cut-Order Invariant):**
   - Keep `(shift_count > 0) <==> (direction != "none")`, `shift_count == 0 ==> (cooldown_s == 0 && cut_remaining_s == 0)`, `0 <= cut_remaining_s <= cooldown_s`, and `enabled ==> rear_teeth, from_teeth in cassette` across all committed AND restored states (adding `cut_remaining_s <= cooldown_s` to `PolicySet::validate_state` at `policy_set.hpp:46-57` and `drivetrain::validate(DriveSnapshot)` at `writers/drivetrain.cpp:38-47`, and routing `CadenceShifter::set_state` or encapsulating `PolicySet::shifting`).
   - Scope `shift_count == 0 ==> rear_teeth == from_teeth` to **reset/committed trajectories** only (since `from_teeth` is dead state when `cut_remaining_s == 0` and `test_native_drive_policies.cpp:279-282` pins arbitrary in-cassette `from_teeth` on `PolicySet::restore`), and guard `CadenceShifter::update` auto-band admission (`shifting.cpp:64`) with `&& next.cut_remaining_s <= 1e-12` (or unified helper).
2. **`P04b` (Unified Torque-Cut Active Predicate):**
   - Extract one `constexpr bool shift_cut_active(const ShiftingSnapshot&)` helper and use it everywhere (`shifting.hpp:103`, `shifting.cpp:64,87,112`, `writers/drivetrain.cpp:63,203,726`, `runtime/step.cpp:1285`), eliminating the `(0.0, 1e-12]` split window.
3. **`P04c` (Launch Stationary/Upshift Protocol):**
   - Require: (i) mutual exclusion `!shift_request || !stationary_rear_teeth`; (ii) single-step upshift bridging across `Perception` delay under `shift_cooldown_s >= period_s` (or clear `commands.shift_request` after the first physics sub-step in `RiderIntent::resolve_full`); (iii) `shift_up == false` on exit from `accelerate`, full latch reset on `done`/`arm`/`reset`, and `request_count == 0 && request_teeth == 0 && blocked_teeth == 0 && retry_tick == -1` enforced in `LaunchPlan::restore` for `LaunchPhase::release` (`launch_plan.cpp:289-296`) just like `hold`.

# Stage 3 (`R1`) Independent Research Report — `P05`: `LaunchPlan`, `FrontLiftReaction`, `RiderIntent` & `SeatedPostureProgram` Restore Guards & Bounded Progress

- **Inspected Commit:** `0502660dd14bd1021b3da90a61e20dcb1d5805a1`
- **Compliance Verdict (`R1` Agent):** `partially_violated`
- **Product Justification Verdict (`R1` Agent):** `mixed_subclauses`

## 1. Producers, Mutators, Consumers & Potential Breakers
- **State Owners & Files (note: `SeatedClimbPolicy` and `SeatedPostureProgram` live in `intent.{hpp,cpp}`; separate `seated_climb.*` / `posture_program.*` files do not exist, `CMakeLists.txt:116-175`):**
  - `rider::LaunchPlan` (`native/src/rider/launch_plan.hpp:51-106`, `launch_plan.cpp:61-311`): owns `State s_` (`phase`, `phase_ticks`, `launch_ticks`, `blend_ticks`, `rise_ticks = -1`, `effort_nm`). Mutators: ctor (`:61-69`), `reset()` (`:71`), `arm_start()` (`:73-84`), `update()` (`:154-226`), `restore()` (`:257-311`). Pure readers: `command()` (`:86-98`), `stationary_target_teeth()` (`:228-238`), `shift_request()` (`:240-253`), `state()` (`:255`).
  - `rider::FrontLiftReaction` (`native/src/rider/front_lift_reaction.hpp:48-112`, `front_lift_reaction.cpp:95-284`): owns `State s_` (`phase`, `percepts_seen`, `onset_ticks`, `clear_ticks`, `effort_factor`, `lean_add_rad`, `std::deque<double> hand_n, crank_angle_rad, hand_average_n`). Mutators: ctor (`:95-103`), `reset()` (`:105`), `update()` (`:148-193`), `restore()` (`:208-284`).
  - `rider::Perception` (`native/src/rider/perception.hpp:42-83`, `perception.cpp:78-163`): owns `State s_` (`tick`, `queue`, `delayed`, `crank_prev_rad`, `crank_unwrapped_rad`, `pitch_prev_rad`, `pitch_unwrapped_rad`). Mutators: `reset()` (`:86`), `update()` (`:88-112`), `restore()` (`:118-163`).
  - `rider::SeatedPostureProgram` (`native/src/rider/intent.hpp:132-182`, `intent.cpp:128-266`) & `rider::SeatedClimbPolicy` (`intent.hpp:184-243`, `intent.cpp:270-396`): own `lean_rad_`, `trim_rad_`, `load_samples_`, `delayed_load_share_`, `pulses_` and `time_s_`, `signal_samples_`, `delayed_signals_`, `effort_nm_`, `surge_budget_j_`, `surge_cooldown_s_`, `surge_active_`. Mutators: `reset()`, `seed_lean()` (`:143-148`), `schedule_pulse()` (`:150-161`), `update()` (`:164-250`, `:322-380`), `restore()` (`:260-266`, `:388-396`).
  - `rider::RiderIntent` (`native/src/rider/intent.hpp:265-333`, `intent.cpp:469-715`): owns `std::unique_ptr<Core> core_, staged_` (`:331`). Mutators: `reset()` (`:491-502`), `seed_lean()` (`:504-507`), `arm_standstill_start()` (`:509-520`), `schedule_lean_pulse()` (`:522-527`), `resolve_full(..., advance=true)` via staged `tick()` + `std::swap(core_, staged_)` (`:621-628`), `restore()` (`:653-706`). Non-advancing probe: `resolve_full(..., advance=false)` copies `Core probe = *core_` (`:613-619`).
- **Codecs, Runtime Callers & Consumers:**
  - Wire codecs: `decode_launch_state` (`native/src/rider/senses_codec.cpp:549-575`), `decode_reaction_state` (`:496-525`), `decode_perception_state` (`:429-462`), `decode_intent_state` (`native/src/rider/state_codec.cpp:1076-1111`).
  - Initialization & snapshot callers: `finalize_initial_state` calls `seed_lean` + `arm_standstill_start` (`native/src/initialization/finalize.cpp:71-75`); `capture_initialized` / `compose_bootstrap` / `apply_bootstrap` (`native/src/initialization/capture.cpp:215`, `native/src/runtime/bootstrap.cpp:199, 416`); `SimulationRuntime::from_state_bytes` / `restore_state` (`native/src/runtime/runtime.cpp:666-795`); `PhysicalStep::restore` (`native/src/runtime/step.cpp:3074`).
  - Step-loop producer/consumers (`native/src/runtime/step.cpp:1269-1371, 1702-1720`): `PhysicalStep::apply_forces_body` computes `lean_limit_rad` (`:1291-1294`) and `min_lean_rad` via `SpindleController::min_torso_lean_rad` (`native/src/rider/spindle_controller.cpp:726-746`), calls `rider_intent_.resolve_full` (`step.cpp:1298-1305`), merges `rider_intent_.commands()` into `front_brake`/`rear_brake` (`:1309-1315`) and `SpindleController` (`:1337-1370`), and forwards `owns_shifting` / `stationary_target_teeth` / `shift_request` into `Stepper::step_drivetrain` -> `CadenceShifter::update` (`step.cpp:1705-1718`, `native/src/drivetrain/shifting.cpp:68-136`).
- **Concrete Breakers Identified:**
  - `LaunchPlan::restore` (`launch_plan.cpp:257-311`) + `RiderIntent::restore` (`intent.cpp:653-706`) omit `!config_.plan_enabled` guard; `LaunchPlan::update` (`launch_plan.cpp:157`) then freezes restored non-idle state forever.
  - `FrontLiftReaction::restore` (`front_lift_reaction.cpp:283`) assigns `s_ = s;` across three `std::deque<double>` fields without staging on `std::bad_alloc`.
  - `SeatedPostureProgram::restore` (`intent.cpp:260-266`) & `decode_intent_program` (`state_codec.cpp:1066-1068`) accept nonpositive `pulse.duration_s` and arbitrary `lean_rad`.
  - `SeatedPostureProgram::update` (`intent.cpp:204-205, 244-249`) lacks `std::min(max_forward_lean_rad, *lean_limit_rad)` on direct calls and lacks a post-slew upper clamp `lean_rad_ <= forward_limit` when `seed_lean` (`finalize.cpp:72`) exceeds `lean_limit_rad`.

## 2. Sub-Clause Evidence Analysis (Supporting & Opposing)
- **Sub-clause (a): `!config_.plan_enabled ==> state_.phase == LaunchPhase::idle` across construction, `arm_standstill_start()`, `update()`, and `restore()`**
  - **Axis 1 (Compliance — VIOLATED at `restore()`, then frozen by `update()`):** Construction initializes `s_.phase = LaunchPhase::idle` (`native/src/rider/launch_plan.hpp:56,105`, `launch_plan.cpp:61-69`). `LaunchPlan::arm_start` (`launch_plan.cpp:74`) and `RiderIntent::arm_standstill_start` (`intent.cpp:510`) guard `if (!config_.plan_enabled) return false;`. `LaunchPlan::update` (`launch_plan.cpp:157`) guards `if (!config_.plan_enabled) return command();` — preserving `idle` if already `idle`, **but** neither `LaunchPlan::restore` (`launch_plan.cpp:257-311`), `RiderIntent::restore` (`intent.cpp:653-706`), nor `decode_launch_state` (`senses_codec.cpp:549-575`) checks `config_.plan_enabled`. Restoring e.g. `phase = hold` onto a disabled `LaunchPlan` succeeds, publishes `front_brake = rear_brake = 1.` in `RiderIntent::restore` (`intent.cpp:699-702`), and never clears in `update()` because `:157` returns before `!in.eligible` (`:158`). (Contrast `FrontLiftReaction::restore` at `front_lift_reaction.cpp:231-236`, which rejects out-of-domain values when `!ease_enabled` / `!lean_enabled`.)
  - **Axis 2 (Product Justification — Confirmed Engineering Contract):** `launch_plan.hpp:6` explicitly specifies *"Off (always idle) unless rider_launch.plan_enabled"*, and `docs/agents/native-engineering.md:38-40` requires `restore` to validate state invariants up front.

- **Sub-clause (b): `RiderIntent::restore()` / `LaunchPlan::restore()` phase-specific tick bounds (`0 <= rise_ticks`, window upper bounds) & all-or-nothing exception safety**
  - **Axis 1 (Compliance — PARTIALLY VIOLATED):**
    1. `0 <= rise_ticks` is **false by design**: `rise_ticks` uses `-1` as the pre-rise sentinel (`launch_plan.hpp:71`); `LaunchPlan::restore` enforces `s.rise_ticks >= -1` (`launch_plan.cpp:271`) and requires `rise_ticks == -1` in `idle`/`done` (`:279`).
    2. **Missing upper bounds:** `LaunchPlan::restore` enforces `0 <= phase_ticks` (`< hold_ticks_` only in `release`? No: `0 <= phase_ticks` in `hold` `:283-286` — note `phase_ticks >= hold_ticks_` is reachable while `external_brake` holds! — `phase_ticks == 0` in `release..blend` `:289,300`, `0 <= launch_ticks < release_ticks_` in `release` `:291-292`, `release_ticks_ <= launch_ticks < max_launch_ticks_` in `accelerate` `:301-304`, and `0 <= blend_ticks < handover_ticks_` in `blend` `:305-307`). However, it allows `rise_ticks` up to `INT64_MAX` in any active phase (even `hold` at `phase_ticks == 0` where `ramp()` never ran), allows `launch_ticks` up to `INT64_MAX` in `handover`/`blend` (`:301`, where `launch_ticks` stopped incrementing at `<= max(release_ticks_, max_launch_ticks_)`), and `RiderIntent::restore` (`intent.cpp:680-683`) checks `launch_ticks <= delivered` and `hold` `phase_ticks <= delivered` but omits `rise_ticks <= delivered` and `blend_ticks <= delivered`. Furthermore, `SeatedPostureProgram::restore` (`intent.cpp:260-266`) and `SeatedClimbPolicy::restore` (`:388-396`) do zero domain validation, and `decode_intent_program` (`state_codec.cpp:1066-1068`) does not check `pulse.duration_s > 0` or `[0, 1]` on `front_load_share`.
    3. **Exception safety:** `LaunchPlan::restore` (`launch_plan.cpp:310`, trivially copyable `State`) and `RiderIntent::restore` (`intent.cpp:685-705`, staging into `*staged_` before pointer `std::swap(core_, staged_)`) are all-or-nothing. However, standalone `FrontLiftReaction::restore` (`front_lift_reaction.cpp:283`) executes `s_ = s;` across three `std::deque<double>` members without staging (`State candidate = s; s_ = std::move(candidate);` as `Perception::restore` does at `perception.cpp:161-162`), risking partial `s_` mutation on `std::bad_alloc`.
  - **Axis 2 (Product Justification — Mixed):** Up-front validation and atomic staging are confirmed rules (`docs/agents/native-engineering.md:38-40`, `intent.hpp:310-314`); `0 <= rise_ticks` is an erroneous property hypothesis contradicting the `-1` sentinel (`launch_plan.hpp:71`), and unbounded `hold.phase_ticks` while externally braked is physically reachable (`launch_plan.cpp:182`).

- **Sub-clause (c): Effort ceiling composition order (§42) and commanded `torso_lean_rad` in `[-max_backward_lean_rad, min(max_forward_lean_rad, lean_limit_rad)]`**
  - **Axis 1 (Compliance — Effort order HOLDS; `torso_lean_rad` PARTIALLY VIOLATED):**
    1. In `RiderIntent::tick` + `output` (`intent.cpp:556-567, 580-588`), when automatic effort applies (`!control.human_torque_nm.has_value()`), effort composes strictly as: `SeatedClimbPolicy::update` (`:556`, with `spend_surge = !owns`) -> `min` with `compute_climb_cap_nm` (`:558-562`) -> `LaunchPlan` override/convex blend (`:563-566`) -> `FrontLiftReaction` multiplicative `effort_factor` (`:584-588`, bitwise identity when `factor == 1.`).
    2. For `torso_lean_rad`: (i) `SeatedPostureProgram::update` (`intent.cpp:204-205`) computes `forward_limit = lean_limit_rad.value_or(config_.max_forward_lean_rad)` — **replacing** rather than taking `min`; only `PhysicalStep::apply_forces_body` (`step.cpp:1291-1294`) pre-clamps `std::min(config_.intent.max_forward_lean_rad, *rider_control_->lean_limit_rad())`; (ii) `SeatedPostureProgram::update` (`intent.cpp:241-249`) clamps `target` to `[backward_limit, forward_limit]` and post-clamps `lean_rad_ >= backward_limit` (`:247-248`), **but has NO post-slew upper clamp `lean_rad_ <= forward_limit`**. Because `seed_lean` (`intent.cpp:143-148`, called from `finalize.cpp:72`) and `validate.cpp:1137-1140` only enforce `start_lean_rad <= max_forward_lean_rad` (ignoring `rider_control_->lean_limit_rad()`), any `start_lean_rad` (or restored `lean_rad_`) in `(lean_limit_rad, max_forward_lean_rad]` commands `torso_lean_rad > min(max_forward_lean_rad, lean_limit_rad)` until slewed down at `lean_rate_rad_s * dt_s`.
  - **Axis 2 (Product Justification — Confirmed Requirement):** Both the 4-stage effort order and `min(max_forward_lean_rad, lean_limit_rad)` are confirmed in `docs/agents/simulation-decisions.md` «Действует» §12 (`:147-157`) and §42 (`:447-449`).

- **Sub-clause (d): Bounded return to `idle` once `LaunchPlan` leaves `hold` or `FrontLiftReaction` sees steady positive light-hand load**
  - **Axis 1 (Compliance — VIOLATED for `LaunchPlan`; HOLDS with two extreme-config edge cases for `FrontLiftReaction`):**
    1. `LaunchPlan` **never returns to `LaunchPhase::idle` upon completing a launch**: `handover` (when `handover_ticks_ == 0`, `launch_plan.cpp:215`) and `blend` (when `++n.blend_ticks >= handover_ticks_`, `:221`) transition to `LaunchPhase::done`, which stays `done` (or re-arms `done -> hold` on standstill at `:173`) and only becomes `idle` on `!in.eligible` (`:158-160`) or `reset()` (`:71`). Moreover, reaching `done` after leaving `hold` also requires `in.eligible` (`:158`), `config_.plan_enabled` (`:157`), `rise_ticks` not hitting `INT64_MAX` (`:165`), and external `!in.shift.cut_active` to exit `handover` (`:212`).
    2. `FrontLiftReaction` (`front_lift_reaction.cpp:114-192`): under arriving percepts with constant `hand_press_n = c > 0`, once `window_ticks_ + lag_ticks_` percepts fill `hand_n` and `hand_average_n`, `hand_term` is `false` for all `hand_drop_ratio < 1.0`; `deciding` clears in 1 tick (`:164`), `acting` enters `releasing` in `clear_ticks_ + 1` ticks (`:167-169`), and `releasing` reaches `idle` in `max(ceil((1 - effort_factor)/(ease_rate*period_s)), ceil(lean_add_rad/(lean_rate*period_s)))` ticks (`:181-187`). Two permitted boundary configs break this: (i) `hand_drop_ratio == 1.0` (`:53-54`) with oscillating `crank_rpm` (`span` varying so 1-ULP IEEE-754 sum/div rounding makes `average < 1.0 * reference` at `:129-145` while `pitch_rad > config_.pitch_rad`), and (ii) sub-ULP `rate * period_s_` (`:61,63`) after `restore()` into `releasing` (`step_toward` stalls when `value ± step == value` at `:43-46`).
  - **Axis 2 (Product Justification — Mixed):** Bounded handover to normal climbing and self-extinguishing reaction on a step unload (Criterion B) are confirmed in `simulation-decisions.md` §42 (`:404-415, 439-449`), whereas naming `LaunchPhase::idle` instead of `LaunchPhase::done` is a mis-stated property hypothesis (`launch_plan.hpp:23`, `launch_plan.cpp:172-174`).

## 3. Exceptions, Cross-Module Dependencies & Failure Consequences
- **Caller/override bypasses in `RiderIntent`:**
  - When `!config_.enabled || !active` (`native/src/rider/intent.cpp:606-607`) or `!control.rider_enabled` (`:575-576`), `RiderIntent` passes `control` through unchanged.
  - When caller supplies explicit `control.human_torque_nm` (`intent.cpp:551, 580`), `LaunchPlan` becomes ineligible (`s_ = State{}`) and all four effort-ceiling stages in sub-clause (c) are bypassed.
  - When caller supplies explicit `control.posture` (`intent.cpp:577-578`), `SeatedPostureProgram` lean output is ignored; `control.posture->torso_lean_rad` is only bounded by wire `[-0.8, 0.8]` (`native/src/rider/state_codec.cpp:995-998`).
  - Between quantized intention ticks (`step % period_steps_ != 0`, `intent.cpp:612-629`), `output()` holds the last tick's `core.intent` and `core.reaction->command()`.
- **External drivetrain dependency for `LaunchPlan::handover`:**
  - `LaunchPlan::update` (`native/src/rider/launch_plan.cpp:210-217`) has no internal timeout in `handover`; progress requires `!in.shift.cut_active`. In the coupled runtime (`native/src/runtime/step.cpp:1289, 1705-1718`), `CadenceShifter::update` (`native/src/drivetrain/shifting.cpp:59, 205`) sets `cut_remaining_s = shift_cut_duration_s` on shift commit and decrements it by `dt > 0` every physics step, bounding `cut_active` to `ceil((shift_cut_duration_s - 1e-12) / timestep_s)` steps unless continuous shifts re-arm it (impossible while `LaunchPlan::handover` owns shifting without issuing requests, `launch_plan.hpp:97`, `launch_plan.cpp:241`, `shifting.cpp:108-111`).
- **Arithmetic / counter overflow unwinds:**
  - `Perception::update` throws `std::overflow_error` if `tick == INT64_MAX` (`native/src/rider/perception.cpp:92`) or cumulative crank/pitch revolutions exceed `2^52` (`:72-73`).
  - `LaunchPlan::update` throws `std::overflow_error` if `rise_ticks == INT64_MAX` (`launch_plan.cpp:165-166`) — immediately reachable in 1 tick after restoring `rise_ticks = INT64_MAX` (`native/tests/reference/test_launch_plan.cpp:436-437`).
- **Consequences of the uncovered restore gaps:**
  - Restoring a non-idle `LaunchPlan::State` when `!config_.plan_enabled` permanently locks `RiderIntent::commands()` into the restored `LaunchCommand` (e.g., `hold` keeps `front_brake = 1.0`, `rear_brake = 1.0`, `owns_shifting = true` forever because `LaunchPlan::update` returns at `launch_plan.cpp:157` before `!in.eligible` at `:158`).
  - Restoring a `SeatedPostureProgram` pulse with `duration_s <= 0` via JSON/wire (`state_codec.cpp:1066-1068`) succeeds at `restore()` and throws `std::invalid_argument` on the next intention tick (`intent.cpp:114-115`).
  - Direct `PhysicalStep::restore` (`step.cpp:3046-3085`) mutates `step_`, `brake_`, `filters_`, and `rider_control_` (`:3050-3073`) before `rider_intent_.restore(s.rider_intent)` (`:3074`), so a throwing `rider_intent_.restore` leaves `PhysicalStep` partially mutated unless wrapped by `SimulationRuntime::restore_state`'s snapshot rollback (`native/src/runtime/runtime.cpp:755-794`).

## 4. Located Test Coverage vs. Unexercised Gaps
- **Actively Asserted in Located Tests:**
  - `native/tests/reference/test_launch_plan.cpp`: disabled `arm_start`/`update` from fresh idle (`:80-103`); `hold -> release -> accelerate -> handover -> blend -> done` & `done -> hold` restart (`:105-248`); `!eligible -> idle` (`:253-271`); `LaunchPlan::restore` phase/counter checks & atomic throw preservation (`:393-471`). Notably, lines `:436-437` and `:449` explicitly assert that `rise_ticks = INT64_MAX` and `handover.launch_ticks = INT64_MAX` are *accepted*!
  - `native/tests/reference/test_launch_intent.cpp`: 4-stage effort composition (`SeatedClimbPolicy` -> `climb_cap` -> `LaunchPlan` blend -> `FrontLiftReaction` factor, `:140-335`); `RiderIntent::restore` `delivered` clock gate and atomic preservation on throw (`:435-555`).
  - `native/tests/reference/test_front_lift_reaction.cpp`: Criterion B step-unload bounded return `acting -> releasing -> idle` (`:205-268`), `hand_drop_ratio = 1.0` config acceptance (`:381`), and `FrontLiftReaction::restore` switch/buffer/phase guards (`:620-835`).
  - `native/tests/reference/test_seated_posture_program.cpp` & `test_lean_limit.cpp`: `lean_limit_rad` replacing `max_forward_lean_rad` in `SeatedPostureProgram::update` (`test_seated_posture_program.cpp:648-666`), `PhysicalStep` `std::min(max_forward_lean_rad, lean_limit_rad)` clamping (`:682-720`), `min_lean_rad` post-slew lower clamp (`:722-768`).
  - `native/tests/reference/test_native_state_fuzz.cpp` (`:1-411`), `test_native_state_restore.cpp` (`:1-800`), and slow ride suites `test_rider_launch_rides_{normal,careful,aggressive}.cpp` + `launch_ride_metrics.hpp:700-999`: exercise wire round-trip/corruptions and 20 s steep launches + 25% stop-and-restarts.
- **Unexercised / Missing Located Tests:**
  - **Missing located test:** `LaunchPlan::restore` (or `RiderIntent::restore` / `from_state_bytes`) with `!config_.plan_enabled` and `phase != LaunchPhase::idle`, followed by `update()`.
  - **Missing located test:** `start_lean_rad` (via `seed_lean` / `finalize_initial_state`) or restored `program.lean_rad` in `(lean_limit_rad, max_forward_lean_rad]` asserting post-step `torso_lean_rad <= min(max_forward_lean_rad, lean_limit_rad)`.
  - **Missing located test:** `LaunchPlan::restore` with `rise_ticks = INT64_MAX` followed by a single `update()` (which throws `std::overflow_error` at `launch_plan.cpp:166`), and `RiderIntent::restore` with `rise_ticks > delivered` or `blend_ticks > delivered`.
  - **Missing located test:** JSON/wire `decode_intent_program` + `RiderIntent::restore` with `pulse.duration_s <= 0` followed by `resolve()`.
  - **Missing located test:** `FrontLiftReaction` with `hand_drop_ratio == 1.0` + oscillating `crank_rpm` under constant `hand_press_n > 0`, or sub-ULP `rate * period_s` restored into `releasing`.

## 5. Recommended Contract / FSM Refinement
Split and tighten `P05` into implementation-accurate, requirement-aligned invariants and fix the three genuine restore/clamp defects:
1. **Sub-clause (a) [Code fix + invariant]:** Keep `!config_.plan_enabled ==> state_.phase == LaunchPhase::idle` across construction, `arm_start()`, `update()`, AND `restore()` — enforce `if (!config_.plan_enabled && s.phase != LaunchPhase::idle) bad_state("phase", "non-idle while plan is disabled");` in `LaunchPlan::restore` (`native/src/rider/launch_plan.cpp:258`).
2. **Sub-clause (b) [Refine sentinel + add missing bounds + stage `FrontLiftReaction::restore`]:**
   - Replace `0 <= rise_ticks` with `-1 <= rise_ticks` (`rise_ticks == -1` in `idle`/`done`; in `hold`, `rise_ticks < phase_ticks`; in `RiderIntent::restore`, `rise_ticks < delivered` when `rise_ticks >= 0`).
   - Bound `handover`/`blend` `launch_ticks <= std::max(release_ticks_, max_launch_ticks_)` in `LaunchPlan::restore` (`launch_plan.cpp:301`) and `blend_ticks <= delivered` in `RiderIntent::restore` (`intent.cpp:681`).
   - Validate `SeatedPostureProgram::restore` (`duration_s > 0`, `[0, 1]` load shares, finite fields) and `SeatedClimbPolicy::restore` (`time_s >= 0`, `effort_nm >= 0`, `[0, surge_budget_j]`), and stage `State candidate = s; s_ = std::move(candidate);` in `FrontLiftReaction::restore` (`front_lift_reaction.cpp:283`).
3. **Sub-clause (c) [Scope to automatic rider commands + post-clamp or seed-bound]:**
   - Qualify effort composition by `config_.enabled && active && control.rider_enabled && !control.human_torque_nm.has_value()`.
   - Qualify `torso_lean_rad` by `!control.posture.has_value()`, enforce `std::min(config_.max_forward_lean_rad, *lean_limit_rad)` inside `SeatedPostureProgram::update` (or explicitly scope the `min` claim to `PhysicalStep` at `step.cpp:1291-1294`), and ensure `start_lean_rad` / `lean_rad_` respects `forward_limit` (or explicitly allow slew-rate decay from a higher initial lean).
4. **Sub-clause (d) [Fix target state name + explicit assumptions]:**
   - Replace "`LaunchPlan` returns to `idle`" with "`LaunchPlan` reaches neutral post-launch `LaunchPhase::done` (`weight == 0`, `brake == 0`, `!owns_shifting`) in at most `release_ticks_ + std::max<int64_t>(0, max_launch_ticks_ - release_ticks_) + cut_clear_ticks + handover_ticks_` intention ticks, assuming `config_.plan_enabled`, `in.eligible`, bounded `!in.shift.cut_active`, and `rise_ticks` below overflow" (or resets to `LaunchPhase::idle` in 1 tick when `!in.eligible`).
   - For `FrontLiftReaction`, state `hand_drop_ratio < 1.0` and `rate * period_s` above IEEE-754 ULP absorption on `[0, 1]` / `[0, lean_max_rad]` so `step_toward` strictly progresses to `ReactionPhase::idle`.
