# Stage 3 (`R1`) Independent Research Report — `P06`: Unilateral Rider Support Floor, Zero Passive Damping, `PedalRecoveryState` Trap Freedom & Symmetric Grip Release

- **Inspected Commit:** `0502660dd14bd1021b3da90a61e20dcb1d5805a1`
- **Compliance Verdict (`R1` Agent):** `partially_violated`
- **Product Justification Verdict (`R1` Agent):** `mixed_subclauses`

## 1. Producers, Mutators, Consumers & Potential Breakers
- **FSM Classification:** `PedalRecoveryState` (`FSM-018`) and `RiderContactsState::enabled[kGripIndex]` (`FSM-107`) are genuine discrete/hybrid state machines; `coast_hold_nm_` (`FSM-017`) is a scalar integrator with mode-switched reset/hold/anti-windup regimes; `AllocationMemory` (`FSM-020`) is a 4-branch warm-start cache over `(pull_left, pull_right)`; `ConstraintDynamics` / `search_constraint_modes` (`FSM-019`) is a stateless per-step BFS active-set solver (`native/src/rider/mode_search.cpp:9-34`); `contactlaw::normal_contact` (`native/src/contact/laws.hpp:28-38`) is stateless arithmetic.
- **Unilateral Support, Press Loop & `coast_hold_nm_` (`FSM-017`, `FSM-019`, `FSM-020`):**
  - *State & Producers:* `SpindleController::coast_hold_nm_` (`native/src/rider/spindle_controller.hpp:489`), initialized `0.` (`native/src/rider/spindle_controller.cpp:1388`), reset in `reset_activation()` (`1491`) and on coasting (`1731`), held untouched during `crank_reposition` (`1675-1680`), integrated/frozen in pedaling (`1681-1710`). Press margin is hardcoded `kPedalPressMarginN = 25.` (`1353,1655,1783-1785`); no `pedal_normal_margin_n` field exists (`pedal_min_normal_n = 20.` in `native/src/configuration/physical_config.hpp:437` is ignored by spindle in `native/src/rider/state_codec.cpp:774` and used only by legacy `support_rows` in `native/src/rider/response_allocation.cpp:81-102` and default `attachment_budget` at `physical_config.hpp:486`). `AllocationMemory` (`FSM-020`, `spindle_controller.hpp:485`) is mutated in `select_allocation_branch` (`response_allocation.cpp:41-61`) and `LegacyController::compute` (`native/src/rider/legacy_controller.cpp:355-390`).
  - *Accounting & Consumers:* `RiderContactWriter::settle_welds` (`native/src/writers/rider_contacts.cpp:995-1015`) produces frame-up `rider_welds`; `attachment_samples` (`1097-1189`) produces foot/pad-normal samples. `PeriodAccounting::interval_violations` (`native/src/runtime/accounting.cpp:113-132`) calls `attachment_violations` (`native/src/runtime/period_buffer.cpp:96-130`) against `config_.attachment_budget` (`native/src/runtime/runtime.cpp:614-633`). `WorkHistory::add` (`native/src/runtime/samples.cpp:476-505`) aggregates `rider_support.*.min_n`. `runtime::rider_support_ok` (`kSupportPullToleranceN = 6.`, `native/src/runtime/status.hpp:74-81`, `native/src/runtime/status.cpp:171-184`) is an external helper.
  - *Breakers:* `PeriodAccounting` uses `weld_foot_min_normal_n = -1050 N` / `weld_saddle_min_normal_n = -2250 N` (`examples/research/viewer_physics_welded.toml:188-193`) instead of `-6 N`; `decode_spindle_state` (`state_codec.cpp:2065`), `equilibrium_cache.cpp:118`, and `SpindleController::validate_state` (`spindle_controller.cpp:2161-2168`) accept any finite `coast_hold_nm` without clamping to `±weld_servo_limit_nm`.
- **Passive Joint Damping:** Produced by MJCF builders (`native/src/model/{builder,physical_topology,articulated_rider,drivetrain,linkage,legacy_topology,environment}.cpp`); never mutated by `RuntimeBootstrap` (`native/src/runtime/bootstrap.cpp:164`) or `equilibrium_cache.cpp:233,265`; read into `qfrc_passive` (`native/src/runtime/step.cpp:550-586`).
- **`PedalRecoveryState` (`FSM-018`):**
  - *Owner & Mutators:* `SpindleController::pedal_recovery_` (`spindle_controller.hpp:41-45,480`), initialized to `"none"` (`spindle_controller.cpp:1475-1476`), reset in `reset_activation()` (`1482-1483`), advanced in `observe_recovery` (`legacy_controller.cpp:99-108`) when `advance && !steady_state && pedal_attachment == "flat"` (`313-325`), consumed by `recovery_goal` (`109-115,326-339`). Non-advancing probes copy/mutate a local `recovery` map (`312-322`).
  - *Breakers:* `decode_pedal_recovery` (`state_codec.cpp:473-486`) allows `direction = 0.0` (any `finite_real`) for `stage = "escape"`/`"release"`; `SpindleController::validate_state` (`spindle_controller.cpp:2161-2170`) performs zero validation on `state.pedal_recovery` (admitting arbitrary `stage` strings, `direction = 0.0`, or `NaN` via C++ `restore()`).
- **`RiderContactWriter` Spring-Grip (`FSM-107`):**
  - *Owner & Mutators:* `RiderContactsState::enabled[kGripIndex]` and `grip_xi_local[2]` (`native/src/writers/rider_contacts.hpp:239-243`), mutated in `reset`/`initialize_settled_state` (`rider_contacts.cpp:240-291`), `release_all` (`293-299`), `set_enabled` (`301-343`), `assign_state` (`348-353`), `equilibrium_cache.cpp:90-106,158`, and `RiderContactWriter::evaluate` (`679-808`). Non-advancing probes copy `RiderContactsState w = state_;` (`501`) and publish only `probe_` (`794-797`).
  - *Breakers:* In-loop mutation `if (!s.reachable) w.enabled[kGripIndex] = false;` (`rider_contacts.cpp:744`) combined with per-side read `s.active = w.enabled[kGripIndex] && s.reachable;` (`701`) breaks left/right symmetry; `decode_rider_contacts_state` (`state_codec.cpp:2511,2539-2545`) and `assign_state` also accept `!enabled[kGripIndex]` with non-zero `grip_xi_local`.

## 2. Sub-Clause Evidence Analysis (Supporting & Opposing)
- **Sub-clause (a1) — Unilateral pedals/saddle & `-6 N` accounting check:**
  - *Axis 1 (Compliance):* **Violated in `PeriodAccounting`; holds only on `flat` pads or post-hoc `rider_support_ok` callers.** `flat` pads enforce `normal >= 0` (`native/src/writers/rider_contacts.cpp:545-548`, `native/src/contact/laws.hpp:28-38`). Linked `spindle`/`weld` pedals and `pin`/`weld` saddle use bidirectional MuJoCo `connect`/`weld` equalities (`native/src/model/articulated_rider.cpp:124-150`). `PeriodAccounting::interval_violations` (`native/src/runtime/accounting.cpp:113-132`) delegates to `attachment_violations` (`native/src/runtime/period_buffer.cpp:96-130`), which checks `config_.attachment_budget` (`native/src/runtime/runtime.cpp:614-633`)—configured in all research profiles to F1 envelopes `weld_foot_min_normal_n = -1050.0 N` and `weld_saddle_min_normal_n = -2250.0 N` (`examples/research/viewer_physics_welded.toml:188-193`), or `+20 N` / `0 N` when `weld_*` is unset (`native/src/configuration/physical_config.hpp:481-495`). `runtime::rider_support_ok` (`kSupportPullToleranceN = 6.`, `native/src/runtime/status.cpp:171-184`) is never invoked by `PeriodAccounting`, `ModelStatus`, or `ReferenceMonitor`.
  - *Axis 2 (Justification):* **Confirmed physical requirement with documented deferred runtime-budget wiring.** `AGENTS.md:191,197` and `docs/agents/simulation-decisions.md` «Действует» `#9, #34, #42` (`52-65,336-374,416-430`) confirm the unilateral physics and `-6 N` ride-acceptance rule, while «Решено — не сделано» `#5, #8, #14` (`562-574,582-586`) explicitly record that F1 `attachment_budget` (`-1050/-2250 N`) was left unchanged for now.
- **Sub-clause (a2) — Spindle press margin & `coast_hold_nm_` (`FSM-017`) anti-windup:**
  - *Axis 1 (Compliance):* **Holds during normal stepping (modulo misnamed constant and restore clamp gap).** `SpindleController::compute` enforces `kPedalPressMarginN = 25.` (`native/src/rider/spindle_controller.cpp:1353,1655,1783-1785`; there is no `pedal_normal_margin_n` identifier) as a priority-power press target when `!steady_state && mean_crank_torque_nm > 0`, and freezes `coast_hold_nm_` whenever `raw_task + std::max(0., hold_nm) >= crank_press_ceiling_nm` while clamping `applied_hold` to `[-task, std::min(task, crank_press_ceiling_nm - task)]` (`1688-1710`). However, `decode_spindle_state` (`native/src/rider/state_codec.cpp:2065`), `equilibrium_cache.cpp:118`, and `validate_state` (`spindle_controller.cpp:2161-2168`) do not enforce `|coast_hold_nm| <= weld_servo_limit_nm`, so an out-of-range restored `coast_hold_nm_` persists un-clamped while `at_press_limit` or `!advance`.
  - *Axis 2 (Justification):* **Confirmed requirement** (`simulation-decisions.md` «Действует» `#9`, `52-104`); `pedal_normal_margin_n` is an incidental misnaming in the candidate statement.
- **Sub-clause (a3) — MuJoCo passive joint `damping == 0` everywhere:**
  - *Axis 1 (Compliance):* **Holds on `mjModel::dof_damping`.** No MJCF builder (`native/src/model/{builder,physical_topology,articulated_rider,drivetrain,linkage,legacy_topology,environment}.cpp`) emits joint `damping`, so `model->dof_damping[i] == 0.0` for all DOFs in all modes/variants. (Constraint-level `solreflimit` damping on joint limits remains active.)
  - *Axis 2 (Justification):* **Confirmed requirement** (`AGENTS.md:199,215-218`, `simulation-decisions.md` «Действует» `#26`, `246-253`).
- **Sub-clause (b) — `PedalRecoveryState` (`FSM-018`) closed typed states & `escape` trap freedom:**
  - *Axis 1 (Compliance):* **Violated.** (1) `PedalRecoveryState` (`native/src/rider/spindle_controller.hpp:41-45`) uses open `std::string stage = "none"`, not a closed enum. (2) Actual stages are `("none", "release", "escape", "raise", "return")` (`native/src/rider/legacy_controller.cpp:99-108`, `state_codec.cpp:473-486`), not `("none", "release", "lift", "escape", "reengage")`. (3) `decode_pedal_recovery` (`state_codec.cpp:481-484`) accepts any `finite_real` `direction` (including `0.0`), and `SpindleController::validate_state` (`spindle_controller.cpp:2161-2170`) ignores `pedal_recovery` entirely (admitting `direction = 0.0`, `NaN`, or `stage = "bogus"` via C++ `restore()`). In `stage == "escape"`, `0.0 * (sole[0] - origin[0]) > ext[0] + pad_half + radius` (`> 0`) is identically `false` (`legacy_controller.cpp:105`), permanently trapping the foot under the pedal (`113-114`).
  - *Axis 2 (Justification):* **Relevant engineering state-integrity contract** on a retained legacy (`flat` pedal) path (`simulation-decisions.md:48,117-123` select `spindle` for production rides), with misnamed stages (`lift`/`reengage`) in the candidate statement.
- **Sub-clause (c) — `RiderContactWriter` spring-grip (`FSM-107`) left/right release symmetry:**
  - *Axis 1 (Compliance):* **Violated.** In `RiderContactWriter::evaluate` (`native/src/writers/rider_contacts.cpp:685-751`), `s.active = w.enabled[kGripIndex] && s.reachable` (`701`) and `if (!s.reachable) w.enabled[kGripIndex] = false` (`744`) run sequentially inside `for (side = 0; side < 2; ++side)`. Left (`side = 0`) unreachable disables `w.enabled[kGripIndex]` before `side = 1` runs, releasing **both** hands in the same step; right (`side = 1`) unreachable runs **after** `side = 0` has already applied `qfrc` (`736-740`), stored non-zero `w.grip_xi_local[0]` (`745`), and marked `diagnostics.grip.enabled = true` (`774`), leaving `enabled[kGripIndex] == false` with non-zero `grip_xi_local[0]` and `elastic_energy_j > 0`. Single-hand overload (`719-750`) is symmetric between hands (latching `w.enabled[kGripIndex] = false` at `750` after the loop) but still applies the non-overloaded hand's `qfrc` and keeps its `grip_xi_local` non-zero on the release step.
  - *Axis 2 (Justification):* **Engineering symmetry contract conflicting with frozen legacy-oracle golden parity** (`rider_contacts.hpp:19-23`, `test_native_rider_contacts.cpp:532-579`) on a non-primary (`spring` grip) topology (`simulation-decisions.md:48-49` selects `connect`).

## 3. Exceptions, Cross-Module Dependencies & Failure Consequences
- **Topology-Split Unilateral Semantics (`flat` vs Linked Equalities):** Only `flat` box-pad contacts (`contactlaw::normal_contact`, `native/src/contact/laws.hpp:28-38`) are physically unilateral in forward dynamics. Production profiles (`examples/research/viewer_physics_*.toml`) use `spindle` pedals + `pin` saddle (`connect_foot_*`, `connect_saddle` in `native/src/model/articulated_rider.cpp:124-150`), which are bidirectional MuJoCo soft spring-damper equalities (`solref = max(2*dt, closure_time_constant_s) 1`) that physically pull in tension whenever controller demands or terrain impacts exceed rider vertical equilibrium (e.g., `-107...-396 N` on `rough_uphill_savage`, `native/tests/reference/test_realistic_pedelec_acceptance.cpp:533-537,599-605`; `-255 N` settle transient, `native/tests/reference/test_native_rider_allocation.cpp:986-990`).
- **Dual Projection & Silent Strict-Monitor Gap on `-6 N`:** `attachment_samples` projects foot normals onto foot `+z` (`spindle`) or box-pad normal (`native/src/writers/rider_contacts.cpp:1137,1159,1170`) and checks `attachment_budget` (`-1050 N` / `-2250 N` in `viewer_physics_welded.toml:188-193`), whereas `rider_welds` / `WorkHistory::rider_support` projects onto bicycle `frame_up` (`rider_contacts.cpp:952,1000-1010`) and is populated **only** when `linked_pedals_` / `linked_saddle_` is true (never for `flat`). Because `runtime::rider_support_ok` (`native/src/runtime/status.cpp:171-184`) is never called by `PeriodAccounting`, `ModelStatus::observe`, or `ReferenceMonitor::observe`, a `--check-policy strict` ride (`bike-ride` / `NativeRideRuntime`) will **not** fail or stop on pulls between `-6 N` and `-1050/-2250 N` unless an external caller inspects `accounting_state()["history"]["rider_support"]`.
- **Controller-Soft Press Floor & Legacy Fallback:** `kPedalPressMarginN = 25.` (`spindle_controller.cpp:1353`) is skipped when `steady_state == true` (`1773-1774`), reduced to `>= 0 N` (`f_weight[s]`) during `crank_reposition` (`1758-1782`), and subject to joint-strength/speed/positive-power clipping in `limit_torques` (`1091-1147`). In `LegacyController` (`weld`), if all effort scales `{1.0, 0.5, 0.25}` fail QP feasibility (`legacy_controller.cpp:367-373`), `invalid_controller = true` is emitted (`accounting.cpp:370` -> `"rider_controller.infeasible"`) while fallback torque is still applied.
- **Non-`dof_damping` Dissipation Exceptions:** While `model->dof_damping == 0` holds everywhere, rider joint limits (`limited="true"`, `articulated_rider.cpp:50-51`) carry MuJoCo's default `solreflimit = (0.02, 1)` constraint damper, dissipating ~97% of end-stop work (`rider_end_stop_loss_step_j` in `accounting.cpp:174-199`; documented exception in `docs/agents/simulation-decisions.md:250-253,286-292`). Also `fork_travel`/`shock_stroke` use `solreflimit = "0.01 1"` (`linkage.cpp:50,87`), rolling brakes mutate `dof_frictionloss` (`step.cpp:1323`), equilibrium settle injects external crank PD damping `kd = 88` (`settle_contacts.cpp:30-31,53-59`), and experimental `gpu/rider_kernel.hpp:29-31,195-198` injects soft-edge/posture `kd` into `qfrc`/`ctrl`.
- **Consequences of Sub-Clause (b) & (c) Violations:**
  - **(b)** Restoring `PedalRecoveryState{stage="escape", direction=0.0}` (or `stage="release", direction=0.0`) via JSON wire or C++ `restore()` permanently disables leg stance/pedaling force on that foot (`recovered[s] = false`, `blends[s] = 0`, `legacy_controller.cpp:321-327`) and parks the foot goal directly beneath the pedal center (`113-114`) until `command.enabled == false` triggers `reset_activation()`.
  - **(c)** Right-hand-only unreachability (`side = 1`) leaves `enabled[kGripIndex] == false` alongside non-zero `grip_xi_local[0]`, `elastic_energy_j > 0` (`rider_contacts.cpp:744-746,801-816`), active left-hand `qfrc` in the release step, `diagnostics.grip.enabled == true`, and conflated `diagnostics.grip.release_loss_j = per_side[0].grip_loss + per_side[1].grip_loss` (`781-782`), delaying left-hand energy dump to the next step; left-hand-only unreachability (`side = 0`) immediately releases both hands in the same step.

## 4. Located Test Coverage vs. Unexercised Gaps
- **Actively Exercised & Asserted:**
  - *Zero passive joint damping (`dof_damping == 0`):* Exhaustively asserted across `viewer_physics_{welded,distributed,pneumatic}.toml`, `weld/spring`, motor clutch, geometric mid-drive, and `standard`/`ride` `none`/`lumped` builds in `native/tests/model/test_mjcf.cpp:125-129,161,182,188,193,206-219`, plus `joint_passive_damping_nms_rad` rejection in `native/tests/reference/test_native_spindle_controller.cpp:1340-1348`.
  - *Spindle press floor, `coast_hold_nm_` anti-windup & `-6 N` ride checks:* `native/tests/reference/test_seated_pedaling_cycle.cpp:1271-1288,1393-1570` actively asserts `min_{front,rear}_press >= -6 N`, `min_saddle_support_n >= 0 N`, `steps_at_press_limit > 0`, and `windup_steps_at_limit == 0` under 60 N·m demand; `test_native_spindle_controller.cpp:600-660,670-726,917-964` tests coasting zero-reset, reposition hold preservation, and resting leg-weight press; `test_realistic_pedelec_acceptance.cpp:255-264,319,454,481,528` and `launch_ride_metrics.hpp:699-715,761-770` assert `-6 N` via `rider_support_ok` / `LaunchMetrics` on smooth tracks (`rough_uphill_savage` is parked at `test_realistic_pedelec_acceptance.cpp:599-605` due to `-107...-396 N` pulls; `climb_15pct_standstill` at `499-512` omits `require_support_within_pull_tolerance`).
  - *Legacy allocator (`FSM-019`, `FSM-020`) & `attachment_budget`:* Exercised in `native/tests/rider/test_constraints.cpp:27-77`, `native/tests/reference/test_native_rider_allocation.cpp:154-292,570-617,668-734,1302-1416`, and `native/tests/reference/test_attachment_budget.cpp:45-224`.
- **Unexercised Paths & Coverage Gaps (Missing Located Tests):**
  - *`PeriodAccounting` `-6 N` pull enforcement:* No test asserts that `PeriodAccounting` / `ReferenceMonitor` flags `-7 N` pulls on `viewer_physics_welded.toml` (it cannot, because `weld_foot_min_normal_n = -1050 N` / `weld_saddle_min_normal_n = -2250 N`); `test_native_runtime_accounting.cpp:486-573` mutates `weld_foot_min_normal_n` to `+100 N` to trigger strict failure.
  - *`coast_hold_nm` restore bounds:* No test checks out-of-bounds `coast_hold_nm` (e.g., `1e6`) through `decode_spindle_state`, `equilibrium_cache`, or `SpindleController::restore`.
  - *`PedalRecoveryState` (`FSM-018`) transitions & `direction == 0.0` trap:* Only default `"none"` state roundtrip (`test_constraints.cpp:56-64`) and diagnostic string `"escape"` roundtrip (`native/tests/rider/test_contacts.cpp:111-114`) are tested. **Zero located tests** exercise `observe_recovery` / `recovery_goal` transitions (`none -> release -> escape -> raise -> return -> none`) on `pedal_attachment = "flat"`, and `test_native_state_fuzz.cpp:270-402` fuzzes only `DriveWriter` and `Stepper::set_state`—never `decode_spindle_state` or `SpindleController::restore` with `direction = 0.0` or invalid `stage`.
  - *Spring-grip (`FSM-107`) left/right symmetry:* `native/tests/reference/test_native_rider_contacts.cpp:532-554` (`test_unreachable_hand_releases_and_reports_loss`) tests **only** `rider_forearm_right` (`side = 1`) unreachability and **pins the asymmetric golden state** (where `enabled.grip == false` while `grip_xi_local.left != 0`), with no left-unreachable comparison test; `test_pair_force_limit_releases_both_hands` (`557-579`) perturbs only `rider_forearm_left` (`side = 0`) and pins the single-step asymmetric `qfrc` golden.

## 5. Recommended Contract / FSM Refinement
Split `P06` into three orthogonal, accurately named properties aligned with product scope:
1. **`P06a` (Confirmed Requirement — Rider Unilateral Support, Spindle Press Anti-Windup & Zero Joint Damping):**
   - Keep `model->dof_damping[i] == 0.0` for all DOFs across all MJCF modes/variants, explicitly noting the documented MuJoCo `solreflimit` end-stop impedance exception (`simulation-decisions.md` «Действует» `#26, #32`) and setup-only `crank_hold_torque` (`#33`).
   - Separate physical enforcement (`flat` pad `normal_contact >= 0`) from closed-form `spindle` controller targets (`kPedalPressMarginN = 25.0 N` when `!steady_state && mean_crank_torque_nm > 0`, `>= 0 N` during coast/reposition, and `coast_hold_nm_` anti-windup freeze when `raw_task + max(0, coast_hold_nm_) >= crank_press_ceiling_nm`, plus clamping `|coast_hold_nm| <= weld_servo_limit_nm` in `decode_spindle_state` / `validate_state`).
   - State the `-6 N` rule accurately: either (i) wire `runtime::rider_support_ok` (`min_n >= -6.0 N`) into `PeriodAccounting` / `ModelStatus` once «Решено — не сделано» `#5/#8/#14` are resolved, or (ii) specify that `PeriodAccounting` enforces `ArticulatedConfig::attachment_budget()` while ride-level acceptance gates enforce `rider_support_ok(accounting_state()["history"]["rider_support"])`.
2. **`P06b` (Engineering State-Integrity Contract — `PedalRecoveryState` Closed Enum & Non-Zero Escape Direction):**
   - Replace `std::string stage` in `PedalRecoveryState` with `enum class PedalRecoveryStage : std::uint8_t { None, Release, Escape, Raise, Return }` (fixing the misnamed `lift`/`reengage` labels to `raise`/`return`), and represent `direction` as a closed sign enum (`{-1, +1}`) whenever `stage != None` (or enforce `(stage == None) || (direction == -1.0 || direction == 1.0)` in both `decode_pedal_recovery` and `SpindleController::validate_state`).
3. **`P06c` (Product Decision / Engineering Symmetry Contract — Cohesive Two-Hand Spring-Grip Release):**
   - First resolve whether legacy Python golden parity (`test_native_rider_contacts.cpp:532-579`) on the non-primary `grip_attachment = "spring"` mode may be updated. If cohesive pair release is desired, compute reachability and trial spring forces for **both** hands (`side = 0, 1`) before deciding pair release (`!per_side[0].reachable || !per_side[1].reachable || per_side[0].overloaded || per_side[1].overloaded`), and on release zero `qfrc`, `grip_xi_local`, and `elastic_energy_j` for **both** hands in the same step while dumping `old[0] + old[1]` elastic energy to `release_loss_j` (and enforce `!enabled[kGripIndex] ==> grip_xi_local == {0, 0}` in `decode_rider_contacts_state` / `assign_state`).


---

# Stage 3 (`R1`) Independent Research Report — `P07`: Observational Purity & Rollback-Brake Parity of Non-Advancing Probes (`advance == false` / `probe_step_inputs`)

- **Inspected Commit:** `0502660dd14bd1021b3da90a61e20dcb1d5805a1`
- **Compliance Verdict (`R1` Agent):** `violated`
- **Product Justification Verdict (`R1` Agent):** `mixed_subclauses`

## 1. Producers, Mutators, Consumers & Potential Breakers
- **Probe Entry Points & Callers**:
  - `NativeRideRuntime::probe_step_inputs` (`native/src/runtime/runtime.cpp:816-828`) validates open/healthy state and demands, then delegates to `PhysicalStep::probe_step_inputs` (`native/src/runtime/step.cpp:2953-3012`), which calls `apply_forces(true, false, front_demand, rear_demand, std::nullopt, control, std::nullopt)` (`step.cpp:2962-2963`, `resolved = nullptr`) and reads `d->ctrl`, `m->dof_frictionloss`, `accumulator_`, and writer probe views (`step.cpp:2964-3011`). No CLI/viewer/replay loop calls `probe_step_inputs` (`native/src/cli/ride.cpp:120-152`, `native/src/viewer/ride.cpp:170-189`, `native/src/replay/session.cpp:213-253`); only tests call it.
  - `prepare_research_startup` (`native/src/initialization/research_startup.cpp:50-66`) calls `physical.apply_forces(true, false, 0., 0., std::nullopt, safe)` at `t=0` before `SetupOwner::seal_bootstrap()` (`native/src/initialization/setup_owner.cpp:274-276, 305`).
  - Equilibrium/settle helpers call `apply_initial_forces(context, false)` -> `physical.apply_forces(false, false, ...)` (`native/src/initialization/settle_contacts.cpp:34-63`; `finalize.cpp:85`, `initial_state.cpp:196`, `equilibrium.cpp:102, 123`, `refine.cpp:88, 114`).
- **State Owners, Mutators, Restore/Codec Boundaries & Consumers**:
  - `SpindleController::coast_hold_nm_` (FSM-019, `native/src/rider/spindle_controller.hpp:478`): initialized `0.` (`:478`), serialized via `state()`/`restore()` (`native/src/rider/spindle_controller.cpp:2080-2081, 2106`) and `encode_spindle_state`/`decode_spindle_state` (`native/src/rider/state_codec.cpp:2065, 2113-2115`), reset in `reset_activation()` (`spindle_controller.cpp:2074`). Mutated in `populate_leg_torques(..., commit_hold)` (`spindle_controller.cpp:1681-1735`), which `SpindleController::compute` calls with `commit_hold = true` at `:1953` WITHOUT checking `advance`. Consumed on subsequent pedaling `compute()` calls (`:1605, 1681, 1707-1710`).
  - `SpindleController::command_enabled_` (FSM-017, `spindle_controller.hpp:474`): initialized `true`, serialized (`spindle_controller.cpp:2079, 2105`; `state_codec.cpp:2064, 2109-2110`). Unconditionally overwritten in `SpindleController::compute` (`spindle_controller.cpp:1294`) and `LegacyController::compute` (`native/src/rider/legacy_controller.cpp:296`) even when `advance == false`. Consumed in `SpindleController::write` (`spindle_controller.cpp:2047`) on non-tick advancing steps (`step.cpp:1533-1536`).
  - `SpindleController::allocation_memory_` (FSM-025/026, `spindle_controller.hpp:489`; `native/src/rider/response_allocation.hpp:29-32`): serialized as `last_branch`/`last_solution` (`state_codec.cpp:929-937, 2128-2132`), cleared in `reset_activation()` (`spindle_controller.cpp:2069`). Mutated unconditionally by `LegacyController::compute` -> `allocate_response(..., c_.allocation_memory_)` (`legacy_controller.cpp:370`; `native/src/rider/response_allocation.cpp:59, 159, 239`), unlike `pedal_recovery_` which IS restored on `!advance` (`legacy_controller.cpp:307, 380`). Consumed in `select_allocation_branch` (`response_allocation.cpp:44-57`) and SLSQP warm-start (`:47, 195`).
  - `mjModel::dof_frictionloss` & `Stepper::posvel_fresh_` (FSM-104, `native/src/runtime/static_brake.cpp:44-70`, `native/src/stepper.hpp:396-405, 526`): mutated unconditionally by `brake_.apply(m, brakes.final_front, brakes.final_rear)` + `stepper_->note_posvel_stale()` (`step.cpp:1323-1324`). Consumed by `ensure_posvel_fresh` (`step.cpp:1160-1168, 1384`), `probe_step_inputs` (`step.cpp:2967-2971`), `capture_initialized` (`native/src/initialization/capture.cpp:177-182`), and `capture_presentation` (`native/src/runtime/presentation.cpp:43, 185-187`).
  - Additional probe-mutated live state: `SpindleController` diagnostics/`last_terms_`/`joint_torques_nm_` (`spindle_controller.cpp:1295-1318, 1954-2020`; `legacy_controller.cpp:299-306, 373-388`), consumed by `capture_accounted_presentation` (`runtime.cpp:710`, `presentation.cpp:190-197`) and non-tick `publish_raw`/`capture_presentation` (`step.cpp:2166-2253`, `presentation.cpp:157-163`); `PhysicalStep::wheel_x_m_` & `rider_state_` (`step.cpp:1250-1258`, read at `:1266-1268`), `snapshots_`/`accumulator_`/`acc_inputs_` (`step.cpp:1318-1320, 1541-1542, 2297-2339`), `mjData` (`d->ctrl`, `d->qfrc_applied`, `d->xfrc_applied`, `d->efc_*`); and `CruiseWriter` (`step.cpp:1553-1582`, `native/src/writers/cruise.cpp:48-84`). By contrast, `DrivetrainWriter` (`native/src/writers/drivetrain.cpp:945-989`), `RiderContactWriter` (`native/src/writers/rider_contacts.cpp:409-422`), `TireWriter` (`native/src/writers/tire.cpp:918-930`), and `RiderIntent` (`step.cpp:1265-1288`) isolate their committed state on `!advance`.
  - `PhysicalStep::rollback_hold_` (FSM-106/107, `native/src/runtime/step.hpp:194, 635`): serialized in `StepState` (`step.cpp:2559, 2610`), mutated by `rollback_brake_demand` in `advance_body` (`step.cpp:1694-1696, 2367-2383`), bypassed with `.rollback_demand = 0.` in `apply_forces_body` when `resolved == nullptr` (`step.cpp:1308-1316`).

## 2. Sub-Clause Evidence Analysis (Supporting & Opposing)
- **Sub-clause (a) — Observational Purity of Non-Advancing Probes**:
  - **Axis 1 (Factual Compliance): VIOLATED** on all four named fields plus additional live step/controller fields whenever `probe_step_inputs` or `apply_forces(..., advance=false)` runs:
    1. `SpindleController::coast_hold_nm_` (`spindle_controller.cpp:1605, 1681-1735, 1953`): `control_tick` is forced `true` by `!advance` (`step.cpp:1248`), so every probe calls `rider_control_->compute(..., advance=false)`. Line `1953` calls `populate_leg_torques(ps, true)` (`commit_hold = true`). While pedaling (`mean_crank_torque_nm > 0.`) gates integration on `advance` (`:1691`), any coasting/braking probe (`mean_crank_torque_nm <= 0. && !crank_reposition`, `:1725-1735` — e.g. `human_torque_nm = 0.` or `front_demand > 0.` triggering `legs_coast`) sets `hold_nm = 0.` and overwrites `coast_hold_nm_ = 0.`, altering subsequent pedaling `advance()` joint torques!
    2. `SpindleController::command_enabled_` (`spindle_controller.cpp:1294, 2047`; `legacy_controller.cpp:296`): overwritten unconditionally on every probe. When `control_clock_.steps_per_period > 1` (e.g. `.005 / .0005 = 10`), probing at a non-tick `step_` (`is_tick(step_) == false`) with flipped `control.rider_enabled` persists into the next `advance()`, which skips `compute()` (`step.cpp:1497, 1533-1536`) and calls `write(d, torques)`, gating all rider actuators by the probe's `command_enabled_` (`spindle_controller.cpp:2047`)!
    3. `SpindleController::allocation_memory_` (`legacy_controller.cpp:370`; `response_allocation.cpp:44-60, 159, 239`): on `"flat"`/`"weld"` pedal topologies (`legacy_ != nullptr`), `allocate_response` unconditionally commits `memory.branch` and `memory.solution` (`:239`), altering branch-try order (`:44-57`) and SLSQP warm-starts (`:47, 195`) on subsequent `advance()`.
    4. `mjModel::dof_frictionloss` (`step.cpp:1323-1324`; `static_brake.cpp:67-68`): mutated in-place on `stepper_->model()` and never restored. Even though `NativeRideRuntime::snapshot()`/`render_frame()` read cached `committed_presentation_` (`runtime.cpp:716, 860-884`), mutating `dof_frictionloss` marks `stepper_->note_posvel_stale()`, forcing a full `engine::forward` instead of `forward_skip` on the next `advance()` if brake bounds differ (`step.cpp:1163, 1384`), and mutates live `PresentationState` on raw `PhysicalStep::capture_presentation` (`presentation.cpp:43, 185-187`).
    5. Additional leaky state: `SpindleController` / `LegacyController` overwrite `last_terms_`, `saturated_ik_`, `support_diagnostics_`, `allocation_diagnostics_`, `effort_diagnostics_`, `joint_torques_nm_`, `joint_capacity_nm_` (`spindle_controller.cpp:1295-1318, 1954-2020`), corrupting non-tick `advance()` telemetry (`step.cpp:2166-2253`) and `advance_interval`'s pre-step `capture_accounted_presentation` (`runtime.cpp:710`); `PhysicalStep` overwrites `wheel_x_m_` and `rider_state_` (`step.cpp:1250-1258`, read on non-tick steps at `:1266-1268`), `snapshots_`, `accumulator_`, `acc_inputs_`, `mjData` force/ctrl/efc buffers (`step.cpp:1318-1320, 1511, 1538-1542, 2297-2339`); `CruiseWriter` zeroes `torque_nm` and integrates `integral_mps_s` without checking `advance` (`step.cpp:1553-1582`, `cruise.cpp:48-84`).
  - **Axis 2 (Product Justification): MIXED**. Non-mutation of controller/writer/step state is an explicit engineering contract (`runtime.hpp:137-138`: *"no committed state moves"*; `step.hpp:313-314`; `docs/agents/native-engineering.md:38-41`; `docs/TESTING.md:516`) protecting confirmed physics («Действует» #9 weld-servo hold, `docs/agents/simulation-decisions.md:130-169`). However, requiring low-level `PhysicalStep::apply_forces(..., advance=false)` itself not to touch `mjModel::dof_frictionloss` contradicts `prepare_research_startup` (`research_startup.cpp:59-66`), which relies on `apply_forces(true, false, 0., 0., ...)` clearing settle holding brakes in `m->dof_frictionloss` before `seal_bootstrap()`, and `probe_step_inputs` (`step.cpp:2967-2971`), which reads `m->dof_frictionloss` after `apply_forces` returns.
- **Sub-clause (b) — Hill-Hold `rollback_demand` Parity**:
  - **Axis 1 (Factual Compliance): VIOLATED**. `advance_body` (`step.cpp:1694-1704`) computes `hold = rollback_brake_demand(qvel[root_x_dofadr_], &control)` (`step.cpp:2367-2383`) and passes `&brakes` to `apply_forces`. In contrast, `probe_step_inputs` (`step.cpp:2962-2963`) leaves `resolved = nullptr`, and `apply_forces_body` (`step.cpp:1308-1316`) hardcodes `.rollback_demand = 0.`. Whenever `cfg.rollback_brake` is enabled (`rider_enabled == true`, effort drive mode) and either `speed_mps < -cfg.rollback_engage_mps` (`-0.25` m/s) or `rollback_hold_ == true && speed_mps <= -cfg.rollback_release_mps`, `probe_step_inputs` omits hill-hold braking from `front_brake_bound_nm`, `rear_brake_bound_nm`, `brake_hold` forces, and `brakes.riding` (`motor_braking` / `legs_coast`).
  - **Axis 2 (Product Justification): RELEVANT ENGINEERING CONTRACT (for `active == true` probes only)**. Hill-hold is a confirmed requirement («Действует» #19, `simulation-decisions.md:208-209`), and `probe_step_inputs` promises parity with an advancing interval's pre-solve assembly (`step.cpp:2947-2950`). Only `apply_forces(active=false, advance=false)` during equilibrium settle (`settle_contacts.cpp:41-52`, `step.cpp:1301-1305`) legitimately omits rollback.

## 3. Exceptions, Cross-Module Dependencies & Failure Consequences
- **Control-Clock Period Dependency (`steps_per_period > 1`)**: `apply_forces_body` sets `control_tick = !advance || initializing_ || control_clock_.is_tick(step_)` (`native/src/runtime/step.cpp:1248-1249`). When `control_period_s == timestep_s` (`steps_per_period == 1`), the next `advance()` runs `compute()` and overwrites `command_enabled_`, `rider_state_`, and controller diagnostics, masking those leaks (though `coast_hold_nm_` and `allocation_memory_` leaks STILL persist across ticks!). In production profiles (`timestep_s = 0.0005`, `control_period_s = 0.005`, `steps_per_period = 10`; `docs/agents/simulation-decisions.md:639-640`), probing on any of the 9 non-tick steps per period directly corrupts the held actuator gate (`command_enabled_`), held road lookahead (`rider_state_`), and published `RawStep` / `PresentationState` controller diagnostics on subsequent non-tick `advance()` calls.
- **`NativeRideRuntime` Snapshot Shielding vs. Live `PhysicalStep` Exposure**: `NativeRideRuntime::snapshot()` and `render_frame()` read cached `committed_integration_state_` and `committed_presentation_` (`native/src/runtime/runtime.cpp:714-718, 860-884`), shielding them from immediate post-probe reads of `mjModel::dof_frictionloss` and `mjData`. However: (1) raw `capture_presentation` on `PhysicalStep` (`native/src/runtime/presentation.cpp:43, 185-187`) reads mutated `m->dof_frictionloss` and `last_terms_` immediately; (2) `NativeRideRuntime::advance_interval` (`runtime.cpp:710`) calls `capture_accounted_presentation(committed_presentation_)` BEFORE `advance_physics`, leaking probe-mutated `last_terms_.saturated` into `committed_presentation_.joints_saturated` if `advance_interval` aborts before commit; and (3) a probe that changes brake bounds flips `StaticBrake` / `posvel_fresh_` (`step.cpp:1323-1324`), altering whether the next `advance()` executes a full `engine::forward(m, d)` (`step.cpp:1163, 1384`).
- **Bootstrap & Equilibrium Callers Rely on `apply_forces(advance=false)` Side Effects**: `prepare_research_startup` (`native/src/initialization/research_startup.cpp:63-66`) intentionally uses `apply_forces(true, false, 0., 0., std::nullopt, safe)` before `stepper.forward()` and `seal_bootstrap()` (`native/src/initialization/setup_owner.cpp:274-276, 305`) to clear settle holding brakes in `m->dof_frictionloss`, `d->ctrl`, and `d->qfrc_applied` prior to `capture_initialized` (`native/src/initialization/capture.cpp:177-182`). Incidentally, because `safe.human_torque_nm = 0.`, that call also zeroes any `coast_hold_nm_` transferred into bootstrap. Meanwhile `apply_initial_forces` (`native/src/initialization/settle_contacts.cpp:41-52`) calls `apply_forces(false, false, ...)` during equilibrium search where runtime `rollback_brake` must stay inactive.
- **Reachability in Production**: `NativeRideRuntime::probe_step_inputs` has zero callers in `native/src/{cli,viewer,replay,recording,research}/` (verified in `native/src/cli/ride.cpp:63-214`, `native/src/viewer/ride.cpp:24-189`, `native/src/replay/session.cpp:94-289`); it is a public C++/test diagnostic seam (`native/src/runtime/runtime.hpp:137-140`). Direct `PhysicalStep::apply_forces(..., false, ...)` runs in initialization/research startup and unit tests.

## 4. Located Test Coverage vs. Unexercised Gaps
- **Located Tests Exercising `probe_step_inputs` / `advance=false`**:
  - `native/tests/reference/test_native_runtime_step.cpp:200-238` (`test_probe_step_inputs_matches_interval_staging_without_advancing`): calls `rt->probe_step_inputs(command, 0.35, 0.15)` at `t=0` (stationary, `speed_mps == 0`, `coast_hold_nm_ == 0`, `rider_enabled == true`), checks `snapshot().integration_state` and `step` before/after, and compares one subsequent `advance(1, command, 0.35, 0.15)` against a clean runtime. Because both probe and `advance(1)` use `front=0.35 > 0` (`legs_coast = true`, `coast_hold_nm_ == 0`), `rider_enabled == true`, spindle topology (`legacy_ == nullptr`), and `speed_mps == 0` (`rollback_hold_ == false`), every single violation in `P07` is masked!
  - `native/tests/reference/test_rider_brake_channel.cpp:458-518` (`test_probe_step_inputs_exposes_rider_brake_demands_without_advancing_intent`): verifies `RiderIntent` non-mutation across `probe_step_inputs` at `step=0`, without prior pedaling (`coast_hold_nm_ == 0`), non-tick `command_enabled_` flip, legacy allocator, or backward speed.
  - `native/tests/reference/test_native_input_contracts.cpp:298-355`: asserts invalid demands/control throw on `probe_step_inputs` without moving `snapshot().integration_state`.
  - `native/tests/reference/test_native_drivetrain.cpp:56-70, 221-237`: verifies `DrivetrainWriter` probe isolation (`policies` & ratchets preserved when `advance=false`).
  - `native/tests/reference/test_native_rider_allocation.cpp:1245-1300` (`test_command_does_not_read_solved_reactions`): calls `rider_control().compute(data, command, false)` twice with identical `command`, never checking `allocation_memory_` preservation across differing probe vs. step commands.
- **Unexercised Paths / Coverage Gaps (Missing Located Tests)**:
  1. Pedaling for N steps to build nonzero `coast_hold_nm_`, calling `probe_step_inputs` with `human_torque_nm = 0.` or `front_demand > 0.`, then advancing with pedaling and comparing against an unprobed twin.
  2. Calling `probe_step_inputs` with `rider_enabled = false` on a non-tick step (`step % steps_per_period != 0` when `steps_per_period > 1`) followed by `advance()` with `rider_enabled = true`.
  3. Calling `probe_step_inputs` on a `"flat"`/`"weld"` `LegacyController` rig with extreme wrench demands and checking `state().allocation_memory` (`last_branch`/`last_solution`) and subsequent `advance()` parity.
  4. Checking `mjModel::dof_frictionloss` / `Stepper::posvel_fresh()` restoration after `probe_step_inputs(cmd, 0.8, 0.8)` followed by `advance(step+1, cmd, 0.0, 0.0)`.
  5. Calling `probe_step_inputs` while rolling backward (`qvel[root_x] < -0.25` m/s or with `rollback_hold_ == true`) with `front_demand = 0, rear_demand = 0` and comparing `front_brake_bound_nm` / `rear_brake_bound_nm` against `advance()`.

## 5. Recommended Contract / FSM Refinement
Split `P07` into two contract-aligned properties separating high-level probe non-interference from low-level `apply_forces` staging:
1. **`P07a` (Observational Purity of `probe_step_inputs` & Non-Advancing Controller/Writer Evaluation)**:
   - Gate all persistent controller and step mutations on `advance == true`: in `SpindleController::compute`, pass `commit_hold = advance` at `native/src/rider/spindle_controller.cpp:1953` (preserving `coast_hold_nm_`) and stage/restore `command_enabled_`, `last_terms_`, `pd_split_`, `saturated_ik_`, `ik_reach_limited_`, `joint_torques_nm_`, `joint_capacity_nm_`, and diagnostics when `!advance`; in `LegacyController::compute` (`native/src/rider/legacy_controller.cpp:296, 370`), pass a scratch copy of `c_.allocation_memory_` when `!advance`; in `PhysicalStep::apply_forces_body` (`native/src/runtime/step.cpp:1248-1258, 1553-1582`), do not overwrite committed `wheel_x_m_`, `rider_state_`, or `CruiseWriter` state when `!advance`.
   - Keep `PhysicalStep::apply_forces(..., advance=false)` writing `mjModel::dof_frictionloss`, `mjData::ctrl`, and `mjData::qfrc_applied` because `prepare_research_startup` (`native/src/initialization/research_startup.cpp:65`) and `PhysicalStep::probe_step_inputs` (`step.cpp:2967-2971`) require those staged writes; instead, have `PhysicalStep::probe_step_inputs` (or `NativeRideRuntime::probe_step_inputs`) snapshot and restore `mjModel::dof_frictionloss` and `Stepper::posvel_fresh_` (or explicitly restrict model-buffer purity to `NativeRideRuntime` post-restore).
2. **`P07b` (Pure Hill-Hold Brake Parity on Active Probes)**:
   - Extract a `[[nodiscard]] double peek_rollback_brake_demand(double speed_mps, const RideControl *control) const noexcept` helper from `PhysicalStep::rollback_brake_demand` (`step.cpp:2367-2383`) that evaluates the hysteresis guard against `rollback_hold_` without mutating `rollback_hold_`.
   - In `apply_forces_body` (`step.cpp:1308-1316`), when `resolved == nullptr`, set `.rollback_demand = active ? peek_rollback_brake_demand(...) : 0.` so `probe_step_inputs` (`active == true`) matches `advance_physics` while equilibrium settle (`active == false`, `settle_contacts.cpp:48`) remains unaffected.

# Stage 3 (`R1`) Independent Research Report — `P08`: `engine::invoke` TLS Error Containment, `Stepper` Fail-Closed Poisoning & RTSan Non-Blocking Discipline

- **Inspected Commit:** `0502660dd14bd1021b3da90a61e20dcb1d5805a1`
- **Compliance Verdict (`R1` Agent):** `partially_violated`
- **Product Justification Verdict (`R1` Agent):** `mixed_subclauses`

## 1. Producers, Mutators, Consumers & Potential Breakers
- FSM-021 (`engine::invoke` TLS Frame):
  - State: `thread_local Frame *active_frame` (`native/src/engine_call.cpp:78`), `Frame::{jump, previous_frame, previous_handler, forward_handler, error}` (`native/src/engine_call.cpp:66-74`), MuJoCo TLS slot via `_mjPRIVATE_getTlsLogHandler`/`_mjPRIVATE_setTlsLogHandler` (`native/src/engine_abi_312.hpp:15-17`).
  - Mutators/Producers: `engine::invoke` (`native/src/engine_call.cpp:398-434`), `restore_frame` (`180-183`), `log_handler` (`124-132`), `fatal_message` (`112-122`).
  - Consumers: All `engine::*` wrappers (`native/src/engine_call.cpp:483-776`), `RiderContactWriter::refresh_kinematics*` (`native/src/writers/rider_contacts.cpp:1330-1344`), `Transmission::evaluate_candidate` (`native/src/drivetrain/transmission.cpp:187`), `RenderOwner::{apply,draw}` (`native/src/viewer/render_owner.cpp:98-106, 124-140`).
- FSM-022 & FSM-023 (`Stepper::poisoned_` & `allowed_time_callback_`):
  - State: `bool Stepper::poisoned_ = false` (`native/src/stepper.hpp:518`), `TimeCallback Stepper::allowed_time_callback_ = nullptr` (`522`).
  - Writers/Mutators: `Stepper` ctors (`native/src/stepper.cpp:73, 83`), `refresh_time_callback_policy` (`211-230`, warms `engine::native_time()` static `epoch` at `219`, `native/src/engine_call.cpp:472`), `mutate` (`native/src/stepper.hpp:165-174`), `try_step` (`native/src/stepper.cpp:240-254`), `try_forward` (`256-270`), `reset` (`284-311`), `set_state` (`556-609`).
  - Guards/Observers: `is_poisoned()` (`native/src/stepper.hpp:155`), `require_healthy()` (`native/src/stepper.cpp:232-238`), `require_unmarked_time_callback()` (`188-199`).
  - Breakers / Bypass Paths:
    1. `Stepper::kinetic_energy() const` (`native/src/stepper.cpp:700-708`): calls `engine::mul_m` (`704`) without `mutate()` or `catch (const engine::EngineFailure&)`; `poisoned_` is non-`mutable`.
    2. `Stepper::rider_contacts_{release_all,set_enabled,attachment_samples,settle}` (`native/src/stepper.cpp:775-784, 807-822`): check `require_healthy()` on entry, but do NOT wrap in `mutate()` (stale comments at `native/src/stepper.hpp:427-428, 444-445` assumed the T63-deleted Python binding wrapped them), so `EngineFailure` from `RiderContactWriter::refresh_kinematics*` (`native/src/writers/rider_contacts.cpp:334, 350, 900, 908`) escapes without setting `poisoned_ = true`.
    3. Unchecked accessors `Stepper::{model,data}` (`native/src/stepper.hpp:176-177`) and `Stepper::{drive,suspension,resistance,tire_writer,cruise,rider_contacts}` (`native/src/stepper.cpp:319-352, 752-758`, `stepper.hpp:346`): omit `require_healthy()`, allowing engine-invoking writer calls (`Transmission` `copy_model`/`set_const` at `native/src/drivetrain/transmission.cpp:187, 296, 418`) when poisoned and failing to poison `Stepper` if they throw when healthy (e.g., `native/src/initialization/initial_state.cpp:195`).
    4. `Stepper::mutate` (`native/src/stepper.hpp:165-174`): catches only `engine::EngineFailure`; non-`EngineFailure` throws after partial `mjData`/`mjModel` mutation leave `poisoned_ == false`.
- FSM-028 (`ControlClock`):
  - State: `steps_per_period_` (`std::int64_t`), `held_` (`std::optional<rider::NamedEntries<double>>`) (`native/src/runtime/control.hpp:121-122`).
  - Mutators/Consumers: ctor (`native/src/runtime/control.cpp:49-71`), `hold` (`80-82`), `held` (`84-88`), `reset`/`held_state` (`native/src/runtime/control.hpp:108-112`); owned by `PhysicalStep::control_clock_` (`native/src/runtime/step.hpp:618`), mutated/read in `PhysicalStep::{reset,apply_forces_body,state,restore}` (`native/src/runtime/step.cpp:1182, 1248-1249, 1522-1536, 3024, 3051-3054`).
  - Breaker: Ctor absolute tolerance `std::abs(static_cast<double>(steps) * timestep_s - period_s) > 1e-9` (`native/src/runtime/control.cpp:67`) accepts non-integer `period_s / timestep_s` (e.g. `1.5e-9 / 1e-9 == 1.5` or `0.6e-10 / 1e-10 == 0.6`) whenever `timestep_s <= 2e-9`, and `std::nearbyint` (`65`) depends on active `FE_*` rounding mode.

## 2. Sub-Clause Evidence Analysis (Supporting & Opposing)
- Sub-clause (a) — Axis 1: HOLDS (within `engine::invoke`'s `Operation` contract). `engine::invoke` (`native/src/engine_call.cpp:398-434`) pre-screens `unsupported_callback()` before touching TLS (`401-405`), installs trivially destructible `Frame` (`66-74, 407-424`) onto `active_frame` (`78`) and `_mjPRIVATE_setTlsLogHandler(&log_handler)` (`410`), and runs `setjmp(frame.jump)` (`426`). On `mjLOG_ERROR`, `log_handler` -> `fatal_message` (`112-132`) copies bounded error text (`95-110`) and `std::longjmp`s (`121`) without C++ stack unwinding across C frames. Both normal (`428`) and `longjmp` (`431-433`) exits call `restore_frame` (`180-183`), restoring `active_frame` and `previous_handler`.
  - Axis 2: Confirmed native engineering policy (`docs/NATIVE_POLICIES.md:107`, `docs/agents/native-engineering.md:13, 39`, `docs/TESTING.md:857-868`).
- Sub-clause (b) — Axis 1: PARTIALLY VIOLATED.
  - Holds on `try_step`/`try_forward`/`step`/`forward` (`native/src/stepper.cpp:250-251, 266-267, 272-282`), `mutate` for `EngineFailure` (`native/src/stepper.hpp:170-173`), `reset()` post-`reset_data` `catch (...)` (`native/src/stepper.cpp:287-309`), `set_state()` pre-reset snapshot + `recovering` writer-reset `catch (...)` (`562-608`), and non-poisoning of `ErrorKind::unsupported_callback` (`188-199, 245-251, 261-267`).
  - Violated on four reachable C++ paths: (1) `Stepper::kinetic_energy() const` (`native/src/stepper.cpp:700-708`) calls `engine::mul_m` without `try/catch` and cannot set non-`mutable` `poisoned_`; (2) `Stepper::rider_contacts_{release_all,set_enabled,attachment_samples,settle}` (`775-784, 807-822`) check `require_healthy()` on entry but omit `mutate()`, so `EngineFailure` in `RiderContactWriter::refresh_kinematics*` (`native/src/writers/rider_contacts.cpp:334, 350, 900, 908`) leaves `poisoned_ == false`; (3) `Stepper::{drive,tire_writer,rider_contacts,suspension,resistance,cruise,model,data}` (`native/src/stepper.cpp:319-352, 752-758`, `stepper.hpp:176-177, 346`) neither check `require_healthy()` when poisoned nor latch `poisoned_` if writer engine calls throw; (4) `Stepper::mutate` (`stepper.hpp:165-174`) does not latch `poisoned_` on non-`EngineFailure` exceptions after partial state/data mutation.
  - Axis 2: Confirmed engineering contract (`docs/NATIVE_POLICIES.md:108-109`, `docs/TESTING.md:866-879`), but P08(b)'s universal phrasing over all mutation/engine calls inherits stale pre-T63 Python-binding assumptions (`native/src/stepper.hpp:427-428, 444-445`).
- Sub-clause (c) — Axis 1: PARTIALLY VIOLATED (conditional on error/warning path).
  - Both `Stepper::try_step` and `Stepper::try_forward` carry `noexcept BIKE_NONBLOCKING` (`native/src/stepper.hpp:152-153`, `native/src/rtsan.hpp:30-37`). Once `refresh_time_callback_policy` (`native/src/stepper.cpp:211-230`) warms `engine::native_time()`'s function-local static `epoch` (`native/src/engine_call.cpp:472`), normal steps and prechecked errors (`poisoned_`, unvetted `mjcb_time`, `unsupported_callback()`, `nplugin != 0`, and out-of-range `opt.solver` via lock-free `invalid_solver_error` at `native/src/engine_call.cpp:159-178, 599-623`) do not allocate, lock, or throw.
  - However, any *non-prechecked* MuJoCo `mju_error` (e.g. `m->opt.integrator = 99` in `mj_step`) or `mju_warning` forwarded via `frame->forward_handler` (`native/src/engine_call.cpp:130-131`) executes MuJoCo's C `vsnprintf`/stdio formatting, taking libc locks inside the `[[clang::nonblocking]]` region.
  - Axis 2: Confirmed engineering contract for valid/prechecked paths (`docs/NATIVE_POLICIES.md:109`, `docs/TESTING.md:1301-1333`), but `docs/TESTING.md:880-885` explicitly disclaims RTSan safety for arbitrary MuJoCo fatal formatting beyond `opt.solver`.
- Sub-clause (d) — Axis 1: PARTIALLY VIOLATED (sub-nanosecond & FP-rounding edge cases).
  - `ControlClock::held()` while `Empty` (`!held_.has_value()`) strictly throws `std::runtime_error("control clock holds no command yet")` (`native/src/runtime/control.cpp:84-88`), both after construction and after `reset()` (`native/src/runtime/control.hpp:108`).
  - `ControlClock` ctor (`native/src/runtime/control.cpp:49-71`) enforces `steps >= 1` and `|steps * timestep_s - period_s| <= 1e-9` (`66-69`). Because `1e-9` s is an absolute duration tolerance (unlike `decode_runtime_config`'s relative `1e-9 * control_period_s` at `native/src/runtime/bootstrap.cpp:266-267`), `timestep_s <= 2e-9` accepts non-integer ratios (e.g. `ControlClock(1e-9, 1.5e-9)` -> `ratio = 1.5`, `steps = 2`, residual `5e-10 <= 1e-9`; `ControlClock(1e-10, 0.6e-10)` -> `ratio = 0.6`), and `std::nearbyint` (`65`) can misround inexact binary64 quotients (e.g. `0.003 / 0.0001`) under `FE_DOWNWARD`/`FE_TOWARDZERO`.
  - Axis 2: Confirmed simulation/product requirement (`docs/agents/simulation-decisions.md:155-156, 619-622`, `docs/NATIVE_POLICIES.md:20-23`, which explicitly specifies *"within the source alignment tolerance"*).

## 3. Exceptions, Cross-Module Dependencies & Failure Consequences
- Environmental / caller assumptions & hard aborts:
  - Assumes no concurrent cross-thread mutation of process-global `mjcb_*` / `mju_user_*` callbacks (`native/src/engine_call.cpp:134-150`, `native/src/stepper.cpp:211-230`). MuJoCo's TLS log slot isolates threads (`native/src/engine_abi_312.hpp:16`), whereas callback slots are process-global.
  - `engine::invoke` requires `Operation` (`void (*)(void*) noexcept`) to construct only trivially destructible automatic objects across potential `longjmp` (`native/src/engine_call.hpp:20-23`, `native/src/engine_call.cpp:74`). Null `active_frame`, `message`, or `frame->error` inside `log_handler`/`fatal_message` calls `std::abort()` (`native/src/engine_call.cpp:116-117, 126-127`).
  - Pinned to exact MuJoCo ABI `312` (`mjVERSION_HEADER == 312`, `_mjPRIVATE_setTlsLogHandler`; `native/src/engine_abi_312.hpp:9-23`, `native/src/engine_call.cpp:55-64`).
- Architectural & runtime consequences:
  - Production `PhysicalStep` (`native/src/runtime/step.cpp:1155-1159, 1761-1769, 1810`) does NOT call `Stepper::try_step` or `Stepper::try_forward`; it calls throwing `engine::forward*` / `engine::step*` inside `stepper_->mutate(...)`. In production TUs, `Stepper::try_step`/`try_forward` are only called by `Stepper::step()`/`forward()` (`native/src/stepper.cpp:275, 281`).
  - `NativeRideRuntime` maintains its own outer `bool poisoned_` (`native/src/runtime/runtime.cpp:898-902, 916`, `native/src/runtime/step.hpp:661`), mitigating `Stepper::mutate`'s non-`EngineFailure` gap during `NativeRideRuntime::reset()`, whereas standalone `Stepper` callers (`SetupOwner`, `PhysicalInitialState`, `RuntimeRigAccess`, C++ tests) lack that outer latch.
  - `DrivetrainWriter::try_components_into` (`native/src/writers/drivetrain.cpp:546-551`) catches `engine::EngineFailure` and translates it to `CoreStatus::engine_failure` without notifying `Stepper`, leaving `Stepper::poisoned_ == false` if a transmission commit fails inside `try_components_into`.
  - Unguarded C calls outside `engine::invoke` (`mj_kinematics`, `mj_comPos`, `mj_mulJacTVec`, `mj_jac*` in `native/src/writers/drivetrain.cpp:260`, `native/src/drivetrain/transmission.cpp:160-161, 178-179, 505`, `native/src/rider/spindle_controller.cpp`, `native/src/writers/tire.cpp`) are permitted by `check_contracts.cpp:2251-2253` (which scans only `mj_(loadModel(Buffer)?|makeData|forward|step|resetData|setConst)`); any `mjLOG_ERROR` inside those unguarded C calls would bypass `engine::invoke` and terminate via MuJoCo's default error handler.

## 4. Located Test Coverage vs. Unexercised Gaps
- Actively Asserted Coverage:
  - Sub-clause (a): `native/tests/test_contracts.cpp:2591-2777, 2899-2946`, `native/tests/engine/test_boundaries.cpp:277-460`, and `native/tests/reference/test_native_engine_errors.cpp:365-652` exercise `mjLOG_ERROR` `setjmp`/`longjmp` capture, nested `Frame` TLS handler restoration, warning forwarding, all 11 `mjcb_*`/`mju_user_*` rejections, `nplugin != 0`, and 4-thread concurrent fault isolation. `native/src/tools/check_contracts.cpp:2251-2288` statically polices raw `mj_{loadModel*,makeData,forward,step,resetData,setConst}` outside `engine_call.cpp`.
  - Sub-clause (b): `native/tests/test_contracts.cpp:2779-2849`, `native/tests/engine/test_boundaries.cpp:382-460`, and `native/tests/reference/test_native_engine_errors.cpp:454-611, 655-737` assert fatal poisoning on `try_step`/`step`, refusal of `try_step`/`try_forward`/`step`/`forward`/`state`/`set_inputs`/`mutate`/`qfrc_actuator`/`qacc_smooth`, retention of `poisoned_` on invalid-shape `set_state`, recovery + writer reset on valid `set_state()` and `reset()`, and non-poisoning on `unsupported_callback`. Runtime prefix/reset recovery is asserted in `native/tests/reference/test_native_research_failures.cpp:19-58` and `native/tests/reference/test_native_runtime_commands.cpp:173-194`.
  - Sub-clause (c): `native/tests/rtsan_control.cpp:22-56` verifies RTSan allocation interception in the `rtsan` preset; `native/tests/test_contracts.cpp:2851-2897` asserts `mjcb_time` warming (`engine::native_time`) and custom `mjcb_time` blocking (`custom_time_calls == 0`).
  - Sub-clause (d): `native/tests/reference/test_control_clock.cpp:39-145` and `native/tests/reference/test_native_clock_contracts.cpp:100-149` assert `ControlClock` rejection of non-integer `(.002, .005)`, non-positive/non-finite durations, overflow/underflow ratios, and `Empty` `held()` throws initially and after `reset()`.
- Unexercised Paths & Coverage Gaps:
  - Missing tests for post-`reset_data` writer-throw poisoning in `Stepper::reset()` (`stepper.cpp:304-309`) and `Stepper::set_state()` (`595-607`).
  - Missing tests for `Stepper::kinetic_energy()` fatal `mj_mulM`, direct C++ `Stepper::rider_contacts_{set_enabled,release_all,attachment_samples,settle}` `EngineFailure`, and `stepper.drive().*` access while `is_poisoned() == true` (all unexercised bypasses).
  - RTSan (`BIKE_NONBLOCKING`) is compiled as a no-op in default `release`/`asan` gates (`native/src/rtsan.hpp:35`) and never exercises non-prechecked `mju_error` (e.g. invalid `opt.integrator`) or forwarded `mju_warning` inside `try_step`/`try_forward`.
  - Missing tests for `ControlClock` with `timestep_s <= 2e-9` s, directed `FE_*` rounding modes, and runtime restore of `StepState` with non-tick `step` + `held_control = nullopt` hitting `control_clock_.held()` in `PhysicalStep::apply_forces_body` (`native/src/runtime/step.cpp:1534`).

## 5. Recommended Contract / FSM Refinement
Refine P08 into four exact, scope-accurate sub-invariants and fix the C++ `Stepper` boundary gaps left after T63:
1. **(a) TLS Error Containment (`engine::invoke`):** Keep as stated, explicitly noting the trivial-frame `noexcept` `Operation` precondition (`native/src/engine_call.hpp:20-23`) and scoping to calls routed through `engine::invoke` (enforced for `mj_{loadModel*,makeData,forward,step,resetData,setConst}` by `check_contracts.cpp:2251-2253`).
2. **(b) `Stepper` Fail-Closed Poisoning:** Either (i) code-fix `Stepper` by wrapping `rider_contacts_{release_all,set_enabled,attachment_samples,settle}` in `mutate()`, guarding/latching `kinetic_energy()` (or routing through `mutate` / `mutable bool poisoned_`), and documenting `model()`/`data()`/writer refs as low-level caller-`mutate`-responsible seams; or (ii) narrow the spec statement to `Stepper`'s guarded entry points (`try_step`, `try_forward`, `step`, `forward`, `mutate` on `EngineFailure`, and `reset`/`set_state` post-`reset_data` throws), while preserving non-poisoning on `ErrorKind::unsupported_callback`.
3. **(c) `BIKE_NONBLOCKING` Realtime Status Path:** Qualify `Stepper::try_step` and `Stepper::try_forward` as satisfying `BIKE_NONBLOCKING` across normal stepping and prevalidated error paths (`poisoned_`, unvetted `mjcb_*`/`mju_user_*`, `nplugin != 0`, out-of-range `opt.solver`) once `refresh_time_callback_policy` has warmed `mjcb_time`, explicitly excluding unprechecked MuJoCo C `mju_error`/`mju_warning` `snprintf`/stdio paths per `docs/TESTING.md:880-885`.
4. **(d) `ControlClock` Alignment & Hold Gate:** State that `ControlClock` rejects `held()` while `Empty` (`std::runtime_error`) and enforces `steps = nearbyint(period_s / timestep_s) >= 1` within the `1e-9` s absolute alignment tolerance (`|steps * timestep_s - period_s| <= 1e-9`, `ratio < 2^63`; `docs/NATIVE_POLICIES.md:22`), or tighten `control.cpp:65-69` with `std::round` + `std::min(1e-9, 1e-9 * period_s)` to eliminate sub-nanosecond and `FE_*` edge cases.

# Stage 3 (`R1`) Independent Research Report — `P09`: `NativeRideRuntime` Single-Owner Exclusion, Committed-Prefix Preservation & Balance/Brake/Crash Monitors

## 1. Producers, Mutators, Consumers & Potential Breakers
(a) EXCLUSION: advancing_ flag declared native/src/runtime/runtime.hpp:236 (comment 231-237 documents mutual exclusion); AdvancementGuard class native/src/runtime/runtime.cpp:74-86 (non-blocking compare_exchange_strong at 77-80, throws std::runtime_error("advance already in progress on this runtime"), destructor releases). NativeResearchRuntime uses a separate ResearchGuard on busy_ native/src/runtime/research.cpp:14-28; every entry guarded (e.g. advance_control 343, acknowledge_control 397, snapshot 408, close 677, test_fail_at_step 683); ordering busy_->advancing_ consistent (research close 677-681 calls runtime_->close() which takes advancing_). Golden RuntimeAccess native/src/capture.cpp:176,196 nests busy_->advancing_. ~NativeRideRuntime takes no guard (standard lifetime contract: destroying an object during an in-flight call is UB anyway). (b) COMMITTED PREFIX: committed_* producers: initialize_accounting runtime.cpp:656-662 (bootstrap values), reset() 977-988, advance_interval() 717-722, external_interval test-rig runtime_access.cpp:99-103. advance_interval() 701-735 is the sole normal commit path: accounting_->collect 705, capture_accounted_presentation 710, test_failure_boundary 711 (solver=99 probe 675-688), advance_physics 712, accounting_->push 713, engine::get_state into next_integration_state_ 714-715, capture_presentation into next_presentation_ 716, committed swaps 717-718, committed_step_/time_s_/outcome_ writes 719-721, cache_research_boundary 722, finish-position gate 723-725, flush 726/close_period 727-734. (c) CRASH: producers: physical_contact_crash step.cpp:1843-1845 (contact-derived causes, staged post-mj_step in solve_tail); latch site step.cpp:2260-2268 — set_event called only when !crash_detector_.event().has_value() (contact causes win same-step conflicts); CrashDetector::check control.cpp:363-381 — early return 365 if event_ latched, then pitch_over 368-373, else handlebar_contact 374-379 (priority: contact > pitch_over > handlebar within one step; first step wins across steps). Mutators: set_event control.hpp:367-369 (unconditional — callers gate), restore path step.cpp:3065 set_event(s.crash), init-time clear initialization/finalize.cpp:69. crash_outcome mapping runtime.cpp:62-69 (pitch_over+pitch<0 -> crash:loop_out, pitch>0 -> crash:endo, else crash:<cause>); committed_outcome_ = crash_outcome(crash) at 721; latched outcome permanently stops the loop (792 !committed_outcome_). Codec brake_latch_codec.cpp:11-33 — exact keys {front,rear}, each null or finite real, reject-before-commit per hpp:19-24. ## 2. Sub-Clause Evidence Analysis
(a) Single-owner exclusion + non-latching rejected close: HOLDS. All 19 public entries take the CAS guard; acquisition is non-blocking and fail-fast (runtime.cpp:74-86); close() latches closed_ only at 996 AFTER the guard at 995, so a guard-rejected close leaves closed_ false — verified by test_native_runtime_commands.cpp:94-153 which asserts advance/reset/snapshot/probe_step_inputs/close all throw 'advance' under an in-flight advance and !closed() at 143-144. Guard-before-state-check ordering is deliberate (776-778 comment): the pre-guard require_open at 763/arg validation is side-effect-free; post-guard re-check at 780 is race-free since close() holds the same flag. Reentrant same-thread entry also fails (no thread-identity exemption — intended). Research layer composes via its own busy_ flag with consistent busy_->advancing_ ordering; the inner runtime_ is private/unreachable so advance_interval bypass at research.cpp:376 is safe. Boundary note: destructor and closed() are unguarded by design (atomic read; object-lifetime contract). Committed-prefix preservation: HOLDS. committed_step_/time_s_/outcome_/integration/presentation are written ONLY at 717-721 after the whole interval (physics+accounting push+state capture) succeeds; advance() catches EngineFailure (801-808), best-effort flushes owned captures, rethrows; Stepper is poisoned (stepper.hpp:170-172) so retry cannot re-run the dead engine. Snapshots render the last committed boundary, never live mjData — acknowledged design at runtime.hpp:216-218 ('The engine may already be partially mutated when a fatal call throws'). Test test_native_runtime_ownership.cpp:420-482 proves bitwise-exact prefix preservation (frame->step==3, integration_state bitwise equal, history last_id==2, buffered 0, poisoned-advance rejection 464-467, reset recovery 468-479); test_native_research_failures.cpp:16-54 pins status/snapshot/rows prefix at the failure step. Caveats (source-supported, minor): capture_accounted_presentation(committed_presentation_) on InvalidReferenceRun paths (706-710, 729-734, 740-744) rewrites the joints_saturated lane of committed_presentation_ — by design, still describes the committed boundary; publish_raw's step_+=1 at step.cpp:2259 precedes the crash latch at 2260 — a bad_alloc in set_event's string copy would leave live step_ ahead of committed_step_ (theoretical only); a post-mj_step non-engine throw (alloc) leaves mjData ahead of the committed count — retry continues from the advanced engine state, consistent with the documented boundary contract. (b) CrashDetector first-cause latch: HOLDS. event_ early-returns at control.cpp:365 once latched; the contact-crash producer checks !event().has_value() before set_event at step.cpp:2260-2261 so a latched cause can never be overwritten; check() runs every interval at 2269 until latched. Same-step priority contact(catch_plane_contact/rider_ground_contact) > pitch_over > handlebar_contact; across steps, first commit wins. Episode-local: cleared only via bootstrap/init restore (step.cpp:3065 set_event(s.crash) replays the fresh episode's null; initialization/finalize.cpp:69) — no advancing-path clear exists. Outcome conversion at runtime.cpp:62-69 maps pitch_over sign to loop_out/endo; latch outcome halts the advance loop (792) and flushes accounting (726). No dedicated unit test asserts first-cause irreversibility under conflicting causes — source-supported, under-tested (see test_coverage). (c) BalanceMonitor: MOSTLY HOLDS with one literal inaccuracy and one gate narrowing. 'burns grace_left_s only while riding == true': literally FALSE as worded — started_s is ENGAGED only while riding (control.cpp:300) so parked samples never consume grace, but once engaged grace_left_s = max(0, started_s+grace_s - time_s) burns in wall-clock; the !riding early-return at 304-309 does NOT pause the decay (only a braked stop re-arms via 299). A rolling-brake window (riding=false, stop=none) therefore keeps consuming already-started grace — this is exactly the confirmed product decision (simulation-decisions.md:160-168: 3 s after release re-armed only by a full braked stop; brief rolling braking pauses dwell, not grace). Charitable reading ('grace engages only while riding') holds. Re-arm on BalanceStop::braked: HOLDS at monitor level (299); production gate narrows it — balance_channel emits braked only when motor_braking && standstill && (launch phase==hold || grace already spent) (step.cpp:2803-2809), so a mid-grace braked stop does not re-arm (re-arming mid-grace would extend grace — arguably the intent). Sticky event: HOLDS — `if (!event) event = current_event` at 316 latches the first event for the episode; only reset()/restore() clears. current_event clearing on speed recovery: HOLDS and broader — cleared on !riding, in-grace, or speed>=floor (304-309). (d) StaticBrake latch round-trip: VIOLATED-BY-DESIGN at the StepState layer, HOLDS at the codec layer. StaticBrake::state/restore and the wire codec round-trip bitwise (test_static_brake_latch.cpp proves two-instance continuation, exact-key decode, atomic invalid rejection, probe non-mutation). StepState.brake_latch is exported by state() (step.cpp:3037) and required on decode (runtime.cpp:263,314-316; bootstrap.cpp:391-399), i.e. the serialization contract DOES claim to preserve the anchors — but PhysicalStep::restore() calls brake_.reset() at step.cpp:3061 and never applies s.brake_latch. This asymmetry is deliberate and documented ('a fresh episode still re-bites (brake_.reset() stays the rule)' step.hpp:203-206; pinned 'as ruled' by test_static_brake_latch.cpp:199-208). Impact is benign for all actual callers (every restore caller is init/bootstrap-time — initial_state.cpp:205, refine.cpp:75, initial_pose.cpp:62, runtime.cpp:544 — where a fresh episode legitimately re-bites; a standstill under held demand re-latches at the current angle on the first advancing step since |omega|<=engage). The exposure is latent: any future mid-episode checkpoint-resume built on StepState would silently drop the anchors.

## 3. Exceptions, Dependencies & Consequences
Exceptions: guard rejection is std::runtime_error (non-blocking, immediate); post-close rejection is std::logic_error via require_open (runtime.cpp:496-498); input rejections (non-finite demands 766-769, negative/nonfinite budget 770-773, negative target 774, behind-committed target 781-783, invalid control 784) throw invalid_argument BEFORE any mutation — a rejected call never dirties committed state (test_native_runtime_ownership.cpp:405-414). Mid-advance EngineFailure -> Stepper poisoned (stepper.hpp:170-172) -> all subsequent advancing calls fail 'poison' until reset() rebuilds contexts (runtime.cpp:975-977); advance() catches it only to flush accounting captures and rethrow the ORIGINAL exception (801-808, comment 802-803 forbids masking). Non-engine mid-advance throws (InvalidReferenceRun from accounting collect/close_period/flush at 705/728/739, bad_alloc anywhere) propagate uncaught but leave committed_* at the last successful interval — with two consequences: (1) accounting may retain a full unacked period that the next advance retries via `if (accounting_->full()) flush_accounting()` at 790 (documented 788-789); (2) a post-mj_step throw leaves mjData ahead of committed count — retry continues from the advanced state, an accepted boundary-semantics trade-off (runtime.hpp:216-218). Dependencies: BalanceMonitor depends on brakes.riding (resolve_brakes step.cpp:2409-2415, which excludes rollback-only demand via motor_braking = caller||rider at ~2412 — a rollback-held standstill yields stop=none, deliberate per 'holds without declaring braking'), on launch phase rider_intent_.launch().phase (2806), on monotonic time enforced by its own rewind guard (control.cpp:293-295) and GroundedFilter's (control.cpp:106-108). CrashDetector depends on contacts.handlebar_in_contact() and physical_contact_crash staging (step.cpp:1843-1845). StaticBrake latching depends on advance flag (probe vs advancing, static_brake.cpp:97) and hold config engage/release bands. Assumptions verified: single-owner threading per native-engineering.md:45 is enforced by the fail-fast flag rather than assumed; non-negative monotone timestamps enforced at decode (runtime.cpp:266-267) and monitor level. Consequences of the (d) asymmetry: semantic fidelity preserved for t=0 replay (fresh episode re-bites), bitwise fidelity NOT preserved through PhysicalStep::restore — a fabricated bootstrap carrying non-null latches decodes fine but silently drops them on restore (no diagnostic emitted).

## 4. Test Coverage & Gaps
COVERED: (a) concurrency — test_native_runtime_commands.cpp:94-153 drives a real second thread holding the guard via advance(1e9, budget=5s) and asserts advance/reset/snapshot/probe_step_inputs/close each throw 'advance' plus !closed() at 143-144; close-then-everything at 156-165; invalid-input no-mutation at test_native_runtime_ownership.cpp:405-414. (a-prefix) engine-failure prefix — test_native_runtime_ownership.cpp:420-482 (bitwise prefix, poison-until-reset, reset recovery + row reproduction), test_native_runtime_commands.cpp:173-193 (real numerical-wall failure keeps committed prefix readable), test_native_research_failures.cpp:16-54 (research-layer prefix: step/status.error/recorded_rows/snapshot pinned at injected step; reset bumps generation). (c) balance — test_balance_monitor.cpp:63-345 directly exercises grace engagement/consumption, riding-gated start, rolling-brake pause without re-arm, full braked-stop re-arm, sticky first event, current_event clearing on recovery AND on !riding, reset, plus production presentation/summary consumption of balance fields. (d) latch — test_static_brake_latch.cpp:94-252: real JSON codec crossing, bitwise continuation between two StaticBrake instances through advance/release/re-latch/reseat sequences, probe non-mutation, atomic invalid-restore rejection (216-252), and runtime-level reset clearing (199-208 'as ruled'). GAPS: (1) no dedicated crash-latch unit test — conflicting causes (e.g. pitch_over latched then a contact crash, or same-step contact-vs-pitch priority) and post-restore irreversibility are asserted nowhere; coverage is indirect via end-to-end crash outcomes (e.g. loop_out prevention in acceptance tests) and the env-outcome mapping; (2) the production BalanceStop gate at step.cpp:2803-2809 (launch-hold-phase and grace_spent branches) is not unit-tested in isolation — monitor tests inject stop directly; (3) concurrency test covers only the advance-in-flight direction — no test holds the guard via reset()/close()/snapshot() while racing another entry, no same-thread reentrancy test, no RuntimeRigAccess::Guard rejection test; (4) no bootstrap-level test feeding a document with non-null brake_latch through NativeRideRuntime construction to pin the deliberate drop (the 'as ruled' behavior is asserted only at the PhysicalStep::reset level); (5) no test exercises a NON-EngineFailure mid-advance throw (e.g. allocation) for prefix preservation — the EngineFailure path is covered but the un-poisoned-retry path (790) is only code-reviewed; (6) external_interval's divergent literal "crash:"+cause (runtime_access.cpp:103) vs crash_outcome()'s loop_out/endo mapping (runtime.cpp:62-69) is unexercised by any assertion.

## 5. Recommended Refinement
Reword subclause (c) to match confirmed mechanics and intent: 'grace_left_s engages on the first riding sample (started_s latched only while riding, control.cpp:300) and then burns in wall-clock — non-braked !riding windows pause the dwell timer but do NOT pause or re-arm grace; a full braked stop (BalanceStop::braked) re-arms grace by clearing started_s (control.cpp:299); the production braked-stop classification additionally requires launch-hold phase or already-spent grace (step.cpp:2803-2809), so a mid-grace braked stop cannot extend the current grace window; the first balance-loss event latches permanently per episode (control.cpp:316) while current_event clears on speed recovery, in-grace, or non-riding samples (304-309).' Reword subclause (d) to state the actual contract rather than assume symmetry: 'brake_latch serializes both optional anchors through a strict codec (exact keys, null-or-finite, reject-before-commit) and StaticBrake::restore() applies them atomically; PhysicalStep::restore() deliberately does NOT apply them — it resets latches so every fresh episode re-bites (step.cpp:3061, step.hpp:203-206); the wire preserves the field, the apply path drops it by design.' Suggested test additions (all currently absent): a CrashDetector/PhysicalStep test latching one cause then presenting a conflicting cause asserting first-wins both across steps and same-step (contact-vs-pitch priority); a balance_channel-level test driving the launch-hold and grace_spent branches of the stop gate rather than injecting stop; guard tests for reset-in-flight/close-in-flight directions, same-thread reentrant snapshot()-in-advance rejection, and RuntimeRigAccess::Guard; a construction-level bootstrap test asserting a non-null serialized latch is silently dropped (or asserting whatever the ruling intends if mid-episode resume is ever added); and harmonize or document external_interval's literal crash-outcome mapping (runtime_access.cpp:103) against crash_outcome() (runtime.cpp:62-69). If a mid-episode StepState checkpoint/restore feature is ever introduced, step.cpp:3061 must be revisited — it is the single point where the serialized latch field is intentionally discarded.
