# Stage 3 (`R1`) Independent Research Report — `P10`: `ModelStatus`, `ReferenceMonitor`, `PeriodAccounting` & `AsyncCloseRunner` Transactional Period-Close & Sync Fallback

- **Inspected Commit:** `0502660dd14bd1021b3da90a61e20dcb1d5805a1`
- **Compliance Verdict (`R1` Agent):** `partially_violated`
- **Product Justification Verdict (`R1` Agent):** `mixed_subclauses`

## 1. Producers, Mutators, Consumers & Potential Breakers
- **FSM-032 `ModelStatus` (`native/src/runtime/status.hpp:41-59`, `status.cpp:186-288`):**
  - *State:* `counts`, `first`, `last_interval = -1`, `maximum_compression_fraction`, `maximum_linkage_error_m`, `numerically_valid = Wire("not_evaluated")`, `calibration_status` (`native/src/runtime/status.hpp:42-49`).
  - *Producers/Mutators:* `ModelStatus::observe` (`native/src/runtime/status.cpp:186-219`), `ModelStatus::reset` (`221-227`), `ModelStatus::from_wire` (`251-288`), invoked from `PeriodAccounting::flush_local` (`native/src/runtime/accounting.cpp:739-740`), `AccountingState::from_wire` (`native/src/runtime/accounting.cpp:436`), and `NativeRideRuntime::initialize_accounting` (`native/src/runtime/runtime.cpp:580-584`).
  - *Consumers:* `ModelStatus::as_wire`/`state_wire` (`native/src/runtime/status.cpp:229-249`), sample `"model_status"` channel (`native/src/runtime/accounting.cpp:741`), `PeriodAccounting::snapshot` (`native/src/runtime/accounting.cpp:834-840`), `NativeResearchRuntime::read_status` (`native/src/runtime/research.cpp:536-579`), `evaluate_run_quality` (`native/src/research/quality.cpp:94-119`), `recording::ResearchEpisodeSource` (`native/src/recording/research_source.cpp:149-168`).
  - *Breakers:* `ModelStatus::from_wire` (`native/src/runtime/status.cpp:257-276`) allows `count == 0`, duplicate keys in `counts`, `counts.empty() != !first.has_value()`, and `counts` non-empty with `last_interval == -1` (bypassing `NativeRideRuntime::initialize_accounting`, `native/src/runtime/runtime.cpp:580-583`).
- **FSM-033 `ReferenceMonitor` (`native/src/runtime/status.hpp:28-39`, `status.cpp:68-107`):**
  - *State:* `strict`, `first_failure`, `warning_pending` (`native/src/runtime/status.hpp:29-31`).
  - *Producers/Mutators:* `ReferenceMonitor::observe` (`native/src/runtime/status.cpp:68-76`), `take_warning` (`78-83`), `from_wire` (`90-107`), `PeriodAccounting::take_publication` (`native/src/runtime/accounting.cpp:485`), `apply_publication` (`495`), `flush_local` (`763, 792-794, 812`), `NativeRideRuntime::take_warning`/`reset` (`native/src/runtime/runtime.cpp:942-954, 973-974`).
  - *Consumers:* `PeriodAccounting::snapshot` (`native/src/runtime/accounting.cpp:837`), `cli::ride` (`native/src/cli/ride.cpp:277-285`), `viewer::hud` (`native/src/viewer/hud.cpp:200-209`).
- **FSM-034/035 `PeriodBuffer`, `PeriodAccounting` & `AsyncCloseRunner` (`native/src/runtime/{period_buffer,accounting,async_close}.{hpp,cpp}`):**
  - *State:* `PeriodBuffer::{capacity_, next_interval_, buffer_}` (`native/src/runtime/period_buffer.hpp:42-45`); `PeriodAccounting::{state_, buffer_, pending_, retained_, latest_, async_, detached_, detached_at_}` (`native/src/runtime/accounting.hpp:179-192`); `AsyncCloseRunner::{evaluator_, sequence_, stopping_, finished_, worker_lost_, failure_, jobs_, pending_, results_}` (`native/src/runtime/async_close.hpp:55-68`).
  - *Mutators:* `PeriodAccounting::push`/`close_period`/`submit_period`/`collect`/`detach_async`/`flush`/`flush_local`/`acknowledge_samples` (`native/src/runtime/accounting.cpp:462-832`); `AsyncCloseRunner::submit`/`peek`/`acknowledge`/`detach_pending`/`work` (`native/src/runtime/async_close.cpp:22-141`).
  - *Breakers:* `flush_local`'s `restore` lambda (`native/src/runtime/accounting.cpp:658-672, 711-741, 795-798`) mutates `RawStep::channels` in place before rollback; `AsyncCloseRunner::work` inner `catch` (`native/src/runtime/async_close.cpp:127-131`) leaves `worker_lost_ == false`, raising `AsyncPublicationError` (`48-53`) which `PeriodAccounting::collect` (`native/src/runtime/accounting.cpp:550`) and `submit_period` (`568`) do not auto-detach.
- **FSM-036 `SensorPipeline` (`native/src/runtime/sensors.{hpp,cpp}`):**
  - *State:* `SensorState::{queue, last_time, delivery_time, startup, cursor, samples_attempted, samples_dropped}` (`native/src/runtime/sensors.hpp:36-42`).
  - *Producers/Mutators:* `SensorPipeline::reset`/`import_state`/`push`/`read` (`native/src/runtime/sensors.cpp:81-167`), `startup_sensor_state` (`native/src/research/rider_random.cpp:41-48`), `ResearchEpisode` ctor & `consume_sample` (`native/src/runtime/research.cpp:86-98, 204-211`).
  - *Consumers:* `NativeResearchRuntime::observation`/`status` (`native/src/runtime/research.cpp:548-550, 623`), `research::PolicySession` (`native/src/research/policy_session.cpp:111-125`).

## 2. Sub-Clause Evidence Analysis (Supporting & Opposing)
- **Sub-clause (a) `ModelStatus` relational, positivity, and sticky `numerically_valid` invariants:**
  - *Axis 1 (Compliance — **VIOLATED in `from_wire()`, nuanced in `observe()`**):*
    - Across default construction, `reset()`, and `observe()` (`native/src/runtime/status.hpp:42-49`, `status.cpp:186-227`), staging `next_counts`/`next_first`/`next_numerical` before commit preserves `(counts.empty() <==> !first.has_value())`, `count >= 1` (with `int64_t::max()` overflow guard at `201-202`), and sticky `false` on `numerically_valid` (`211-213`).
    - *Nuance on `observe()`:* `include_energy == true` is **necessary but not sufficient** to evaluate `numerically_valid`: `status.cpp:209-210` checks `energy != nullptr && !object(*energy).empty()`. If `include_energy == true` and `"energy"` is absent or `{}` (tested at `native/tests/research/test_status.cpp:92-95`), `numerically_valid` remains `"not_evaluated"`. Within `PeriodAccounting::flush_local` (`native/src/runtime/accounting.cpp:706, 716-718, 739-740`), `"energy"` is always non-empty, so there it evaluates iff `measured == true`.
    - *Concrete violations in `ModelStatus::from_wire()` (`native/src/runtime/status.cpp:251-288`):* (1) line `259` checks `if (count < 0)` rather than `count <= 0`, accepting `count == 0` (violating `count >= 1` and making `as_wire()` report `"model_valid": false` at `232` with zero counts); (2) lines `257-276` never check `result.counts.empty() == !result.first.has_value()`, accepting both `{counts: {}, first: {...}, last_interval: 0}` and `{counts: {"x": 1}, first: null, last_interval: -1}`; (3) duplicate keys in `counts` `WireObject` are not rejected (`257-261`). Moreover, `{counts: {"x": 1}, first: null, last_interval: -1}` even passes `NativeRideRuntime::initialize_accounting` (`native/src/runtime/runtime.cpp:580-583`, which only checks `last_interval != -1`).
  - *Axis 2 (Product Justification — **Mixed**):* Sticky `model_valid`/`numerically_valid`, preservation of original failure facts, and excluding unmeasured forwarded intervals from `numerically_valid` are **confirmed requirements** (`docs/agents/simulation-decisions.md:288-292` «Действует» item 32, `docs/RECORDING.md:55-60, 84-88`, `docs/NATIVE_POLICIES.md:123`). Strict `from_wire` cross-field invariants beyond valid bootstrap payloads are an **engineering contract** (`docs/agents/native-engineering.md:45`).

- **Sub-clause (b) `ReferenceMonitor` earliest `first_failure`, at most one non-strict warning, and strict `InvalidReferenceRun` after `flush_local` commit:**
  - *Axis 1 (Compliance — **HOLDS per episode in `flush_local`/`collect`/`detach_async`, with bootstrap & crash-cleanup caveats**):*
    - `ReferenceMonitor::observe()` (`native/src/runtime/status.cpp:68-76`) latches `first_failure` and sets `warning_pending = !strict` only when `!first_failure.has_value()` (`72-75`); `take_warning()` clears `warning_pending` (`78-83`). Across async transport, `take_publication()` clears worker `warning_pending` (`native/src/runtime/accounting.cpp:485`) while `apply_publication()` OR-merges main's unconsumed `warning_pending` (`495`).
    - In `PeriodAccounting::flush_local()` (`native/src/runtime/accounting.cpp:792-813`), `InvalidReferenceRun` is thrown at line `812` **strictly after** committing `state_`, `latest_`, `pending_`, `retained_`, controller/contact diagnostics, and `buffer_.clear()` (`800-809`). The same post-commit order holds in `collect()` (`544-548`) and `detach_async()` (`529-534`).
    - *Caveats:* (1) Single-warning bound is per episode (reset on `NativeRideRuntime::reset`, `native/src/runtime/runtime.cpp:972-975`, though unconsumed warning from the outgoing episode is preserved into `pending_warning_` at `973-974`); (2) `NativeRideRuntime::initialize_accounting` (`576-585`) does not reject a bootstrap wire state that already carries `monitor.first_failure`; (3) when `EngineFailure` occurs mid-period (`native/src/runtime/runtime.cpp:801-807`), `advance()` flushes the partial period inside `catch (...) {}`, intentionally suppressing `InvalidReferenceRun` so `EngineFailure` propagates.
  - *Axis 2 (Product Justification — **Confirmed Requirement**):* Explicitly specified in `docs/agents/simulation-decisions.md:275-276` («Действует» item 30), `docs/RIDE.md:118-126, 308-312`, and `docs/RECORDING.md:55-60`.

- **Sub-clause (c) `flush_local` pre-commit `RawStep` restoration & `AsyncCloseRunner` fallback via `detach_async()`:**
  - *Axis 1 (Compliance — **PARTIALLY VIOLATED on both halves**):*
    - *Violation C1 (`RawStep` NOT restored untouched):* `flush_local()` moves `raw.channels` into `channels` (`native/src/runtime/accounting.cpp:711-712`) and mutates it **in place** via `merge(channels, effort.channels)`, `set(..., "energy")`, `set(..., "attachment_violations")`, conditional `add_detail(...)`, and `set(..., "model_status")` (`713-741`). On pre-commit exception (`795-798`), `restore()` (`658-672`) moves those **mutated** `channels` back into `raws[i].channels` (acknowledged by comment at `599-603`). Worse, if an explicit partial `flush()` (`raws.size() < capacity`, tail row `raw.full == false`) throws after `add_detail` ran on `index + 1 == raws.size()` (`720-733`) and the caller pushes another `RawStep` before retrying `flush_local()`, that former tail row is no longer `index + 1 == raws.size()`, so retry does NOT overwrite `add_detail` keys and publishes contaminated channels!
    - *Violation C2 (`AsyncCloseRunner` failures do NOT generally degrade via `detach_async()`):* In `AsyncCloseRunner::work()` (`native/src/runtime/async_close.cpp:112-131`), any job evaluation failure (except `InvalidReferenceRun`, which is captured into `strict_error` at `116-117`) is caught by the inner `catch (...)` (`127-131`), setting `failure_` while leaving **`worker_lost_ == false`**. Consequently, `peek()` (`46-54`) throws **`AsyncPublicationError`**, NOT `AsyncWorkerLost`. `PeriodAccounting::collect()` (`native/src/runtime/accounting.cpp:543-552`) only catches `AsyncWorkerLost`, and in `submit_period()` (`564-572`), when `submit()` throws `AsyncWorkerLost` (`async_close.cpp:25-26`), line `568` calls `collect(false)` which immediately throws `AsyncPublicationError` **before** line `569` (`if (async_) detach_async();`) executes! Only `AsyncWorkerLost` (`worker_lost_ == true` or premature `finished_`) auto-degrades; for `AsyncPublicationError`, `detach_async()` is only reached if called directly on `PeriodAccounting` (`NativeRideRuntime` has no caller to `detach_async()`).
    - *Holding core of `detach_async()`:* When `detach_async()` **is** invoked (`native/src/runtime/accounting.cpp:514-538`, `async_close.cpp:78-90`), `collect()` only acknowledges after `apply_publication()` commits (`545-546`), `detach_pending()` extracts all unacknowledged `pending_` periods in FIFO sequence order after `worker_.join()`, and `detach_async()` replays `detached_[detached_at_]` from committed `state_`, advancing `detached_at_++` only after `apply_publication()` (`532-533`), guaranteeing zero lost or duplicated periods.
  - *Axis 2 (Product Justification — **Mixed Engineering Contract & Overly Strong Hypothesis**):* Staging before commit and retry equivalence are **engineering contracts** (`docs/agents/native-engineering.md:38-41`), whereas bit-for-bit untouched `RawStep::channels` on rollback and automatic fallback on *arbitrary* worker exceptions contradict intentional implementation design (`accounting.cpp:566-567, 599-603`; note `docs/NATIVE_POLICIES.md:23-24` still contains a stale T34 note claiming `async_telemetry` is unimplemented).

- **Sub-clause (d) `SensorPipeline` `cursor == samples_attempted`, non-decreasing `delivery_time`, and startup `cursor == 1` at `t = 0`:**
  - *Axis 1 (Compliance — **HOLDS with episode-boundary scope**):*
    - `cursor == samples_attempted` holds unconditionally: initialized `{0, 0}` (`native/src/runtime/sensors.hpp:41`), rolled back on `reset()` failure (`sensors.cpp:85-88`), incremented together after `queue.push_back` in `push()` (`139-143`, bounded by `state_.cursor >= tape_.noise.size()` at `126`), untouched in `read()` (`146-167`), and enforced in `import_state()` (`91`: `next.cursor != next.samples_attempted`).
    - `read(now)` enforces `now >= 0` (`147`) and `now >= *state_.delivery_time` (`148-149`) before updating `state_.delivery_time = now` (`165`). (`reset()`/`import_state()` replace episode state wholesale at episode boundaries, `86, 120`.)
    - Startup non-duplication holds in research startup: `startup_sensor_state()` (`native/src/research/rider_random.cpp:41-48`) calls `reset(initial_t0)` (`cursor = 1`) and `read(0.)`, `ResearchEpisode` (`native/src/runtime/research.cpp:90-96`) asserts `startup.cursor == 1 && startup.samples_attempted == 1`, imports it, calls `read(0.)` (keeping `cursor == 1`), and schedules `next_sensor_step = c.sensor_steps >= 1` (`90, 111`) so step 0 does not re-push `t = 0` (`204-210`). (Note: on a raw standalone `SensorPipeline` *before* `reset()`, `cursor == 0`.)
  - *Axis 2 (Product Justification — **Confirmed Engineering Policy**):* Startup `cursor == 1` non-duplication is an explicit contract in `docs/NATIVE_POLICIES.md:26-28`; `cursor == samples_attempted` and monotonic `delivery_time` are sound engineering contracts.

## 3. Exceptions, Cross-Module Dependencies & Failure Consequences
- **Assumptions & Preconditions Required for Holding Clauses:**
  - `PeriodBuffer::push` requires finite non-decreasing `[time_s, end_time_s]` and consecutive `raw.interval_id == next_interval_` (`native/src/runtime/period_buffer.cpp:43-59`).
  - `ModelStatus::observe` requires `interval_id > last_interval` and `std::isfinite(time_s)` (`native/src/runtime/status.cpp:188-189`).
  - `ReferenceMonitor::observe` requires finite `failure.time_s >= 0` (`native/src/runtime/status.cpp:70-71`).
  - `SensorPipeline` requires validated `SensorConfig` and `NoiseTape` (`noise.size() == dropout_uniform.size() >= 1` for startup `reset()`), finite non-negative strictly increasing `source_time_s`, and non-decreasing `now >= 0` (`native/src/runtime/sensors.cpp:54-80, 123-127, 147-149`).
- **Exception / Error Suppression & Lifecycle Boundaries:**
  - **Engine crash cleanup (`NativeRideRuntime::advance`):** On `engine::EngineFailure`, `try { flush_accounting(); } catch (...) {}` flushes any partial period and deliberately suppresses `InvalidReferenceRun` so `EngineFailure` is not masked (`native/src/runtime/runtime.cpp:801-807`). `NativeResearchRuntime::handle_failure` similarly catches trailing `InvalidReferenceRun` and records it in `cleanup_notes` (`native/src/runtime/research.cpp:333-334`).
  - **Episode reset boundaries:** Warning one-shotness (`ReferenceMonitor::warning_pending`) and `SensorPipeline::delivery_time` monotonicity hold *within* an episode / `ReferenceMonitor` / `SensorPipeline` run; `NativeRideRuntime::reset` (`native/src/runtime/runtime.cpp:972-975`), `ModelStatus::reset` (`native/src/runtime/status.cpp:221-227`), and `SensorPipeline::reset`/`import_state` (`native/src/runtime/sensors.cpp:81-121`) reinitialize state for a new episode.
- **Concrete Consequences of Identified Violations:**
  1. **Corrupted `model_valid` / `first_model_violation` via `ModelStatus::from_wire` (`native/src/runtime/status.cpp:257-276`):** Accepting `count == 0` yields `counts.empty() == false` (`model_valid == false` in `as_wire()`, line `232`) with zero violations and `first == nullopt`; accepting `counts: {"x": 1}, first: null, last_interval: -1` bypasses `NativeRideRuntime::initialize_accounting` (`native/src/runtime/runtime.cpp:580-583`) and permanently marks a fresh episode `model_valid == false` with `first_model_violation == null`.
  2. **Stale channel contamination after aborted partial `flush_local()` (`native/src/runtime/accounting.cpp:658-672, 720-733`):** If an explicit partial `flush()` (`raws.size() < capacity`, last row `raw.full == false`) throws before commit (e.g., `pending_.reserve` at `790`), `restore()` puts back `channels` carrying `add_detail` keys (`"rider_welds"`, `"rider_control"`, `"endpoint_mass"`, `"component_work_j"`). If another `RawStep` is pushed before retrying `flush_local()`, that former tail row now has `raw.full == false && index + 1 != raws.size()`, skipping `add_detail` (`720`) yet retaining stale `add_detail` channels and double-counting `rider_support` in `WorkHistory::add` (`native/src/runtime/samples.cpp:479`).
  3. **Blocked `NativeRideRuntime` on async worker failure (`native/src/runtime/async_close.cpp:46-54, 127-131`, `native/src/runtime/accounting.cpp:543-552, 565-571`):** Any non-`InvalidReferenceRun` exception inside worker job execution leaves `worker_lost_ == false`, causing `peek()` to throw `AsyncPublicationError` instead of `AsyncWorkerLost`. Neither `collect()` nor `submit_period()` calls `detach_async()`, and `NativeRideRuntime` exposes no `detach_async()` entry point, leaving unacknowledged periods stranded in `async_` unless `reset()` discards the episode or a direct `PeriodAccounting` caller invokes `detach_async()`.

## 4. Located Test Coverage vs. Unexercised Gaps
- **Actively Asserted in Tests:**
  - *`ModelStatus::observe` & `reset`:* `native/tests/research/test_status.cpp:61-98, 112-165` asserts `"not_evaluated" -> true -> false -> false` stickiness, `include_energy == false` and empty `"energy"` masking (`92-95`), earliest `first_model_violation` preservation, `reset()`, and non-advancing `interval_id` rejection.
  - *`ReferenceMonitor` & strict post-commit throw:* `native/tests/reference/test_reference_monitor.cpp:58-103`, `native/tests/reference/test_monitor_strictness.cpp:153-204, 471-513`, and `native/tests/reference/test_native_runtime_accounting.cpp:509-613` assert earliest `first_failure` latching, one-shot `take_warning()`, and that strict `InvalidReferenceRun` is raised only after all period samples, `latest_`, and `snapshot()` commit.
  - *`AsyncCloseRunner` & manual `detach_async()` replay:* `native/tests/integration/test_async_close.cpp:126-380` and `native/tests/reference/test_async_recovery.cpp:133-309` assert worker parity with sync accounting, `AsyncPublicationError` surfacing on injected `before_close_` failure (`225-248`), and lossless/duplication-free replay when the test manually calls `accounting.detach_async()`.
  - *`SensorPipeline`:* `native/tests/reference/test_sensor_noise.cpp:186-236`, `native/tests/reference/test_native_research_sensors.cpp:108-282`, and `native/tests/research/test_bootstrap.cpp:383-434, 618-656` assert `cursor == samples_attempted`, startup `cursor == 1` non-duplication, backward `delivery_time` rejection, non-increasing `source_time_s` rejection, `import_state` validation, and tape exhaustion safety.
- **Unexercised Paths & Coverage Gaps:**
  1. **Zero negative/fuzz tests on `ModelStatus::from_wire` & `ReferenceMonitor::from_wire` cross-field invariants:** `native/tests/reference/test_native_state_fuzz.cpp` fuzzes `DriveWriter::restore` and `Stepper::set_state`, not `AccountingState::from_wire`; `count == 0` (`status.cpp:259`), `counts.empty() != !first.has_value()` (`257-276`), and `t = 0` bootstrap with `{counts: {"x": 1}, first: null, last_interval: -1}` (`runtime.cpp:580-583`) are completely untested.
  2. **Zero fault-injection / rollback tests on `PeriodAccounting::flush_local`'s `restore()` lambda (`accounting.cpp:658-672, 795-798`):** `native/tests/test_contracts.cpp:4016-4018, 4145-4233` sweeps `std::bad_alloc` on `OwnedNativeEpisode`, explicitly noting `PeriodAccounting` is not linked into `native_contract_tests`. Neither channel mutation on rollback nor partial-flush-then-push `add_detail` contamination is tested.
  3. **Zero tests exercising automatic `AsyncWorkerLost` fallback (`worker_lost_ == true`):** Every fault test in `test_async_close.cpp` and `test_async_recovery.cpp` injects via `before_close_`, which sets `worker_lost_ = false` (`AsyncPublicationError`) and invokes `detach_async()` manually; the automatic `catch (const AsyncWorkerLost&) { detach_async(); }` path in `accounting.cpp:550-552` and the masked `submit_period` catch block at `565-572` are unexercised for true `worker_lost_`.

## 5. Recommended Contract / FSM Refinement
Split and tighten `P10` into implementation-accurate, requirement-aligned contracts and file targeted fixes for the deserialization/rollback gaps:
1. **Refine & fix (a) `ModelStatus`:**
   - *Specification refinement:* State that `numerically_valid` transitions `"not_evaluated" -> {true, false}`, `true -> {true, false}`, `false -> false`, and is evaluated iff `include_energy && find(channels, "energy") != nullptr && !object(*find(channels, "energy")).empty()` (`native/src/runtime/status.cpp:209-214`).
   - *Code fix warranted:* Harden `ModelStatus::from_wire` (`native/src/runtime/status.cpp:257-276`) to require `count >= 1`, unique keys in `counts`, `counts.empty() == !result.first.has_value()`, and `result.counts.empty()` when `result.last_interval == -1` (plus `!monitor.first_failure.has_value()` in `NativeRideRuntime::initialize_accounting`, `native/src/runtime/runtime.cpp:580-584`).
2. **Refine (b) `ReferenceMonitor`:**
   - Scope single-warning emission and `first_failure` latching to a single episode/`ReferenceMonitor` lifecycle, and explicitly note the intentional `EngineFailure` cleanup exception (`native/src/runtime/runtime.cpp:801-807`, `research.cpp:333-334`) where trailing `InvalidReferenceRun` is suppressed/captured so primary engine failures propagate after committing partial-period diagnostics.
3. **Refine & fix (c) `PeriodAccounting` Rollback & `AsyncCloseRunner` Recovery:**
   - *Specification refinement:* Distinguish **lost-worker transport** (`AsyncWorkerLost`, auto-degraded via `detach_async()` in `collect()`/`submit_period()`) from **worker evaluation failure** (`AsyncPublicationError`, surfaced to the caller without automatic fallback, recoverable only via explicit `PeriodAccounting::detach_async()`). State that *explicit* `detach_async()` replays unacknowledged periods in order without loss or duplication.
   - *Code fix warranted:* In `PeriodAccounting::flush_local` (`native/src/runtime/accounting.cpp:658-672, 711-741`), either strip synthesized keys (`effort.channels`, `"energy"`, `"attachment_violations"`, `add_detail` keys, `"model_status"`) inside `restore()` or copy the small `raw.channels` top-level spine before mutating so aborted partial flushes leave `RawStep::channels` genuinely untouched.
4. **Refine (d) `SensorPipeline`:**
   - Keep `cursor == samples_attempted` as an unconditional invariant, qualify `delivery_time` monotonicity as holding across `read(now)` calls within an episode (resettable via `reset()`/`import_state()`), and state startup non-duplication as `cursor == 1` at `t = 0` *after* `reset(initial)` / `startup_sensor_state()` import (`native/src/runtime/sensors.cpp:81-96`, `research.cpp:90-96`).

# Stage 3 (`R1`) Independent Research Report — `P11`: `NativeResearchRuntime` Control-Window Staging, Retry Idempotency & Exclusive Episode Outcome Machine

- **Inspected Commit:** `0502660dd14bd1021b3da90a61e20dcb1d5805a1`
- **Compliance Verdict (`R1`):** `partially_violated`
- **Product Justification Verdict (`R1`):** `mixed_subclauses`

## 1. Producers, Mutators, Consumers & Potential Breakers
All in native/src/runtime/research.{hpp,cpp} unless noted. PRODUCERS/MUTATORS: window — set begin_control:184-196 (start=runtime_->committed_step_, count=min(control_steps, max_steps-start) so target>start always); read advance_control:351,355,364,376, finish_window:295,309-310, stop:658, fail_policy:663, reset_unlocked:629, test_fail_at_step:684, status 'active':443, begin guard:176; cleared acknowledge_control:403, handle_failure:338, reset (new episode 645). delivery_pending/pending_transition — staged atomically finish_window:313-322 (reserve_next:301-303 pre-allocates journals; the only throwing copy `pending=result` at 313 precedes all mutation; ops 318-322 are noexcept: counter bump, SensorObservation POD copy, optional<double>, bool store); cleared ack:401-402; delivery flag cleared handle_failure:339 (pending_transition is unreachable-with-value at that point). requested_count/completed_count — ++195 per accepted window / ++319 per finish_window; read status:449. sample_cursor — init -1 (hpp:74); claimed before side effects consume_samples:283; skipped consumed at 281; acknowledge_through accounting.cpp:859-863 erases only <=cursor. stop_requested — set only stop:658 (requires window&&!done); read+conditional-truncate+cleared acknowledge_control:404-405; surfaced status:443. Outcome flags — consume_sample gates in fixed order: numerical_quality (253-258: numerically_valid=false, truncated, early return under stop_at_outcome), model_violation (260-264, gated by stop_on_model_violation), crash (265-271, terminated, 'crash:*' from committed_crash_), finish (272-275, terminated, e.truth->position_m>=track_length_m); finish_window 'duration' truncation only when !done (296-299); stop() direct truncate when idle (659); ack 'operator_stop' only when !done (404); fail_policy terminated+reason='policy_error'+error only when !done (664-668); handle_failure sets terminated unconditionally then reason 'invalid_controller'|'simulation_error' + error 'InvalidReferenceRun: '|'RuntimeError: ' (326-330), flushes+consumes trailing samples with stop_at_outcome=false (333-336), restores reason (337), resets window (338), clears delivery_pending (339). Delay queue — commands.emplace_back(start+delay_steps):192; drained per iteration 360-363; motor channel from motor_applied, rider channels from programs.apply(window->control, time) 364-369; applied_records journal on effective-control change 370-374 (startup row ctor:99); runtime_->advance_interval(applied, window->front, window->rear):376 — exactly one committed step per call (runtime.cpp:701-735, committed_step_=physical_->step() at 719; step.cpp:2229 assigns interval_id=step_ then step_+=1 at 2259). Sensor gate — next_sensor_step=sensor_steps ctor:90, +=sensor_steps on push 208-210. Journals: requested:193, transitions:315, observations:97,316, rider_commands_journal:233-241 (dedup via flat_record_equal:47-64); cursor-drained prepare/acknowledge_applied:529-555 and rider_commands:556-581 (vector erase only in verify capture). e.truth:211 per consumed sample; e.observation:320 at finish; e.demand_nm:321 (per-window snapshot seen_demand:186 -> transition:310). error — handle_failure:330, fail_policy:667, set_error_text:670-675 (no production callers). CONSUMERS: PolicySession::advance_pending policy_session.cpp:225-243 (s.completed staging + exactly-once ack + queued ops after ack; matches NATIVE_POLICIES.md:92 'act once per boundary, ack once before queued actions'); drain_active lifecycle.cpp:47-56 (stop() then advance/ack loop — exercises the line-350 early-return and is the production path that can set stop_requested mid-window); cli/research.cpp:74-121 frame hooks (pending()='active||delivery_pending' 29-31); viewer/research.cpp:11-20; replay/session.cpp:213-253 (begin per recorded request:228-230, advance+evidence compare:232-235, ack:238); ResearchRun lifecycle.cpp finish:202-260 / seal:141-201 (drain-then-seal); seal_episode research.cpp:506-514 (flush only, NEVER consumes); evidence_snapshot:471-481 (no window/pending guard); legacy_snapshot:515-524 (REJECTS window||delivery_pending — asymmetric with seal); exit code exit_codes.cpp:3-9 (error present -> 3 before validity flags); take_pending_warning:687-694 -> runtime.cpp:942-954. BREAKERS: (1) handle_failure never clears stop_requested (324-340) — begin->stop->advance-fails leaves done()&&stop_requested observable via status:443 and inside sealed evaluation (evidence_unlocked:462-470 embeds status_unlocked); also transiently, stop requested before a self-terminating window leaves stop_requested=T&&done=T during delivery_pending until ack:405. (2) consume_samples(false) inside handle_failure:335 runs outcome gates with terminated already true — lines 253-264 set truncated unconditionally -> terminated&&truncated (and numerically_valid=false); reason overwrite is restored at 337 but flags are not. (3) The same conjunction is reachable on the normal path under async_telemetry: the post-loop `if (has_raws()) {flush; consume_samples();}` at 379-382 iterates the unclaimed tail of the done publication and each consume_sample runs gates without checking e.done() (the done check is only the post-call loop break at 285). (4) handle_failure's unconditional terminated (326) + reason overwrite converts an already-truncated episode into terminated&&truncated if finish_window staging throws after truncation (OOM corner). (5) set_error_text's precondition (terminated && !delivery_pending, 672) permits attaching error text to non-error outcomes ('finish','crash:*') — dead API but flips exit code to 3 (exit_codes.cpp:5) and episode_exit_code reads 'error' before 'reason'. (6) Claim names 'runtime_error' — actual reason strings are 'simulation_error'/'invalid_controller'/'policy_error' (327-330, 666).

## 2. Sub-Clause Evidence Analysis
(a) HOLDS, and is exception-safety-airtight. delivery_pending<=>pending_transition.has_value(): the pair is published/cleared together (318+322, 401-402); between 318 and 322 only noexcept ops run, and every throwing op (result copy 313, journals' reserve 301-303, sensors.read 305, demand_at 304, model_valid 308) precedes publication, so no reachable boundary shows one without the other — pending_transition can only be set by finish_window which sets the flag in the same non-throwing tail. delivery_pending=>window: window is not reset by finish_window; it survives through the pending period and is cleared only at ack:403 or handle_failure:338 which also clears the flag. 0<=completed<=requested<=completed+1: requested++ only in begin_control:195 (guarded by 'window||delivery_pending' throw:176 and done throw:177); completed++ only in finish_window:319 (requires window:295; cannot run twice because advance early-returns at 350 during pending). Retry idempotency: advance_control:350 `if (e.delivery_pending) return e.pending_transition;` returns a copy of the identical staged value with zero state mutation — modulo that argument validation (345-348) and require_open (344) run first, so a retry with an invalid budget/target or on a closed runtime throws instead of returning the staged transition (boundary-conditional, not a state effect). Allocation ordering in begin_control mirrors the discipline: reserve_next:189 -> the sole allocating emplace:192 -> non-allocating journal/last_requested/counter/window 193-196. (b) PARTIALLY VIOLATED. !(terminated&&truncated) holds on every primary path (per-sample early returns 257/263/270, consume loop break 285, 'duration' gated !done 296, operator_stop gated !done 404, fail_policy gated !done 664) — but is reachably broken: consume_samples(false) inside handle_failure can set truncated on the already-terminated episode (measured bad-energy or model-violating trailing sample hits 253-264); the async-telemetry post-loop consume at 379-382 runs at least one more sample's gates after done; and a finish_window staging throw on a truncated episode is overwritten by terminated at 326. 'done() ==> !stop_requested' is violated persistently — handle_failure omits clearing it (only ack:405 clears) — and transiently during delivery_pending when a stop-requested window self-terminates (status:443 reports both until ack). 'consume_samples(false) never sets truncated when terminated' is literally false as coded: the gates do not test e.terminated. 'error iff error-reason' holds along production paths (invalid_controller/simulation_error/policy_error all attach at 330/667; no other outcome attaches) but the claimed reason 'runtime_error' does not exist in the taxonomy ('simulation_error'/'invalid_controller'), and set_error_text's guard would let error text ride on 'finish'/'crash:*' — currently uncalled. (c) HOLDS. The while at 355 bounds iterations to target-start; each iteration issues exactly one advance_interval:376 and advance_interval commits exactly one step (runtime.cpp:712-719; step.cpp:2259) or throws; yields at 356-359 only exist when the optional args are supplied; done() exits early (fewer steps); consume_samples and the delay-queue drain do no physics. Transition.physics_steps = committed-start (309) <= bound. (d) HOLDS as an implicit but consistent contract. sample_cursor = last claimed interval_id (283), one sample per committed interval (accounting.cpp:746 assigns interval_id=raw.interval_id; step.cpp:2229). Mid-period finish/numerical_quality/crash: the done sample is consumed, later samples of the same publication remain pending_ (acknowledge_through erases only <=cursor, accounting.cpp:859-863); seal_episode:506-514 flushes buffered raws into retained_ but never consumes, so the sealed physical tape covers the full committed prefix while research-side aggregates (truth, sensors, energy max, tracker) stop at the cursor — 'an engine failure can leave a physical suffix beyond the completed-control prefix; both extents are saved' (RECORDING.md:74-75). The lag is observable as accounting.pending_intervals (state_wire, accounting.cpp:842) and recorded_rows vs step (status:447-448). handle_failure deliberately consumes the tail instead (consume_samples(false):335) giving cursor=committed-1 post-failure. Caveats: the cursor itself is not exported; a sample that throws inside consume_sample is permanently marked consumed (283-291 'claim before side effects'); seal tolerates an open window while legacy_snapshot:520-521 rejects one — asymmetry is documented intent ('Sealing never integrates or completes an external window', 510-511).

## 3. Exceptions, Dependencies & Consequences
EXCEPTION-SAFETY: every boundary mutator is ordered validate->allocate->publish — begin_control reserves requested (189) before the only allocating emplace (192), finish_window reserves both journals (301-303) before the throwing copy (313), so invalid-input/bad_alloc retries leave zero observable mutation (native-engineering.md:38-41 honored). consume_samples claims sample_cursor BEFORE side effects and acknowledges it even on throw (283-291) — a sample that breaks consume_sample is permanently 'consumed' and skipped on retry (281); deliberate Python-contract port. handle_failure itself can throw (cleanup-notes push_back:334/336, string building:330) -> the outer catch (386-391) sets terminated+window/delivery cleared but does NOT set reason or error, permitting a terminal episode with neither — an OOM-only residual hole in the error-iff clause. DEPENDENCIES: single-owner assumption enforced by ResearchGuard CAS on busy_ (14-28) — every public method including status/snapshot; the physical runtime has its own AdvancementGuard (runtime.cpp:779) and owns committed_step_/committed_crash_/finish flushing (runtime.cpp:719-726 — crash triggers an internal partial-period flush which is how the crash sample reaches pending_). Async telemetry (config_.async_telemetry) changes which consume path sees the post-done tail. The delay queue e.commands is never cleared on failure — dead state inside the terminated episode (harmless, reset replaces the episode 642-648). valid_for_learning (hpp:58-60) excludes only 'simulation_error'/'policy_error'; 'invalid_controller' is absent from the exclusion but no transition is ever fabricated for failures, so the gap is inert. CONSEQUENCES: the two reachable (b) violations flow into status() and the sealed evaluation/run_metadata evidence (evidence_unlocked:462-470 embeds status_unlocked including stop_requested); exit code stays 3 regardless because 'error' is checked first (exit_codes.cpp:5), so the stale flag does not corrupt the exit path but does corrupt recorded evidence and any consumer keying on status.stop_requested. sealed evidence after a mid-window seal legitimately contains requested==completed+1 (test_research_capture.cpp:243-275 pins complete_control_steps=0, physical_rows=1) — consistent with, not a violation of, (a).

## 4. Test Coverage & Gaps
COVERED: window latching + zero-budget pure-poll + target-step pacing yield + resume + double-ack throw — native/tests/research/test_bootstrap.cpp:523-556 (checks_yield_and_pacing), native/tests/reference/test_native_viewer_snapshot.cpp:183-219 (target slice, rejected stale target keeps pending); stop semantics — test_bootstrap.cpp:563-611 (stop_requested pending until ack, applied once at ack, idle-stop truncate), test_native_research_environment.cpp:80-88 (queued stop -> operator_stop), 127-140 (stop on ended episode preserves reason); boundary non-mutation — env:60-123 (invalid begin/budget/reset leave state untouched, advance-without-window throws:121-122); failure prefix — test_bootstrap.cpp:663-695, test_native_research_failures.cpp:19-58 (simulation_error reason, 'RuntimeError: ' error text, completed_count=0, no fabricated transition, reset recovers), test_lifecycle.cpp:211-226 failure_child; strict -> InvalidReferenceRun -> reason 'invalid_controller' env:742-779; session-level yield/drain/idempotent finish test_lifecycle.cpp:107-134 boundary_and_retry, queued_reset:135-151; seal idempotence + mid-window extents test_research_capture.cpp:116-163, 243-275 (mid-window seal: complete_control_steps=0, physical_rows=1, requested row retained), interruption drain:309-344; applied/rider_commands cursor contracts test_research_capture.cpp:167-203, test_native_research_capture.cpp:20-51, test_rider_brake_channel.cpp:502-509; measured-vs-unmeasured energy gate test_status.cpp:112-165 (RuntimeRigAccess::consume_sample with stop_at_outcome=true only, lines 149,157); full-vs-off evaluation parity incl. requested_count/completed_count test_status.cpp:231-285; oracle/golden transition boundaries env:307-832 (physics_steps, duration reason, sensor counters 1+(step-1)/sensor_steps:821-823). GAPS: (1) no test ever calls advance_control while delivery_pending — the line-350 retry is exercised only transitively via drain loops and never asserts returned-transition identity or side-effect-freedom; (2) no stop()+mid-window-failure combination -> the stale stop_requested persistence is undetected; (3) no consume_samples(false) or consume-on-terminated test -> the terminated&&truncated conjunction undetected (rig seam called only with true); (4) no async_telemetry research episode -> the post-done consume at 379-382 unexercised; (5) no assertion of pending_transition<=>delivery_pending or the counter algebra at arbitrary boundaries — only happy-path status pins exist; (6) no seal test asserting the sample_cursor/pending_intervals vs committed-step arithmetic after a mid-period finish/numerical_quality outcome (the incomplete-window test yields at step 1 mid-window rather than ending mid-period); (7) set_error_text entirely uncovered and unreferenced outside its own definition; (8) no coverage of handle_failure's own throw path (reason/error possibly unset on a terminated episode).

## 5. Recommended Refinement
Split (b) into separate checkable claims and correct the taxonomy: the error-attaching reasons are 'simulation_error' and 'invalid_controller' (handle_failure:327-330) plus 'policy_error' (fail_policy:666) — there is no 'runtime_error' reason string; the error text prefixes are 'RuntimeError: '/'InvalidReferenceRun: '. Two genuine defects to adjudicate rather than paper over: (1) add `e.stop_requested = false` alongside `e.delivery_pending = false` at handle_failure:339 — a one-line fix matching acknowledge_control:405 semantics, or else the claim must be rewritten to bless a stale flag in failed episodes (a status consumer and the sealed evaluation can observe done()&&stop_requested); (2) decide whether the episode outcome partition survives failure cleanup — if yes, guard the outcome writes in consume_sample with !e.done() (or return early when done) so consume_samples(false) can still fold truth/sensor/energy evidence into the failed episode without flipping terminated&&truncated; if the union of flags is intended evidence, the claim must instead specify that 'numerical_quality'/'model_violation' may co-set truncated on an error-terminated episode and that reason retains the failure via the 337 restore. Refine (b)'s consume claim to: consume_samples(false) folds trailing committed samples with all gates active but must not change the terminal-reason partition — and add the missing tests: identical-retry assertion on advance_control post-completion (returned transition equals the first, no counter/journal/step changes); begin->stop->test_fail_at_step->advance-throws -> status asserts !stop_requested; RuntimeRigAccess::consume_sample(...,false) on a terminated episode with a measured bad-energy trailing sample; an async_telemetry episode exercising the post-done consume; a mid-period finish seal asserting pending_intervals == committed-tail size. Keep (a),(c),(d) as stated — they are accurate descriptions of the implemented contract (and (d) is documented intent in RECORDING.md:74-75 plus the seal comment at research.cpp:510-511), with the note that seal_episode deliberately tolerates open windows while legacy_snapshot rejects them.

# Stage 3 (`R1`) Independent Research Report — `P12`: Whole-Boundary Realtime Measurement, Benchmark Valid-Prefix Isolation & Provider Identity Non-Rescue

- **Inspected Commit:** `0502660dd14bd1021b3da90a61e20dcb1d5805a1`
- **Compliance Verdict (`R1`):** `holds`
- **Product Justification Verdict (`R1`):** `relevant_confirmed_requirement`

## 1. Producers, Mutators, Consumers & Potential Breakers
PRODUCERS: monotonic_clock()=steady_clock (realtime_measurement.cpp:117-119); measure_run/measured() (realtime_measurement.cpp:156-166, 35-113); NativeDriver incl. boundary()/reason()/advance()/flush()/close() (realtime_driver.cpp:34-137); parse_options/--benchmark (realtime_options.cpp:39-77); resolve_measurement_ride (realtime_driver.cpp:139-153); compare_runs/build_report/steady_state_evidence (realtime_report.cpp:48-137); installed_provider_identity (installed_provider.cpp:31-80); current_provider_identity+required_provider_images (provenance.cpp:26-58); collect_provider_image+loaded_provider_images_impl via dl_iterate_phdr (provider_images.cpp:114-153); compiled identity declarations (BikeProviderIdentity.cmake:66-139, CMakeLists.txt:239-284, 327-338). MUTATORS: driver->start() rebases started_ (realtime_driver.cpp:70, re-invoked inside the measured window at realtime_measurement.cpp:43); terminal_="wall_clock_cap" (realtime_driver.cpp:82-85); resolved->setup.runtime.strict=!options.benchmark (realtime_driver.cpp:150) — the sole benchmark-conditional mutation; monitor.observe sets first_failure, warning_pending=!strict (status.cpp:68-76); model_status.observe mutates counts/numerically_valid sticky (status.cpp:186-219); staged.monitor.strict gates the InvalidReferenceRun throw (accounting.cpp:792-794, 812); replace() rewrites library.path in the returned policy_json only (installed_provider.cpp:16-23, 75-77); annotate_report sets invocation.monitor_strict/benchmark_mode/limitations (realtime_options.cpp:78-89). CONSUMERS: measure_realtime_command attaches steady_state to measured runs only, aggregates all_measured=AND(measured_ok), exit 0/1/2 (realtime_command.cpp:56-108); build_report consumes valid_prefix/measured_ok into eligible->headless_rtf_threshold_met (realtime_report.cpp:71-108); numerics_provider_policy_json()/provider_images() consume current_provider_identity (provenance.cpp:63-77) but currently have no in-tree callers (binding face removed with T63); restore boundary validates state.runtime.monitor.strict==config.strict (runtime.cpp:584-585) and strict_error propagates through async publication (accounting.cpp:528-548). BREAKERS: factory() returning null throws before close (realtime_measurement.cpp:159-160); a throwing close() on the unwind path still counts as exactly one invocation but masks the original error; a frozen injected Clock with a zero-progress driver would not bound the loop (excluded by the stated monotonic-clock assumption); non-glibc Linux raises runtime_error inside loaded_provider_images_impl which propagates — no rescue (provider_images.cpp:147-152); bad_alloc escaping collect_provider_image propagates through the dl_iterate_phdr C frame -> terminate (flagged in docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-16-audit-fixes.md:169, item 7.8).

## 2. Sub-Clause Evidence Analysis
(a) measure_run (realtime_measurement.cpp:156-166): setup_start=clock() at :158 precedes factory() at :159, so factory sits inside setup_wall_s (setup_end at :39) and outside wall_clock_s. The measured window is measured_start(:42) -> driver.start()(:43) -> advance loop(:44-55) -> flush(:56-57) -> measured_end(:59); wall=elapsed(:60) covers exactly [start, advance*, flush]; export_run(:81) happens after measured_end and is timed separately as export_wall_s(:80-83); summary(:68), boundary(:61) and the execution_identity comparison(:70-77) are also outside. close() is invoked exactly once per constructed driver: catch(...){driver->close(); throw;} at :163 covers any unwind from measured() (including the pre-try driver.start() at :43 and non-std::exception types), :164 covers normal return; null factory throws at :160 with no close needed; ~NativeRideRuntime=default (runtime.cpp:1002) performs no second close and NativeRideRuntime::close() is idempotent anyway (runtime.cpp:992-996). Bounded liveness: settings_.max_steps=ceil(duration/dt) is validated finite and <int64_max (realtime_driver.cpp:45-48); each advance clamps target to max_steps (:86) and reason() reports "duration_reached" at :67 or outcome/crash at :66; the whole-run cap clock_()-started_ >= limits_.max_wall_clock_s (default 300 s, RunLimits validated positive run_limits.hpp:18-25,33-35) sets terminal_="wall_clock_cap" BEFORE accepting more physics (:82-85); each runtime_->advance call is itself bounded by compute_slice_s=8 ms (realtime_measurement.hpp:19) honored per interval in NativeRideRuntime::advance (runtime.cpp:792-798); advance exceptions are captured per chunk and exit the loop (realtime_measurement.cpp:48-55). Test-pinned: wall scope 2/5.5/1/3 s split (test_realtime_measurement.cpp:64-71), failure path still closes+exports (:135-140), real-driver wall cap excludes setup and blocks physics (test_measure_realtime.cpp:82-99). (b) --benchmark relaxes only runtime.strict: parse_options sets only out.benchmark (realtime_options.cpp:49-52); resolve_measurement_ride projects identical arguments in both modes and mutates exactly resolved->setup.runtime.strict=!options.benchmark (realtime_driver.cpp:140-151; record_retention=off at :151 is unconditional). Semantically strict gates only whether flush_local throws InvalidReferenceRun (accounting.cpp:792-794, 812): monitor.observe and model_status.observe run identically in both modes (accounting.cpp:739, 763), and every monitor/reference violation is merged into model_status.counts via the attachment_violations channel (accounting.cpp:719 -> status.cpp:149-152) -> model_valid=false; energy quality failures stick numerically_valid=false (status.cpp:209-217). valid_prefix=errors.empty() && steps>0 && model_valid && numerically_valid && identity_unchanged (realtime_measurement.cpp:88, 111) never consults strict. steady_state_evidence maps any invalid prefix to status="diagnostic" via the invalid_prefix reason (realtime_report.cpp:117, 135-136), plus requires complete duration_reached prefix, identity, finite window/wall (:114-134). headless_rtf_threshold_met requires eligible=all runs valid_prefix AND all warmups valid_prefix_comparison AND protocol AND Release AND native (:78, 82-93, 96-104); any violation yields Value{} (null), never true. The gate test documents the intent: --benchmark is the monitor-non-strict throughput mode because strict ends at the first violation; the reported prefix "stays a throughput sample — never a valid-ride claim" (test_realtime_gate.cpp:14-19) and annotate_report stamps benchmark_mode+limitations (realtime_options.cpp:84-87). (c) Relocation rescue is Linux+BIKE_ROOT_INSTALL_NUMERICS only (provenance.cpp:10, 41; define gated at CMakeLists.txt:327-338). The "configure-time paths mismatched" gate lives at the CALLER: current_provider_identity first runs loaded_provider_images on the compiled required list and falls back ONLY on ProviderImageMismatch (provenance.cpp:44-57) — i.e., a required image absent from the loader table or unresolvable (provider_images.cpp:133-145); unavailable inspection and allocation failures propagate (:49-50). installed_provider_identity then requires backend==NUMPY_OPENBLAS with 64/64 BLAS/LAPACK bits (installed_provider.cpp:34-37), a libraries list covering required_images exactly once and order-equality original_name==required_images[index] (:38-40, :57) with absolute non-'.'/'..' NUL-free recorded paths, identity_kind=file and 64-hex sha256 (:57-59). The executable must be absolute, canonical(), regular and inside a directory literally named bin (:41-45); <prefix>/lib must exist as a non-symlink directory and is canonicalized (:46-49). Each candidate lib/<filename> must not be a symlink (:62-63), its canonical resolution must sit DIRECTLY under the canonical lib dir (rejects escapes and subdirectory residency, :64-66), must be a regular file (:67-68), and file_sha256 must equal the compiled policy digest (:69-70); duplicate resolved names are rejected (:72-73). Finally provenance.cpp:55 runs loaded_provider_images(installed.required_images) which via dl_iterate_phdr+canonical(dlpi_name) confirms the exact validated files are the in-process mapped images (provider_images.cpp:114-153) — the "necessary but insufficient" comment at :53-54. Non-glibc Linux throws runtime_error, which propagates rather than rescuing (:147-152). Factual nuance: installed_provider_identity alone does not test whether the original paths are missing — that "iff" is established only through current_provider_identity's catch scope; called directly it would accept any matching adjacent layout.

## 3. Exceptions, Dependencies & Consequences
Exceptions: elapsed() throws invalid_argument on non-monotonic/non-finite clock (realtime_measurement.cpp:15-20); per-chunk advance failures are captured then rethrown into the outer catch that records stage/type (InvalidReferenceRun vs RuntimeError) and exits the loop (:48-55); flush/identity/export failures are recorded as stage-tagged errors (:57-58, :72-77, :81-82), all of which force valid_prefix=false and reason=invalid_controller|simulation_error (:64-67). measure_run's catch-all guarantees close() then rethrows (:163). In the driver, input rejection precedes termination (realtime_driver.cpp:73-77) and the wall cap precedes physics (:82-86). Strict-mode consequences: InvalidReferenceRun thrown at accounting.cpp:812 aborts the ride -> advance-stage error -> measured_ok=false, valid_prefix=false, exit 1 for that run aggregate (realtime_command.cpp:63,108). Async accounting propagates strict_error after merge (accounting.cpp:528-548); bootstrap restore rejects a monitor.strict/config.strict disagreement (runtime.cpp:584-585). Provider path: ProviderImageMismatch is the sole trigger for relocation (provenance.cpp:48); all other exceptions propagate — preserving non-rescue. Dependencies: monotonic clock injection (Clock seam, realtime_runner.hpp:21-24), RunLimits positivity validation, __GLIBC__ dl_iterate_phdr availability, the compiled policy/header pair generated by file(GENERATE) (CMakeLists.txt:282-285), platform::executable_path via /proc/self/exe on Linux (platform/provenance.cpp:64-77). Consequences/residuals: (i) numerics_provider_policy_json()/provider_images() have zero in-tree callers — the rescue path is dead public API inside this checkout, exercised only by test_installed_provider and the configure probe pattern; (ii) collect_provider_image can throw bad_alloc through the C callback (terminate) — audit item 7.8; (iii) no bound exists if an injected clock stalls while the driver makes zero progress — outside the stated assumptions; (iv) headless_rtf_threshold_met is either true or null — never false — by design; (v) the gate's exit code is AND(measured_ok), not valid_prefix, so a violating benchmark run still exits 0 — intended per test_realtime_gate.cpp:14-19 and docs decision 'speed checks don't check pull' (simulation-decisions.md:367).

## 4. Test Coverage & Gaps
Covered: exact wall/scope boundaries and close-on-failure via deterministic FixtureDriver (test_realtime_measurement.cpp:15-71, 135-140); comparability-key invalidation incl. strict (:73-82); threshold requires 3 valid runs+warmup+Release+native (:100-109); benchmark relaxes only the monitor and marks the report (:121-134); real-driver wall cap excludes setup and blocks further physics (test_measure_realtime.cpp:82-99); end-to-end schema-1 benchmark report incl. empty-retention export (:25-59); steady_state diagnostic classification matrix incl. invalid_prefix, non_duration_terminal, invalid_wall_clock, incomplete prefix, identity_changed (test_performance_evidence.cpp:33-89); installed provider acceptance after move, digest/role/ABI preservation, and rejection of empty/mismatched required, bad hash, relative executable, modified bytes, same-name-elsewhere, directory-as-provider, symlink candidate, symlinked lib dir (test_installed_provider.cpp:84-136, Linux-only registration CMakeLists.txt:610-618); the slow gate runs the shipped artifact --benchmark and asserts measured_ok+steps+RTF while first_failure is logged as evidence (test_realtime_gate.cpp:50-126). Gaps: (1) no test exercises the catch-all unwind path (measure_run:163) — all fixture failures are caught inside measured() so close-on-unwind via e.g. throwing start()/clock()/boundary() is unverified; (2) no end-to-end test that a benchmark run WITH a model/monitor violation reports valid_prefix=false/steady_state=diagnostic/headless_rtf=null — the chain is pinned only at unit level (formula :111, steady_state reasons, eligible(:93)); (3) no test for multiple libraries, wrong backend/bits values, or the resolved.parent_path()!=canonical_library_dir escape branch in installed_provider; (4) no test of current_provider_identity's mismatch-gated fallback itself (Linux glibc runtime) — the dl_iterate_phdr confirmation is untested at integration level; (5) no test that summary()/execution_identity()/boundary() post-flush calls stay outside wall_clock_s beyond the fixture clock accounting.

## 5. Recommended Refinement
Split P12 into three independently checkable properties (P12a measure_run protocol, P12b benchmark isolation, P12c provider non-rescue) since the mechanisms share no code. For (a), state the bound quantitatively — termination <= max_wall_clock_s + one compute_slice_s + flush/export, and specify 'exactly once' applies only when factory() returns non-null; also name the assumption that per-advance progress or outcomes bound the loop when the injected clock is real. For (b), cite the load-bearing merge that makes monitor violations poison model_valid even in non-strict mode (accounting.cpp:719 -> status.cpp:149-152) — the property is only true because attachment/effort violations feed model_status.counts; make that an explicit clause ('any reference-monitor violation is also a model-status violation'). For (c), move the 'configure-time paths mismatched' condition out of the function contract into the caller clause (provenance.cpp:46-57) and name the public entry points that are currently uncalled in-tree; add tests: throwing start()->exactly-one close on unwind, an injected violating run proving valid_prefix/steady_state/headless_rtf suppression in benchmark mode, multi-library + wrong-backend + escaping-symlink installed_provider fixtures, and a glibc integration test that a correctly-hashed but unmapped file fails loaded_provider_images. Fix the flagged bad_alloc-through-dl_iterate_phdr terminate hazard (audit item 7.8) by making collect_provider_image noexcept-tolerant (reserve/capture error_code).

# Stage 3 (`R1`) Independent Research Report — `P13`: `SetupOwner` Multi-Stage Bootstrap Lifecycle, Setup-Only Crank Hold & Equilibrium Cache Atomicity

- **Inspected Commit:** `0502660dd14bd1021b3da90a61e20dcb1d5805a1`
- **Compliance Verdict (`R1`):** `partially_violated`
- **Product Justification Verdict (`R1`):** `relevant_engineering_contract`

## 1. Producers, Mutators, Consumers & Potential Breakers
PRODUCERS/WRITERS OF LIFECYCLE STATE: SetupOwner::Impl carries the FSM flags — result:152, settled_state_file:160, sealed_model_sha256:161, research_startup:162, sealed:163 (native/src/initialization/setup_owner.cpp:145-164); context flags initializing=true/completed=false defaults at native/src/initialization/context.hpp:52-53. make_setup builds the impl and context (setup_owner.cpp:171-243); construction is private with only make_setup/settle as friends (setup_owner.hpp:49-69). The single transition function is initialization::settle(SetupOwner&, options) (setup_owner.cpp:250-281): guard 'state.sealed || state.result' -> throw at :252-253; transfer path via initial_state->prepare (:255) or solve via settle_context (:257); then clock resets (:267-270), settled_state_file capture (:271), finalize_initial_state (:274), optional prepare_research_startup (:275-276), initializing=false (:277), completed=true (:278), result store (:279). MUTATORS: context.equilibrium/context.completed written at equilibrium.cpp:96 (reset) and :141 (commit); context.completed also set inside finalize_initial_state at finalize.cpp:119 and context.initializing cleared only at setup_owner.cpp:277; physical.step_ mutated only by the runtime publish path (runtime/step.cpp:2259) and zeroed at finalize.cpp:66, and physical.initializing_ cleared at finalize.cpp:66 — during all of setup, physical.step()==0 holds because settle uses context.stepper.step() directly (equilibrium.cpp:111). GUARDS: require_initializing = 'initializing && physical.step()==0' (initial_pose.cpp:16-19), called by settle_context (equilibrium.cpp:84), initialize_pose (initial_pose.cpp:34), apply_initial_forces (settle_contacts.cpp:35), settle_contacts (:74), refine_equilibrium (refine.cpp:19), finalize_initial_state (finalize.cpp:63), restore_equilibrium_state (equilibrium_cache.cpp:252), PhysicalInitialState::restore/prepare (initial_state.cpp:171,202). capture_initial_state (:283-287), capture_state_file (:289-293), project_initial_stations (:295-299) check only !result||!completed; seal_bootstrap (:301-309) checks sealed (:302) and !result||!completed||initializing (:303-304); research_initial_state (:334-338) checks only the optional member. CONSUMERS: app::prepare (native/src/app/prepare.cpp:6-24) -> make_settled_owner -> seal_owner_into (:13-14, metadata() read AFTER seal at :14); research::prepare_environment_into (native/src/research/environment.cpp:62-69) reads research_initial_state then seals; app::make_ride (native/src/app/physical_session.cpp:4-9) consumes RuntimeBootstrap; cli::settle (native/src/cli/settle.cpp:39-50) settles then capture_state_file+write_state_file without sealing. CRANK HOLD CHAIN: crank_hold_torque (settle_contacts.cpp:24-32) is invoked only at :57 inside apply_initial_forces, gated by require_initializing (:35) plus rider_present() && articulated.pedal_attachment=="spindle" (:53); it reaches dynamics only via the optional external vector -> PhysicalStep::apply_forces (:60-62) -> accumulator 'external' -> d->qfrc_applied (runtime/step.cpp:1395-1396,1538-1540). Runtime paths always pass std::nullopt: advance_interval (runtime/runtime.cpp:712), probe_step_inputs (runtime/step.cpp:2962-2963), prepare_research_startup (research_startup.cpp:65). RESTORE/WIRE BOUNDARIES: refine.cpp place() (:62-84) restores StepState (:75), DriveSnapshot (:76), tire state (:77), rider state+probe (:78), full q0 incl. root_x (:80, root_x excluded from qids at :41), qvel=0 (:82) on every evaluation via residual() (:85-100); restore_equilibrium_state (equilibrium_cache.cpp:251-275) stages all validation before the single stepper.mutate commit (:267-274). CACHE: equilibrium_cache_path (:237-241), load_equilibrium_cache (:242), load_equilibrium_state (:243-247), save_equilibrium_cache (:249, advisory swallows non-bad_alloc), capture_state (:210-235), write_state_file atomic publish (state_file.cpp:59-74). BREAKERS (ways the claim can fail): post-Sealed calls to capture_initial_state/capture_state_file/project_initial_stations/research_initial_state/metadata succeed — no sealed check exists on these const readers (setup_owner.cpp:283-299,311-338) and prepare.cpp:14 depends on post-seal metadata(); failed settle leaves live Stepper material at the last trial pose (flags roll back, material does not) until a retry re-poses via initialize_pose (equilibrium.cpp:96); load_equilibrium_cache trusts the fingerprint-named filename + provider/model identity but never verifies the stored fingerprint_sha256 member that load_equilibrium_state checks (:244), and schema-0 files skip even the identity check (settle_metadata :54-58); auto-cache hits serve the recorded residual_qacc without re-measurement (equilibrium.cpp:103) unlike the explicit path which re-measures (:102).

## 2. Sub-Clause Evidence Analysis
(a) LIFECYCLE — partially holds. Forward progression is enforced: an owner is Unsettled at construction (context.hpp:52-53); settle() rejects a second call or post-seal call ('setup equilibrium can only be completed once', setup_owner.cpp:252-253) — verified at test_setup.cpp:83 and test_equilibrium_state_file.cpp:488-489; seal_bootstrap rejects pre-completion (setup_owner.cpp:303-304; test_pipeline.cpp:128) and a second seal (:302). The [ResearchStartupReady] window exists inside settle() between prepare_research_startup (:276) and initializing=false (:277), gated by context.completed set inside finalize (finalize.cpp:119) and stepper.time()==0 (research_startup.cpp:51-52). Failed settle rolls back flags to Unsettled: settle_context resets completed/equilibrium at equilibrium.cpp:96 and only commits at :141, so an EquilibriumFailure (:133) leaves result unset, completed=false, initializing=true — seal is then rejected by all three conditions at :303-304 (tested: test_setup.cpp:70, test_transfer.cpp:190,199) and settle() may be retried since state.result is unset. HOWEVER the claim's 'rejected after Sealed' conjunct fails for two of the three named methods: capture_initial_state (:284), capture_state_file (:290), project_initial_stations (:296), research_initial_state (:335) and metadata() contain NO sealed check — they are documented observational reads (the settled_state_file comment at setup_owner.cpp:258-266 states capture deliberately keeps serving the finalized context), and post-seal metadata() is required by prepare.cpp:14. Moreover research_initial_state is a const getter, not a mutation — the 'post-Sealed mutations (settle, research_initial_state, second seal_bootstrap) are rejected' clause is wrong on one of three members (settle:252 and seal:302 do reject; research_initial_state returns post-seal). (b) CRANK HOLD — holds. crank_hold_torque (settle_contacts.cpp:24-32) is reachable only through apply_initial_forces (:34-63): require_initializing (:35) enforces exactly context.initializing==true && context.physical.step()==0 (initial_pose.cpp:16-19 — the step==0 conjunct is explicit, not inferred; during setup step_ stays 0 because settle advances context.stepper directly at equilibrium.cpp:111 and step_ only commits in the runtime publish path), and the caller additionally requires rider_present() && pedal_attachment=="spindle" (:53) — strictly stronger than the claimed gate. prepare_research_startup evaluates forces with std::nullopt external (research_startup.cpp:65); every runtime path passes std::nullopt (runtime.cpp:712, step.cpp:2962-2963); the gains equilibrium_crank_hold_kp/kd are consumed only at settle_contacts.cpp:30-31. The comment at settle_contacts.cpp:15-23 documents the setup-only intent ('user ruling 2026-11-13'). (c) RESTORE — holds. Every trial evaluation runs place(x) (refine.cpp:88) which restores the incoming PhysicalStep state (:75), DriveSnapshot (:76), tire names/state (:77), rider contacts state+probe (:78), copies the full q0 including root_x (:80, root_x excluded from the optimization basis at :41), and zeroes qvel (:82); tire/rider candidate values ride in x and are patched into the restored baseline (:69-74), so no rejected candidate's material leaks into the next trial (comment :63-66). On optimizer exit place(best) re-installs the best evaluated candidate (:114) even on non-engine exceptions (:106-115), while engine::EngineFailure rethrows immediately (:110) per the documented poison contract. restore_equilibrium_state (equilibrium_cache.cpp:251-275) validates settle metadata (:254), qpos shape (:255), all material codecs via decode_material (:256, :81-147), integration time (:258-260) and stages nonnegative-checked model rows (:264-265) before install_material (:266) and a single stepper.mutate commit of model rows, integration state, qpos, qvel=0 and time=0 (:267-274); the comment :261-263 states 'All shape/finite/codec checks occur before any mutation'. install_material (:149-162) keeps throwing stages (prepare_state, validate_state, drive restore validation) ahead of commits. (d) BOUNDS & CACHE ATOMICITY — holds. settle_context terminates inside explicit bounds: budget = explicit max_steps>0 or max(40000,round(80s/dt)) (equilibrium.cpp:76-80), cycle = max(1,round(.02/dt)) (:81), overflow/non-finite guards (:19-28), outer while steps<budget (:120), kinetic segments bounded by min/max segment (:40,:48-49,:59), refinement at most 8 attempts x refine_equilibrium(...,80,...) (:130) each bounded by max_evaluations (refine.cpp:103,108-109), failure reported as EquilibriumFailure with steps/residual/worst-qacc (:71-74,:133). fit_sag is bounded by max_evaluations=40 (sag_fit.cpp:28-29) with validated finite domains (:13-21) and acceptance re-check (:30-32); each evaluator call is itself a bounded make_setup+settle (sag_evaluator.cpp:9-19). write_state_file bounds its temp-name retry at 64 attempts (state_file.cpp:68-70), then write+fsync+close+rename+dirsync (:72) with temp cleanup on failure (:73) and leaf-symlink rejection (:65-66). Post-seal cache writes are unreachable: save_equilibrium_cache exists only at equilibrium.cpp:140 inside settle_context (init-gated), and settle() throws once sealed or completed (setup_owner.cpp:252); runtime reset replays the sealed bootstrap (docs/native-startup.md:315-316) — proven by test_post_episode_lifecycle_writes_no_cache_entry (test_equilibrium_state_file.cpp:474-508) which advances, resets twice and asserts byte-identical single canonical entry.

## 3. Exceptions, Dependencies & Consequences
EXCEPTION MODEL: domain violations throw std::invalid_argument (field-path-identified, shared exception class per docs/agents/native-engineering.md:36); unconverged solve throws EquilibriumFailure carrying steps/residual_qacc/worst_accelerations (equilibrium.cpp:73-74,:133, equilibrium.hpp); engine::EngineFailure is propagated unrolled everywhere (equilibrium.cpp:105, equilibrium_cache.cpp:245, refine.cpp:110) under the deliberate Stepper poison contract (comment equilibrium_cache.cpp:261-263); std::bad_alloc likewise propagates from advisory paths (equilibrium_cache.cpp:242,245,249). DEPENDENCIES: physical.step()==0 during setup is structural — step_ increments only in the runtime publish tail (step.cpp:2259) and finalize sets research_accounting_valid_ only at finalize.cpp:119, so advance_physics cannot run inside setup; require_initializing's step!=0 arm is defense-in-depth for test rigs that advance a live context (e.g. test_sag.cpp:587). Assumption timestep_s>0 is enforced at equilibrium.cpp:77. CONSEQUENCES/NUANCES: (1) failed settle rolls back FSM flags but not live material — the Stepper retains the last trial pose until a retry re-poses it via initialize_pose (equilibrium.cpp:96); the owner is inspectable but unsealable (equilibrium.hpp contract). (2) Cache-load asymmetry: the derived-name path (load_equilibrium_cache, equilibrium_cache.cpp:242) never verifies the stored fingerprint_sha256 member that the explicit path enforces (:244), skips provider/model identity for schema-0 files (:54-58), and serves the recorded residual without re-measurement (equilibrium.cpp:103) — trust rests on the canonical filename and, for schema 1, metadata identity; a planted file at the right name with matching provider/model identity would be served. (3) Advisory cache write failures are swallowed (equilibrium_cache.cpp:248-249) — a settle can succeed while leaving no cache entry; deliberate. (4) write_state_file's leaf check is check-then-act (symlink_status then open_regular, state_file.cpp:65-66) — a narrow TOCTOU window remains for a hostile same-directory writer. (5) PhysicalInitialState::prepare assigns context.equilibrium before restore (initial_state.cpp:~209); a restore failure mid-way leaves equilibrium set with completed=false — seal still rejected via !impl_->result (setup_owner.cpp:303). (6) finalize.cpp:85 applies initial forces (incl. crank hold) during finalize — still inside the initializing window, consistent with (b). (7) capture_initial_state captures BEFORE finalize at setup_owner.cpp:271 to preserve tire hysteresis (comment :153-160,:258-266) — a deliberate two-snapshot design.

## 4. Test Coverage & Gaps
COVERED: budgets/domain validation — test_setup.cpp:22-48 (timestep-derived and explicit budgets, invalid max_steps/dt/tolerance) and test_equilibrium_state_file.cpp:434-461 (options reach the solve); failure->unsettled->unsealable — test_setup.cpp:50-84 (EquilibriumFailure fields, seal rejection :70, capture allowed :79-81, post-seal settle rejection :83); seal-before-settle — test_pipeline.cpp:128; settle-once post-seal — test_equilibrium_state_file.cpp:488-489; post-episode cache quiet — test_equilibrium_state_file.cpp:474-508 (settle+seal+advance+reset+advance+reset, byte-identical canonical entry); cache/state-file round trip and validation — test_transfer.cpp:167-200 (miss->hit, explicit restore, corrupted qpos -> settle+seal rejected :189-190, timestep transfer, incompatible transfer rejected :198-199) and test_equilibrium_state_file.cpp:170-508 (round trip, explicit-over-auto precedence :202-236, fingerprint mismatch :243-261, payload-doesn't-fit :263-278, residual re-measurement :284-313, missing file :318-326, field validation :335-361); durable atomic publication — test_transfer.cpp:127-147 (round trip, invalid write leaves prior bytes, leaf symlink rejected); crank hold — test_setup.cpp:86-104 (phase retained), test_launch_preparation.cpp:284-300 (table angle moves settled phase), :505-517 (held within 5 mrad both directions), :522-552 (kp/kd bound into fingerprint and transfer contract), :562-590 (held on spindle profiles, free on lumped/flat); refine restore — test_sag.cpp:200-220 (root_x held at datum :213-214, exact material equality incl. drive/tire/rider/controller :218, no completed equilibrium published :215-216); fit_sag — test_sag.cpp:267-280; reset-replays-bootstrap — test_pipeline.cpp:139-146 (t=0 restore, generation bump, no leaked material). GAPS: no test invokes a second seal_bootstrap (the sealed guard at setup_owner.cpp:302 is unexercised, though native-startup.md:345 documents it); no test calls capture_initial_state/capture_state_file/project_initial_stations/research_initial_state before settle or after seal — post-seal they succeed by design, which would contradict a literal reading of claim (a); no test retries settle() after EquilibriumFailure (allowed by setup_owner.cpp:252, untested); project_initial_stations (setup_owner.cpp:295-299) has no direct native test; the physical.step()!=0 arm of require_initializing (initial_pose.cpp:17) is untested; no explicit assertion that the crank-hold external channel is absent during runtime episodes — it is implicit in free-crank launch/pedaling tests; write_state_file's 64-attempt exhaustion and the load_equilibrium_cache fingerprint-member asymmetry are untested; tests were read as source — none executed in this audit.

## 5. Recommended Refinement
Restate (a) to match the implemented contract: mutating transitions are single-shot and seal-confined — settle() completes once (setup_owner.cpp:252) and seal_bootstrap() succeeds exactly once after a completed finalized settle (:301-309) — while the observational readers capture_initial_state (:283-287), capture_state_file (:289-293), project_initial_stations (:295-299), research_initial_state (:334-338) and metadata() (:311-332) are legal from SettledFinalized onward including after Sealed (required by app::prepare, prepare.cpp:13-14); research_initial_state is a read, not a mutation. Add 'physical.rider_present()' to (b)'s gate (settle_contacts.cpp:53). For (d), tighten the auto-cache load to mirror the explicit path: verify the stored fingerprint_sha256 member inside load_equilibrium_cache (equilibrium_cache.cpp:242) and consider re-measuring residual on cache hits as the explicit path does (equilibrium.cpp:102-103). Suggested new tests: second seal_bootstrap rejection; capture/research readers before settle and after seal (pinning the intended observational semantics); settle() retry after EquilibriumFailure; require_initializing rejection on a context with step()!=0; an auto-cache file with valid name/metadata but corrupted stored fingerprint or inflated recorded residual; write_state_file retry-exhaustion.
