# Stage 3 (`R1`) Independent Research Report — `P14`: `PolicySession` Single-Act-Per-Window, Non-Wedging Queued Operator Drain & Post-Terminal Reset Journaling

- **Inspected Commit:** `0502660dd14bd1021b3da90a61e20dcb1d5805a1`
- **Compliance Verdict (`R1`):** `partially_violated`
- **Product Justification Verdict (`R1`):** `mixed_subclauses`

## 1. Producers, Mutators, Consumers & Potential Breakers
PRODUCERS/MUTATORS — session-level FSM state lives in PolicySession::Impl (policy_session.cpp:27-46): `queued` is produced by `request()` (148-153) from request_stop/pause/resume/set_brakes/reset (261-269) when `pending()` is true; `staged` is produced in `begin_advance` (206) after the single `policy->act`/`rider_behavior->act`+validation block (194-208) and cleared on `begin_control` success (220), on `apply` of stop (140) and in `reset_now` (133); `completed`/`completed_acknowledged` are produced in `advance_pending` (232-237); journal rows are produced by `record()` (86-108) which is invoked at request time (150) and at apply time (146); `events` is cleared per episode in `reset_now` (132); `paused`/`front`/`rear`/`intervention`/`brake_events` mutate in `apply` (141-143) and `begin_advance` (221-222). Runtime-level FSM state (produced/mutated in runtime/research.cpp): `e.window` latched in `begin_control` (172-197, window at 196), `e.delivery_pending`+`e.pending_transition` in `finish_window` (293-323), cleared in `acknowledge_control` (396-406, with `stop_requested`->`operator_stop` at 404) or `handle_failure` (324-340); `stop()` early-returns on `done()` (657-659); `fail_policy` refuses inside a window (663) else latches `terminated`+`policy_error` (664-667). PlaybackClock state (`debt_s_`/`wall_s_`/`step_`/`scale_`/`paused_`) is produced in lifecycle.cpp:263-292. CONSUMERS — `pending()` (policy_session.cpp:52-56,177: `completed` OR status `active`/`delivery_pending`) gates `request()` (149), `begin_advance` (185), `advance_pending` (229), `reset` queue clear (267), `close` guard (308), `drain_active` (lifecycle.cpp:43-46), `run_until` (256-259), the headless loop (cli/research.cpp:117-118) and all viewer hooks (viewer/research.cpp:11-20). `operator_events()` (300) is consumed by `ResearchRun::seal` (lifecycle.cpp:171-174) into `prepared_events_`, stable-sorted by `received_wall_s` (183), re-stamped with `episode_id` (196), committed to `events_` (194-198) and published by `ResearchRecording::publish` where `applied` maps to `applied_step` (research_publication.cpp:29-40). `seal_episode`/`run_metadata` (270-299) -> `EpisodeReceipt` -> `summarize`. `done()`/`paused()` (178-179) feed `run_until`, viewer `terminal`, and CLI terminal checks. BREAKERS/GUARDS — `close()` throws on `pending()` (308); `fail_policy` throws inside a window (research.cpp:663); `handle_failure` clears window+delivery_pending so a physics throw never wedges `pending()` (338-339); `reset()` clears a stale queue only when `!pending()` (267); the ctor restores caller env ownership on `start_policy` failure (170-174, comment 22-23); `ResearchRun::reset` rejects pending resets upfront (lifecycle.cpp:112) and `before_reset` rejects post-finalization resets unless inside finish's drain (87); `advance_pending` acknowledges exactly once via `completed_acknowledged` (234-237; second ack would throw research.cpp:400). RESTORE/WIRE BOUNDARIES — `set_reset_hooks` (302-304) wires `ResearchRun::before_reset` (seal outgoing episode, lifecycle.cpp:86-91) and `new_episode` (bump id, journal `episode_started{reset:true}`, 92-95) around `environment->reset` (policy_session.cpp:126-134); `ResearchPublication::publish` is the final owned-evidence boundary.

## 2. Sub-Clause Evidence Analysis
(a) HOLDS as stated. `Policy::act` and optional `RiderBehavior::act` are invoked only inside `begin_advance`'s non-staged branch (policy_session.cpp:200-203); a `pending()` guard rejects a second window (185), `advance_pending` never touches the policy (225-243), and a `staged` command survives a `begin_control` failure so a retry skips `act` entirely (190-193, 206, intent comment 217-218) — at most one act per control window across budget slices and staged retries. An `act` throw or a `validate_motor_policy_control` rejection of rider-owned fields (`human_torque_nm`/`posture`/`crank_target_rate_rad_s`/`!rider_enabled`, policy.cpp:21-25) is caught (207-208) and routed to `fail_policy`, which latches `reason == "policy_error"` when not already done (research.cpp:661-668); non-standard exceptions map identically. Caveats that do not break the clause: `RideControl` has no brake fields — brakes are session doubles validated by `brakes()` before `act` (20-23, 189), so 'forbidden brake fields' is vacuous; validation order runs `validate_motor_policy_control` before `rider_behavior->act` merges posture (202) which is the sanctioned channel; while a `staged` command exists `pending()` is false so a queued `set_brakes` applies immediately and a brake change can deadlock the staged retry behind 'retry a staged policy command with its original brakes' (191-192) — recoverable only via `request_stop` or matching explicit brakes. (b) PARTIALLY HOLDS, VIOLATED on the exception leg. Commands arriving while `pending()` are queued without mutating the in-flight window (`request()` reads `pending()` via `status()` and only `push_back`s, 148-153) and drain FIFO after the single acknowledge (235-238 vs 154-159) — consistent with docs/NATIVE_POLICIES.md:94. But the consume semantics the property requires do not exist: `apply_queued` executes `apply(queued.front()); queued.pop_front();` so a throw leaves the failing command at the front, and `advance_pending` only clears `completed`/`completed_acknowledged` after the drain returns (238-241), so `pending()` stays true forever (52-53) — `close()` throws 'finish the accepted policy interval before close' (308) and `drain_active` re-raises the same error every call (lifecycle.cpp:43-46, caught only as a secondary 'active control drain failed' at 219-221). Deterministic reachable throw sources in `apply`: `reset_now`'s reseed rejection on configs lacking `raw_initial` (policy_session.cpp:114-123 — the documented 'historical captured bootstrap' case, NATIVE_POLICIES.md:32, research.hpp:39); `start_policy` failure inside a queued reset (`policy->reset`/`metadata`, 73-85, 135 — a plugin ABI reset failure); the `before_reset` hook's 'cannot reset a finalized research launch' (lifecycle.cpp:87) when `session.reset()` is called directly while pending post-finalization (the `ResearchRun::reset` guard at 112 does not cover the public session API); `environment->status()`/`stop()`/`reset()` after an external `environment().close()` via the public accessor (180, research.cpp:166-167,654-656,624-625). Worse than a mere wedge: a partially executed `reset_now` (env reset at 127-128, `events.clear()` at 132, `after_reset` at 134, then `start_policy` fails at 135) is fully re-executed on every subsequent `advance_pending`, and with a `ResearchRun` attached each retry seals another `policy_error` episode receipt via `before_reset`; also `apply` records after acting (146), so a throwing `record` (status() or `events.push_back` allocation) retries an already-applied command — idempotent for pause/resume/brakes but a double-reset for `reset`. (c) VIOLATED as stated. `apply` computes `terminal_before` once before the switch (138) and records `operator_after_terminal`/`applied=false` whenever it was true (146). For a post-terminal reset the record lands in the NEW episode's journal (it follows `events.clear()` at 132), producing an internally inconsistent row `{kind:operator_after_terminal, terminal:false, applied:false}` — `terminal` is recomputed post-reset (88-89) while `kind`/`applied` come from pre-reset state — and the publication emits it with `applied_step=null` (research_publication.cpp:34-35), i.e. journaled as never applied although the new episode exists only because it applied. The same uniform rule is defensible for `stop` (early-returns on `done()`, research.cpp:657), `pause`, and `brakes`; `reset` is the one action whose success creates the successor state, so it needs a per-action or post-hoc `applied` evaluation. A queued reset applied at a boundary that turned terminal mid-window takes the same misclassification. (d) HOLDS in code but production-dead. `PlaybackClock::target` (lifecycle.cpp:278-292) floors debt at 0 after completed-step payment (283), floors elapsed at 0 (284), zeroes debt while paused (285), caps it at `max(.05*scale, timestep_s)` (286), derives `more = floor(debt/dt)` with an overflow guard (288-289), keeps `wall_s_` monotonic (290) and rejects step regression (281); `set_scale` gates {1,2,4,8} (271) and all of `rebase`/`set_scale`/`set_paused` discard stale debt (266-277). However the only in-repo callers are tests — the viewer's production pacer is the separate `viewer::Pacer` offer/completed model (pacer.cpp:34-59) driven by `run()` for every mode including research (ride.cpp:29,104,115,121) — so the clause is verified yet vacuous at the product level.

## 3. Exceptions, Dependencies & Consequences
Exception surface and its consequences: (1) `begin_advance`'s try block (194-209) routes act/validation failures to `mark_policy_failure` -> `fail_policy` (69-72) giving `policy_error`; `environment->begin_control` sits OUTSIDE that try (219) so native acceptance failures do not mislabel policy errors and leave `staged` intact for act-free retry. (2) `advance_pending`'s `environment->advance_control` may yield `nullopt` on budget/target (research.cpp:357-359, returned at policy_session.cpp:230-231) or throw after `handle_failure` clears `window`/`delivery_pending` (research.cpp:384-392, 338-339) — a physics failure therefore can never leave `pending()` stuck; the wedge exists ONLY through the operator-drain leg. (3) `apply` can throw from `status()` (48-51, via `done_status` at 138 or `record` at 88), `environment->stop()`/`reset()` (140,144), `reset_now`'s tape/metadata/hook/policy chain (110-135) or `record`'s `events.push_back` (104); none of it is consumed — the throw skips `pop_front` and the `completed` reset, so the session enters a permanently pending, unclosable state with retry side-effects (duplicate sealed episodes for reset-class failures). (4) `reset()`'s `queued.clear()` when `!pending()` (267) silently drops stale queued commands after a physics failure — their `operator_requested` journal rows survive with no applied counterpart, an audit-log gap. (5) `note_cleanup`/`mark_policy_failure` are advisory and never replace the primary exception (57-72); `FailureReport`/`close_environment` collect secondaries the same way (lifecycle.cpp:33-62). (6) `fail_policy` cannot run inside an active window (research.cpp:663) — safe here because `act` precedes `begin_control`, but a policy failure discovered while a window is in flight has no session-level handler. (7) Dependencies: ordering `request->queued->acknowledge->apply_queued->completed.reset` is load-bearing for every claim; the `completed_acknowledged` flag (38, 234-237) is what makes acknowledge exactly-once; `events.clear()` before `record` is what relocates the apply-time journal row into the successor episode; `ResearchRun::seal`'s unconditional append of `session_->operator_events()` (lifecycle.cpp:172-173) depends on that per-episode clear.

## 4. Test Coverage & Gaps
COVERED — act-once: `test_one_act_per_window_and_queued_pause_brakes` (test_native_research_policy.cpp:48-91), `test_one_act_per_window_across_budget_yields` (95-113), `test_partial_ticks_call_policy_once_and_stop_does_not_start_another` (test_research_frontend.cpp:16-51), `test_native_partial_ticks_latch_policy_until_external_boundary` (test_native_research_frontend.cpp:14-45), `boundary_and_retry` yields + target-step yield (test_lifecycle.cpp:112-121); rider-act once per boundary incl. across reseed (`test_custom_rider_callback_runs_once_per_external_boundary`, test_native_research_environment.cpp:176-218). Queue semantics: `test_stop_and_reset_are_deferred_to_external_boundary` (test_native_research_policy.cpp:144-181) and `queued_reset` (test_lifecycle.cpp:135-151) pin FIFO application at the boundary, non-mutation of the in-flight window, and policy reseed counts; `test_interruption_drains_only_the_already_accepted_control` (test_research_capture.cpp:308-344) and `boundary_and_retry` pin `drain_active` draining only the accepted window. policy_error: `test_policy_failure_has_no_manufactured_transition` + `test_policy_cannot_smuggle_rider_inputs` (test_native_research_policy.cpp:203-252). Input rejection before queueing: `test_invalid_operator_values_do_not_call_policy` (184-200). Ownership restore: `reset_failure_receipt` (test_lifecycle.cpp:184-201). PlaybackClock: all seven oracle ports in test_playback_clock.cpp (budget-yield debt, cap+completed payment, fraction/regressing wall, pause/scale rebase discard, invalid timestep/scale/typing) plus `clock_contract` (test_lifecycle.cpp:91-106). GAPS — (1) NO test exercises a throwing `apply` during drain: nothing injects `policy->reset` failure inside a queued reset, a reseed on a `raw_initial`-less config, a `before_reset`-hook throw during a queued drain, or an externally-closed env; the (b) consume-and-clear requirement has zero coverage either way and the wedge is unobserved. (2) NO journal assertion exists for any post-terminal operator action: `queued_reset` resets mid-window (so `operator_applied` is produced), `test_status.cpp:272` resets post-stop but only checks seed metadata with no `ResearchRun` capturing the journal; neither `operator_after_terminal` (correct leg: post-terminal stop) nor the reset misclassification is pinned anywhere. (3) The staged `begin_control` retry is untested — no test makes `environment->begin_control` throw post-`act` to prove `act` is not re-called (only the code comment at policy_session.cpp:217-218 asserts intent). (4) `PlaybackClock` has no production consumer, so its coverage is real but the property is exercised only by tests; the production pacer (`viewer::Pacer`) has its own distinct debt model not covered by this property.

## 5. Recommended Refinement
Split P14 into four separately-verdicted properties, because the current bundle mixes two holding contracts with two real violations and one dead-code clause. P14a (holds — keep as-is): single `act` per accepted control window across budget/target yields and staged `begin_control` retries; add a regression that injects a `begin_control` failure (e.g. via a rig hook or allocation-failure seam) to cover the untested staged-retry leg. P14b (violated — restate and fix): 'a throwing `apply` during the post-acknowledge drain must consume the command and release the completed transition so `pending()` becomes false'. Minimal conforming change: copy `queued.front()` to a local and `pop_front()` BEFORE calling `apply` (journal the failed apply as a distinct kind such as `operator_apply_failed` with the original `applied=false`, or keep `operator_after_terminal`-style marking), and move `s.completed.reset()`/`completed_acknowledged=false` ahead of or into a scope-guard around `apply_queued()`; alternatively define an explicit fault state that clears `completed` while retaining diagnostics. Decide deliberately whether deterministic apply failures should also `mark_policy_failure`-style terminate the episode — currently they silently wedge. P14c (violated — restate and fix): 'a reset that successfully creates the successor episode is journaled `operator_applied`/`applied=true` in the new episode'. Evaluate `terminal_before`/applied per action (reset's applied flag should reflect reset success, not predecessor terminal state) or record the reset row after `reset_now` with post-state truth; add tests pinning kind/applied for post-terminal reset AND for post-terminal stop (the leg where `applied=false` is genuinely correct). P14d (holds but vacuous): either wire `PlaybackClock` into the research viewer pacing path (replacing or unifying with `viewer::Pacer`) so the bound is production-relevant, or explicitly mark it parity-dead code and drop it from the property's evidence expectations; keep the existing seven-test suite as the behavioral contract either way.

# Stage 3 (`R1`) Independent Research Report — `P15`: `ResearchRun` Multi-Episode Finalization, `PluginPolicy` ABI v1 RAII Order, `TractionControl` & `PhysicalLiveCsv` Schema Safety

- **Inspected Commit:** `0502660dd14bd1021b3da90a61e20dcb1d5805a1`
- **Compliance Verdict (`R1`):** `partially_violated`
- **Product Justification Verdict (`R1`):** `mixed_subclauses`

## 1. Producers, Mutators, Consumers & Potential Breakers
PRODUCERS/MUTATORS. (a) ResearchRun state is produced by the two borrowing ctors (lifecycle.cpp:64-76) which latch session_/environment_ and install PolicySession reset hooks (lifecycle.cpp:69; detached in ~ResearchRun lifecycle.cpp:77). Mutators: ResearchRun::reset (lifecycle.cpp:110-140), seal (lifecycle.cpp:141-201), finish (lifecycle.cpp:202-261), before_reset/new_episode (lifecycle.cpp:86-95). Session-side plant resets all funnel through PolicySession::Impl::reset_now (policy_session.cpp:109-136) which invokes before_reset at :126 BEFORE environment->reset at :127-128 and after_reset at :134; callers are PolicySession::reset (:265-269) -> request (:148-153) -> apply (:137-147) or apply_queued (:154-159) during advance_pending (:225-243). Env-side resets go only through ResearchRun::reset -> NativeResearchRuntime::reset/reset_unlocked (research.cpp:621-653); no internal self-reset exists (research.cpp:338-403 resets only the optional window). (b) PluginPolicy instance lifecycle produced by Library/dlopen + descriptor() (plugin_policy.cpp:18-50), validate_policy_abi (policy.cpp:37-45), create via invoke (plugin_policy.cpp:96-98); mutators Instance::close (:63-69), ~Instance (:54-57), PluginPolicy::reset/act/close/require_open (:102-127); member declaration order library_ (:130) then instance_ (:131) fixes destruction order instance-before-library. (c) Trajectory producers: TractionControlPolicy::act (builtin_policies.cpp:61-89) mutating cap_nm_/last_time_/standstill_s_ (policy.hpp:59-62) and example act (traction_control.cpp:49-93) mutating State{cap_nm,last_time_s,standstill_s,has_cap,has_time} (:11-14); consumers PolicySession::begin_advance (policy_session.cpp:194-208) -> validate_motor_policy_control (policy.cpp:21-25) and PluginPolicy::act -> policy_control_from_abi (policy.cpp:46-71). (d) PhysicalLiveCsv schema produced in emit (preview_csv.cpp:71-97) from first emitted row keys; mutators write (:98-115), write_marker (:116-120), close (:121-135), fields columns_/generation_/next_time_s_/rows_since_flush_/closed_/failed_ (preview_csv.hpp:34-39); consumers cli/ride.cpp:132-135,157-163 (due-gated writes, terminal write+marker), viz::load_ride_csv (ride.cpp:175). CONSUMERS: cli::run_research_loop/research_finish (cli/research.cpp:68-138) drive reset hooks (viewer research.cpp:20; env-bound launch.reset() at cli/research.cpp:107-110) and finish(stop=true); validation/benchmark.cpp:118-132,164 consumes ResearchRun+finish. BREAKERS: publication_->publish throw (lifecycle.cpp:245-253, path_ unset -> retryable), drain_active throw (:219-220 recorded into failures_), post-finalization reset rejection (:87, thrown inside apply_queued leaving the request queued forever = fail-closed poisoned queue), pending-window reset rejection (:112,119-121), invoke() error-buffer/status violations (plugin_policy.cpp:71-82), hash re-check (:100), destroy throw wrapped PolicyFailure (:66-68) or swallowed in ~Instance (:55-56), stream failure -> failed_ latch (preview_csv.cpp:95) + require_open (:63-66), marker-first schema lock (the (d) finding).

## 2. Sub-Clause Evidence Analysis
(a) HOLDS. Seal-before-reset: session-bound runs seal via the reset hook installed at lifecycle.cpp:69 -> before_reset (:86-91) -> seal() invoked at policy_session.cpp:126 strictly before environment->reset (:127-128); env-bound runs seal via ResearchRun::reset -> before_reset() at lifecycle.cpp:136 before environment_->reset (:137-138), then new_episode() (:139). Every session reset path (immediate or queued apply/apply_queued, policy_session.cpp:137-159) transits reset_now -> hooks; no plant-reset path bypasses sealing (research.cpp:621-653 reachable only through those surfaces). Post-finalization rejection: lifecycle.cpp:87 `finalization_attempted_ && !draining_` throws before sealing; draining_ is set only around drain_active inside the FIRST finish call (:217-222), so resets already queued in PolicySession::Impl::queued and applied via apply_queued->apply->reset_now during that drain are permitted (seal + new_episode both run); a repeated finish(stop=true) skips drain entirely because `first` is false (:205,217). finish() idempotency: persistent runs memoize path_ (:203 early return; :245 assignment only after publish succeeds); non-persistent runs never set path_, but the re-executed body is an effective no-op (event() :79 and seal() :142 both early-return on !publication_, final_reason_ latched :224-231, annotate_failure has no receipts, failure_event_recorded_ guard :234) so repeat calls return the same nullopt without new side effects. Failed publications stay retryable because path_ is unset and seal() dedupes by episode_id (:144-145) returning the already-committed receipt. Matches doc NATIVE_POLICIES.md:109. (b) HOLDS. Member order library_ (:130) then instance_ (:131) plus ~Instance calling api->destroy (:54-57) guarantees destroy strictly before ~Library dlclose (:27); comment :130 states the intent. close() exchanges value->nullptr BEFORE api->destroy (:63-65) so a throwing destroy still leaves nothing for ~Instance -> at most once. create-failure cleanup: invoke("create",:96-98) may leave a non-null *instance while returning nonzero -> throw -> instance_ dtor destroys it before dlclose; null-on-success throws at :99 (nothing to destroy); post-create binary hash re-check :100 throws -> dtor path again; validate_policy_abi (:95)/descriptor() failures leave value null (nothing to destroy). destroy throw surfaces as PolicyFailure in close() (:66-68) and is deliberately swallowed in ~Instance (:55-56). Matches doc NATIVE_POLICIES.md:84 exactly once/cleanup-on-failed-create contract. (c) HOLDS on default parameters at source level. Constants identical: plugin .352/.372/.2/200./25./8./.3/.8 (traction_control.cpp:74-89) == TractionControlConfig defaults rear=.352 front=.372 slip=.2 cut=200 recover=25 min_limit=8 standstill=.3/.8 (policy.hpp:37-42; NATIVE_POLICIES.md:49-58). Same op order: elapsed=max(0,t-last) with last_time updated BEFORE the valid gate in both (builtin:67-69 vs plugin:71-73); slip=rear*v5-front*v4 (builtin:70 vs plugin:74); ceiling=max(0,demand-or-v7) equivalent since builtin control.motor_torque_nm=demand and motor_limit unset without a base (builtin:65,71 vs plugin:75); identical cap activate/cut/recover/clamp/release sequence (builtin:72-82 vs plugin:76-85); standstill |v5|<.3 accumulates only while motor torque present, releases torque-only at .8 (builtin:83-86 vs plugin:86-89). Divergent terms (builtin ceiling prefers control.motor_limit_nm, standstill tests control.motor_torque_nm) are reachable only with a configured base policy -> excluded by 'default parameters'; the plugin's create hard-gates config to {} (traction_control.cpp:35-36) so only defaults are reachable through it. Both sides compile under enforced -ffp-contract=off and a FATAL_ERROR fast-math ban (BikeCompilerPolicy.cmake:453-460,1285-1293) applied via bike_apply_native_policy to bike_app (contains builtin_policies.cpp, NativePhysicsResearch.cmake:44, policy applied NativeStartup.cmake:100) and to bike_traction_policy (NativePhysicsResearch.cmake:59-60 -> bike_startup_target_policy -> bike_apply_native_policy, NativeStartup.cmake:6-10), so identical expression trees yield identical IEEE-754 results. Caveat: the builtin emits runtime::RideControl while the plugin emits BikeControlV1 — bit-identity holds after host conversion via policy_control_from_abi (policy.cpp:46-71); presence bits map onto the same optionals, rider_enabled defaults true (intent.hpp:85) matching rider_enabled=1 (traction_control.cpp:65; host also pre-seeds output at plugin_policy.cpp:111-112), and use_saddle is only read when POSTURE present (neither sets it). (d) VIOLATED as a class invariant (latent). emit() derives columns_ from the FIRST emitted row's keys regardless of source (preview_csv.cpp:73-80), writes the header once when columns_ was empty (:87), and commits columns_ on success (:96). write_marker() (:116-120) emits a 2-key {generation,event} row, so a marker-first call permanently locks the schema to {"generation","event"}: the header is already out, every later write() row is projected onto those two columns and all telemetry fields are silently dropped; generation is also emitted as empty-string vs later int64 rows. write() can never repair it because columns_ is non-empty. The claimed 'locks schema from the first scalar write() row' does not hold — the lock attaches to the first emit of any kind.

## 3. Exceptions, Dependencies & Consequences
(a) draining_ exists only inside the first finish(...,stop=true)'s drain_active (lifecycle.cpp:217-222) and is manually cleared after the catch (:221); a drain failure is recorded into failures_ yet seal+publish still proceed and path_ is memoized, so the first finish throws the drain error while later calls return the published path (throw-once-then-succeed nuance). A rejected post-finalization queued reset stays at the head of PolicySession::Impl::queued (pop_front at :157 is never reached when before_reset throws inside apply_queued) — every subsequent advance_pending re-throws: fail-closed poisoned-queue semantics, plus an operator_requested event is still journaled (policy_session.cpp:150) even though the apply never happens. Assumption check: env-bound runs cannot intercept direct environment->reset() calls — the hook mechanism exists only on PolicySession, so ResearchRun::reset is the only sealed env-side surface; within the documented owner-routed contract this is consistent. (b) Consequences hinge on two compile-time facts: member destruction order (instance_ before library_) and close() nulling value before destroy — both compiler-enforced, not runtime-tested; a plugin destroy that throws inside ~Instance is silently dropped (intended nonthrowing-dtor policy, doc NATIVE_POLICIES.md:84). (c) Bit-identity additionally requires the same FP environment on BOTH sides — enforced for the in-repo example and host targets, but the installed ABI header (policy_v1.h; install rule NativePhysicsResearch.cmake:65) permits third-party modules built with contraction enabled, where ulp differences could appear in the identical mul+sub expressions; the contract scope is 'compatible native platform/toolchain' (NATIVE_POLICIES.md:64) so this is within bounds. Non-default configs diverge legitimately (builtin configurable, example fixed). (d) Marker-first produces silent total telemetry loss plus a mixed-type generation column; no exception, note, or guard tells the caller. Reachable only through an ordering the class never forbids; in-tree producers (cli/ride.cpp:157-162 write-then-marker; tests) always write first because the first write() is always due (generation_ nullopt != gen at preview_csv.cpp:69), so the violation is latent, not exercised. The filesystem-stream assumption holds: failed_ latches stream errors (:90-95) and require_open fails closed (:63-66); close() is idempotent and reports the first flush failure (:121-135).

## 4. Test Coverage & Gaps
COVERED: (a) test_lifecycle.cpp boundary_and_retry (:107-134 — failed publish retained receipt, reset rejected post-finalization :126, retry publishes calls==2, third finish idempotent no-reseal :129-133), queued_reset (:135-151 — queued reset applied after exactly one interval, reseed entropy kept), loader_failure_receipt (:152-170 — zero-step receipt, primary preserved across publication failure, retry allowed), interrupt_child (:227-238 — deferred SIGINT, finish idempotent after interrupt), reset_failure_receipt (:184-201 — ctor policy-reset failure restores env ownership, publishes zero-step evidence); test_native_recording_lifecycle.cpp:437-467 seal dedupe ('second terminal notice keeps one receipt', publish calls==1); test_research_recording_lifecycle.cpp:215-318 — interrupt-during-seal, deferred interrupt in finalize, off-main-thread finish publishes. (b) test_policy.cpp:101-125 dynamic_boundary — create_fail, reset_throw, act_throw, bad_number/bad_size/bad_error, destroy_throw + second close() is a no-op (:115-117 'pointer was consumed before invoking destroy'), closed-policy act rejection (:124); fixture policy_fixture.cpp deliberately allocates *out before returning create failure (:38-41) exercising the failed-create cleanup path. (c) builtin_trace test_policy.cpp:37-58 — slip cap integration, fixed-rate recovery, invalid-observation elapsed advance without cap touch, full-recovery disengage, standstill release/restore. (d) preview_contract test_lifecycle.cpp:171-183 — header from first write row, reset event, trailing marker, closed-marker rejection; test_frontend_windows.cpp:103-110 — write->marker ordering with reset event row. GAPS: (a) no test calls the env-bound ResearchRun::reset path for multi-episode sealing; the 'queued reset drained inside finish(stop=true)' draining_ exception is never exercised (queued_reset drains during normal advance, boundary finishes after manual drain); non-persistent finish() idempotency (publication_==nullptr) untested; the poisoned-queue aftermath of a rejected queued reset unexamined. (b) the hash-verification failure path (plugin_policy.cpp:100) is untested (needs binary mutation mid-load); destroy-before-dlclose ordering is structural only — acceptable but never asserted at runtime. (c) no plugin-vs-builtin parity test exists: the example module is never compiled or loaded in tests (BIKE_BUILD_POLICY_EXAMPLES defaults OFF, NATIVE_POLICIES.md:88 'Neither the example nor the failure-fixture modules were compiled or loaded in this delivery'), and builtin_trace exercises NON-default parameters only — default-parameter bit-identity is source-proven, not test-proven. (d) marker-first ordering is never tested — the truncating path is completely unexercised; duplicate-column rejection (:77-79) and the failed_-stream latch are also untested.

## 5. Recommended Refinement
Refine (d) to match either enforcement or documentation: (1) enforce — make write_marker() throw std::invalid_argument when columns_ is still empty ('preview marker requires a prior scalar row'), preserving the current schema-locks-once semantics while failing closed instead of silently dropping telemetry; or (2) defer the schema: let write_marker() emit marker rows on a side channel / buffer until the first write() fixes telemetry columns; or (3) explicitly document write-before-marker ordering as a class contract and add a regression test for the marker-first sequence (current docs only say 'stable first-row columns', NATIVE_POLICIES.md:117, which both readings satisfy). Additional test gaps to close: a plugin-vs-builtin default-parameter trajectory parity test behind BIKE_BUILD_POLICY_EXAMPLES comparing policy_control_from_abi(plugin.act) against TractionControlPolicy::act field-wise; an env-bound ResearchRun::reset multi-episode seal test; a finish(stop=true)-with-queued-reset drain test proving the draining_ exception; a non-persistent finish() idempotency test; a PluginPolicy binary-mutation-mid-load test for the :100 hash re-check; and a documented decision on whether a rejected queued reset should poison the session queue (currently it re-throws on every advance_pending) or be dropped/journaled as rejected.

## 1. Producers, Mutators, Consumers & Potential Breakers
PRODUCERS. Golden producer golden::save uses exclusive create_directory + write_exclusive x3 (golden/episode.cpp:291-365; cli/recording_golden.cpp:94-101). Platform primitives (platform/files.cpp): open_exclusive O_EXCL|O_NOFOLLOW+fstat-regular (108-120), open_regular O_NOFOLLOW+fstat-regular (122-132), write_exclusive (177-185), create_directory mkdir EEXIST (187-193), link_new link() EEXIST (195-205), file_sha256 no-follow stream (207-223), sync_directory fsync (163-175). MUTATORS. RunCapture::event/add_episode only while collecting - require_collecting throws otherwise (capture.cpp:91-93). ResearchRun::event appends to events_ with no finalization guard (lifecycle.cpp:78-85); annotate_failure rewrites receipt metadata/summary on every finish call (96-109, invoked at 233). Inside publish(), nothing mutates destination after the manifest link (only fsync + staging-file removal, publish.cpp:97-106). The post-seal mutators are the CLI sidecar writes named above. CONSUMERS. read_manifest (reader.cpp:29-55): requires a regular parseable manifest.json, exact key set, sealed==true, database=='run.duckdb', UUID regex, report_status complete|failed, file_sha256 covering exactly {run.duckdb[,summary.md]}, sane row_counts, then hash_payloads re-verify. Database read-only ctor (duckdb.cpp:453-495): open_regular pins the inode, engine opens by name with access_mode=READ_ONLY + external access/autoload disabled + temp dir 0B (467-474), post-open dev/ino re-check (482-492); opened_sha256 preads the pinned fd with size+mtime+ctime stability check (497-518). Analyst surfaces recording::{overview,fields,events,window,query,compare} (reader.hpp:50-58; analysis.cpp), the read-only bike-recording CLI (cli/recording.cpp:139-175; export subcommand goes through exclusive export_data), replay::PlaybackSession/ReplaySession consumers (cli/replay.cpp:20,90), and report renderers render_summary/render_report_database (report.cpp:118-124). BREAKERS/FAILURE BOUNDARIES. ## 2. Sub-Clause Evidence Analysis
(a) HOLDS. The sole seal marker is destination/'manifest.json' linked LAST (publish.cpp:86-92, catalog.hpp:20) with no fallible validation after it; staging names it 'sealed-manifest.json' (publish.cpp:69), a name no reader looks for. read_manifest refuses any directory lacking a regular manifest.json (reader.cpp:31-33), so a crash/failure before the commit link yields at most an unsealed staging dir (UNSEALED tombstone + payloads) and/or a half-published destination dir containing payloads but no manifest - zero readable sealed recordings. Verified: SIGKILL at 'checkpoint', 'link_run.duckdb' and 'manifest_link' leaves manifests().empty() (test_publish.cpp:88-96); 'unsealed staging and half-published destinations refuse open' (test_recording_storage.cpp:210-215). No overwrite/adoption anywhere: destination mkdir retries EEXIST with a fresh suffix (publish.cpp:73-79); link_new/link() fails EEXIST on any existing name incl. dangling symlinks (files.cpp:195-205); write_exclusive/open_exclusive use O_CREAT|O_EXCL|O_NOFOLLOW (files.cpp:108-120,177-185; export.cpp:77); a symlink occupying a candidate destination name is skipped not adopted (test_publish.cpp:82-86); write_database's payload link is likewise exclusive (database.cpp:136). Caveat outside the sealed-payload names: viz::detail::write_text intentionally REPLACES regular files (plot.cpp:185-198) - it is the plot sidecar writer, not a recording publication/export path. (b) STRICTLY VIOLATED as written, though deliberate and payload-safe. Inside publish() itself post-seal mutation is clean: after the manifest link only fsync + staging cleanup run (publish.cpp:97-106), and the sealed dir holds exactly {manifest.json, run.duckdb, summary.md} (test_recording_storage.cpp:78-81; RECORDING.md:94-99). But the CLI then writes unhashed sidecars INTO publication->directory post-seal: preview.csv (exclusive, ride.cpp:171), *.png (replacing, ride.cpp:174), ride.html (replacing, ride.cpp:175), and --html rewrites ride.html inside an existing sealed dir (ride.cpp:193; plot.cpp:185-198 truncates regular files, never follows symlinks). Readers tolerate extras - hash_payloads covers only manifest-named files (reader.cpp:20-25) - so hashed evidence is uncorrupted, but the directory's final contents exceed the documented container and a sidecar's corruption is undetectable. Reconciliation is partial: RIDE.md:207-208 confirms preview CSV/recording-derived HTML are produced only WITH recording and ride.cpp:193 consumes preview.csv from the run dir (establishing intended co-location), but RECORDING.md:94-99 lists only the three manifest-covered files and never documents sidecar placement inside the sealed directory. (c) VIOLATED in fact, with a documented-design caveat. ResearchRecording::publish assigns capture_ BEFORE finalize (research_publication.cpp:45, finalize at 47); a finalize failure leaves the capture latched in publication_failed where require_collecting permanently bars new events (capture.cpp:91-93). A retry call hits 'if (!capture_)' false -> skips staging entirely -> republishes the first-attempt evidence; updated 'events' spans are silently ignored. The freshness hole is reachable: finish() emits 'operator_stop_deferred' on every attempt where that attempt's fresh InterruptShield caught a signal (lifecycle.cpp:223,240-244), ResearchRun::event has no post-finalization guard (78-85), annotate_failure mutates receipts each call (233), and finish is retry-capable by contract (lifecycle.hpp:39-42 'an exception leaves receipts available for retry'; finish's first/path_ latches 203-216; tests DO retry finish after publish failure: test_lifecycle.cpp:124-133,160-169). Mitigations: the production CLI never retries a failed finish (run_research retries only when !finalization_attempted(), research.cpp:186-190), so the hole is latent; and NATIVE_POLICIES.md:109 documents 'Repeating a failed publication retries the same owned receipts', i.e. the latch codifies documented intent - though events_ growth between attempts still creates a silent divergence between run.events() and the sealed events table (the deferred fact survives only in failures_.secondary notes, lifecycle.cpp:248,257). (d) HOLDS. Physical path: hooks.reset runs seal('reset') - add_episode + derived emit_all through the OLD journal - before ++episode_id, journal replacement and emit('episode_started') (ride.cpp:147), so an ep-(N+1) event can only follow all ep-N events; RunCapture::event's monotone (episode_id,event_id) check turns any late ep-N event after an (N+1,0) start into a thrown error, not silent poison (capture.cpp:96-97); the 'sealed' gate at ride.cpp:165 blocks publication of an unsealed tail; a thrown episode_started emit aborts the reset and the trailing seal(reason) (ride.cpp:156) still gives the new episode real evidence. Research path mirrors it: before_reset seals the current episode before new_episode emits episode_started (lifecycle.cpp:86-95) and finish always seals before publish (239). Verified by two-episode journal isolation and reset-generation sealing tests (test_native_recording_lifecycle.cpp:361-434,469-538; test_recording_storage.cpp:282+; test_research_recording_lifecycle.cpp:237-245).

## 3. Exceptions, Dependencies & Consequences
DEPENDENCIES. POSIX-only is honored throughout: ::mkdtemp, ::link, ::mkdir, ::open(O_NOFOLLOW/O_EXCL), ::fsync, ::pread, ::fstat (platform/files.cpp; publish.cpp:6-11; database.cpp:79-83); hard-link support is required only inside output_root (staging is mkdtemp'ed inside it, so same-device is guaranteed) - a link-less or non-POSIX output root fails closed before any seal. EXCEPTIONS/EDGE SEMANTICS. On any pre-commit failure BOTH the unsealed staging dir and the half-published destination dir are deliberately left on disk with their paths embedded in the error (publish.cpp:93-96) - diagnostics retained at the cost of garbage directories; neither is readable as a recording. report_status='failed' legitimately seals a DB-only container: manifest file_sha256 then covers exactly {run.duckdb}, summary.md is absent, and readers accept that exact set (publish.cpp:49-58; reader.cpp:45-49; test_recording_storage.cpp:158-182). A disabled capture publishes nothing (publish.cpp:39; finalize.cpp:5-8; test_publish.cpp:60-64). Idempotent success: repeated finalize returns the cached original path, even given a different root argument (finalize.cpp:9,16-18; test_publish.cpp:53-55). Post-commit fsync failure inside cleanup is swallowed deliberately - 'cannot turn a sealed success into a reported failure' (publish.cpp:97-106). CONSEQUENCES. (c) A retried publish writes the first attempt's events_/episodes (database.cpp:110-111 reads capture.events()/episodes()); an operator_stop_deferred or caller-added event arriving between attempts lands in run.events() + failure notes but never in the sealed events table - silent stale-evidence publication. (b) Post-seal the directory accumulates unhashed files (preview.csv, 'Pedal effort.png'-class plots, ride.html); manifest integrity is unaffected (hash_payloads covers only named payloads) but the documented {run.duckdb, summary.md, manifest.json} contents listing is no longer the directory's truth, and the replacing PNG/HTML writers can rewrite their own sidecars in place (incl. a later --html regeneration). batch --overwrite replaces only batch logs, never published run dirs (cli/batch.cpp:42-47,52,65).

## 4. Test Coverage & Gaps
STRONG: per-stage fault matrix over all ~23 stages asserting publication_failed + no manifest + intact RAM evidence + retry to a fresh exclusive destination + idempotent repeat (test_publish.cpp:40-59); disabled capture creates nothing (60-64; test_recording_storage.cpp:131-139); report failure seals DB-only with explicit report_error (test_publish.cpp:65-72; test_recording_storage.cpp:158-182); concurrent finalizers get distinct dirs (test_publish.cpp:73-79); symlink destination not adopted (80-86); SIGKILL pre-commit leaves no sealed recording (88-96); double-SIGINT finalize_guarded seals then throws OperatorInterrupted (97-104); sealed dir contains exactly the three payload files (test_recording_storage.cpp:78-81); collision suffix -2, occupied dir untouched, first publication byte-stable (141-156); link order run.duckdb,summary.md,manifest.json + retryable evidence (184-221); missing/tampered/symlinked payloads and catalog/asset tamper rejected (223-279); multi-episode event isolation (282+; test_native_recording_lifecycle.cpp:361-434,469-538); capture retry with evidence intact (297-321); headless end-to-end rides publish one episode starting with episode_started (766-843); failed publication => nonzero exit + preserved primary (866-917); deferred-SIGINT journaling matrix physical+headless (561-682,919-962); research finish retry via stub publisher (test_lifecycle.cpp:107-134,152-170), InterruptingPublication wrapping the REAL ResearchRecording for deferred-interrupt semantics (test_research_recording_lifecycle.cpp:95-110,215-276), off-main finish publishes (278-337), zero-step evidence on policy-load failure (146-183), NATIVE_POLICIES-documented 'same owned receipts' retry semantics pinned by stub calls==2 assertions. GAPS: (1) No test drives ResearchRecording::publish back into finalize with a publication_failed capture AND a grown events span - the stale-retry latch is untested; stub publishers receive fresh spans by construction (test_lifecycle.cpp:63-69). (2) No test asserts sealed-directory contents after the CLI sidecar block (ride.cpp:169-178): the viewer preview/PNG/HTML writes into the sealed dir are unpinned - the 'exactly 3 files' assertion (test_recording_storage.cpp:78-81) exercises pure finalize only. (3) No test for --html regeneration inside an existing sealed dir (ride.cpp:193). (4) The mid-emit_all failure path inside ride.cpp's seal lambda (partial derived journal + committed episode evidence, ride.cpp:108-115) is unreachable - no injection seam exists inside EpisodeJournal::emit_all. (5) No concurrent reader-vs-sidecar-writer contract test (readers silently ignore unhashed extras).

## 5. Recommended Refinement
Restate (b) to match implemented product truth rather than forbid it: 'the manifest-covered payloads (run.duckdb, summary.md, manifest.json) are hash-protected and never rewritten after the manifest link; documented analyst sidecars (preview.csv, *.png, ride.html) may be created exclusively inside the published directory post-seal and are intentionally NOT manifest-covered' - then fix the doc gap by adding the sidecar line to RECORDING.md:94-99's layout and making the PNG/HTML writers either exclusive (matching preview.csv) or explicitly naming their in-place regeneration contract; today the strict claim is contradicted by cli/ride.cpp:169-178,193. For (c), pick one: either re-stage capture_ on every publish() call while the capture is not yet published - re-staging is a pure function of the current spans and keeps the no-duplicates property because the staged unique_ptr is discarded wholesale on failure (research_publication.cpp:19-45) - so retries honor updated events including a late operator_stop_deferred; or ratify the latch by documenting in the ResearchPublication contract (lifecycle.hpp:39-42) and NATIVE_POLICIES.md:109 that retries must pass the identical event set, and stop finish() from appending operator_stop_deferred once finalization_attempted_ is set (route it solely to failures_.secondary, lifecycle.cpp:240-258) so run.events() can never silently diverge from the sealed events table. As stated the property's (c) clause currently fails on the latched-stale-retry behavior even though the shipped CLI never reaches it.

# Stage 3 (`R1`) Independent Research Report — `P17`: Deferred-`SIGINT` Shielding, `operator_stop_deferred` Journaling & Exit-Code `130` Guarantee

- **Inspected Commit:** `0502660dd14bd1021b3da90a61e20dcb1d5805a1`
- **Compliance Verdict (`R1`):** `partially_violated`
- **Product Justification Verdict (`R1`):** `relevant_engineering_contract`

## 1. Producers, Mutators, Consumers & Potential Breakers
PRODUCERS (deferred-count producers): (1) the process-wide SIGINT handler `shield_sigint_handler` at native/src/platform/signals.cpp:41-49 — one relaxed atomic load of `g_current_target` plus `*t = *t + 1` on a `volatile sig_atomic_t`; no locks, allocation, I/O. (2) `InterruptShield::note()` signals.cpp:109 — the injected seam tests and embedders use. MUTATORS (disposition/target wiring): `InterruptShield::InterruptShield()` signals.cpp:65-95 — main-thread gate via `on_main_thread()` (pthread_main_np on Apple, getpid==gettid on Linux, false elsewhere, signals.cpp:51-59); claims one of 16 fixed `g_shield_slots` (signals.cpp:25,37) by CAS, stores `slot.previous_target` (81), installs the counting handler via sigaction saving `previous_action` (82-90), then publishes `&deferred_` to `g_current_target` LAST (94) so a signal between install and publish lands on the previous scope. `~InterruptShield()` signals.cpp:97-107 — repoints `g_current_target` to `slot.previous_target` FIRST (103), then restores `previous_action` (104), frees the slot: strict LIFO for stack-nested scopes. Copy/move deleted (signals.hpp:36-39) because the counter is a handler-visible fixed address. Off-main or slot-exhausted (>=17 nested) → `slot_<0` flag-holder (signals.cpp:69,78; signals.hpp:46-48). CONSUMERS (deferred() readers / 130 mappers / journal emitters): native/src/cli/research.cpp:142 outer shield over the whole `run_research`; polls at :81-82,:88,:92,:115 → `interrupted` → `throw OperatorInterrupted` (:180); catch maps `interrupted || interrupt.deferred()` → 130 (:193) and the final return forces `interrupt.deferred() ? 130 : result` (:203). native/src/research/lifecycle.cpp:223 inner shield inside `ResearchRun::finish` covering annotate_failure/seal/publish; :240-244 emits `operator_stop_deferred`; :255-258 throws `OperatorInterrupted` or records the secondary note. native/src/recording/interrupt.cpp:6-15 `finalize_guarded` = fresh shield + `finalize` + throw on deferred (production API; only tests call it — test_native_recording_lifecycle.cpp:617,677,977; test_publish.cpp:100). native/src/cli/ride.cpp:67 shield over `physical_run`; consumers `terminal()` (:96) and `hooks.close_requested` (:130) set reason="operator_stop"; exit decided at :183-186. native/src/cli/settle.cpp:41 with checks at :46,:48,:59. native/src/cli/replay.cpp:21,:89 with checks at :28,:61,:79,:96,:109,:117. native/src/cli/batch.cpp:56 + `options.cancelled` (:59) + final check (:78); batch/sweep.cpp:57,:59,:71 same for `bike-research-sweep` (dispatched via native/src/tools/research_sweep_main.cpp:11-13). Child-side convention: cancelled workers get exit_code=130 (batch/process_pool.cpp:56; platform/process.cpp:189 pre-launch cancel; WIFSIGNALED→128+sig at process.cpp:394-395). Journal pipeline: `OperatorEvent` list → `ResearchRecording::publish` adapts each into `journal.emit` (recording/research_publication.cpp:26-44, `operator_stop_deferred` is a non-tail kind emitted in order) → `write_database` inserts events in the single transaction (recording/database.cpp:111) → commit boundary is the manifest link (recording/publish.cpp:86-92). BREAKERS/restore boundaries: exit-code authority is per-command (dispatch only maps exceptions→1/2 at cli/application.cpp:23-30; main.cpp:13-15 → 1). `ride.cpp` physical_run lacks a terminal `interrupt.deferred()` check — see violations. Unshielded commands (cli/simulation.cpp, cli/export.cpp, cli/recording.cpp + recording_main.cpp) keep the default disposition → death-by-SIGINT ⇒ shell-visible 128+2.

## 2. Sub-Clause Evidence Analysis
(a) InterruptShield async-signal-safe lock/allocation-free handler + strict LIFO restoration — HOLDS under the stated assumptions. Handler body is exactly `g_current_target.load(relaxed)` + `*t=*t+1` on `volatile sig_atomic_t` (signals.cpp:41-49); registry is a fixed `std::array<ShieldSlot,16>` with CAS `occupied` flags (signals.cpp:25-38) — no heap, no lock. LIFO: each slot snapshots `previous_target` (signals.cpp:81) and `previous_action` (signals.cpp:86); dtor repoints target then restores disposition (signals.cpp:103-104), which for stack-nested main-thread scopes is strict LIFO (verified test_primitives.cpp:363-374: inner owns the signal, outer resumes). Copy/move deleted so the counter can't dangle (signals.hpp:34-39). Caveats within compliance: LIFO is enforced only by the stack-scope convention — out-of-order destruction of a heap-allocated shield would restore a dangling `g_current_target` (no runtime guard); the 17th nested scope silently installs nothing (flag-holder) so real SIGINTs inside it are attributed to the 16th scope; `sa_flags=0` means no SA_RESTART, so syscalls inside the section can see EINTR; the handler absorbs SIGINT rather than forwarding to the previous disposition — the deferred count is the sole record; a signal delivered to a non-main thread still lands on the process-wide handler and counts on the owning scope, which is correct attribution. (b) SIGINT deferred during shielded episode execution/seal/DuckDB publication/post-run artifact writing → persistence completes cleanly and CLI exits 130 — PARTIALLY VIOLATED. Persistence completion holds everywhere: the counting handler cannot interrupt the section, proven by test_publish.cpp:88-104 (two real SIGINTs at `manifest_link` inside `finalize_guarded` → sealed manifest + OperatorInterrupted) and test_research_recording_lifecycle.cpp:250-275 (SIGINT inside publish() → manifest sealed + `operator interrupt deferred` message). Exit-130 coverage: `run_research` HOLDS end to end — outer shield research.cpp:142 over prepare/run/finish/close; loop polls (:81-82,:88,:92,:115); inner shield lifecycle.cpp:223 over seal+publish → OperatorInterrupted (:256) → catch :185 → `result=130` (:193); unconditional final guard `interrupt.deferred() ? 130 : result` (:203) even covers SIGINT during `environment->close()` and post-finish status/output. `bike-ride --research` shares this path. `bike-settle` (:46,:48,:59), `bike-replay` (:28,:61,:79,:96,:109,:117), `bike-batch`/`bike-research-sweep` (batch.cpp:59,78; sweep.cpp:71; cancelled children 130 via process_pool.cpp:56/process.cpp:189) all hold. VIOLATION in plain `bike-ride` (`physical_run`, ride.cpp:63-187): `reason` becomes "operator_stop" only via `terminal()` (:96) or `hooks.close_requested` (:130) during the run loop. After the loop there is NO `interrupt.deferred()` check — the exit decision at :183-186 uses only `reason`/`model_status`. A SIGINT arriving during `seal()` (:156, incl. `runtime->seal_episode`/`add_episode`/`emit_all`), preview finalization (:157-163), `recording::finalize` DuckDB publication (:166), plot/preview artifact writes (:169-178), or `runtime->close()` (:179) is counted by the still-live shield (constructed :67) and then silently dropped → `return 0` for duration_reached/end_of_track/viewer_closed (or 1 for step_cap/wall_clock_cap). This directly contradicts 'never silently returning 0' — and every sibling frontend (research/settle/replay/batch) has the terminal `interrupt.deferred() ? 130 : ...` guard this one lacks. Secondary edge (not a violation): in run_research's failure path, a SIGINT during the secondary `finish(failure,true)` retry (research.cpp:187) lands on the inner shield; with `failures_.primary` set, lifecycle.cpp:255-257 records only a note (no throw) and the outer counter stays 0 → exit 3; the deferred note is retained in diagnostics. (c) Deferred interrupts arriving before DB commit journaled as `operator_stop_deferred` (RECORDING.md:179-181, 337-338) — PARTIALLY VIOLATED. Research path HOLDS: lifecycle.cpp:240-244 emits `operator_stop_deferred` ({"deferred_during":"publication"}) when the inner shield counted a signal before `publication_->publish(...)` (:245); `ResearchRecording::publish` journals it via `journal.emit` for the current episode (research_publication.cpp:29-44) inside `write_database`'s single transaction (database.cpp:111) before the manifest-link commit (publish.cpp:86-92); end-to-end asserted by test_research_recording_lifecycle.cpp:215-246 (SIGINT inside seal_episode → kind present in the sealed DB) and :250-275 (SIGINT inside publish → NOT journaled, correctly: it arrived at/after the journal boundary). Physical path VIOLATED: `ride.cpp` never emits `operator_stop_deferred` — the only production emission is lifecycle.cpp:241 (ResearchRun::finish), which `physical_run` does not use; a deferred interrupt during seal/finalize leaves no journal trace (only outcome.reason="operator_stop" in summary_json when a run-loop poll saw it). RECORDING.md:179-180 explicitly lists `operator_stop_deferred` among ordinary-physical journal kinds, and the physical-side tests model the contract by MANUAL `journal.emit("operator_stop_deferred",...)` (test_native_recording_lifecycle.cpp:579,645,951) — the test comment at :574-577 claims 'the native ride frontend emits the same kind through research::ResearchRun::finish', which is true only for `bike-ride --research`, not for `physical_run`. Boundary note: the single check at lifecycle.cpp:240 defines 'before commit' — a signal arriving between that check and publish() is counted, completes publication, throws OperatorInterrupted, and is legitimately unjournaled.

## 3. Exceptions, Dependencies & Consequences
DEPENDENCIES: POSIX SIGINT/sigaction; main-thread identification via pthread_main_np (macOS) or gettid==getpid (Linux) — other platforms install nothing (conservative degrade, signals.cpp:51-59); process-wide disposition (SIGINT delivered to any thread counts on the owning scope); documented LIFO stack-scope discipline (signals.hpp:21-23). EXCEPTIONS/CONSEQUENCES: (1) LIFO is contractual, not enforced — non-stack destruction order would leave `g_current_target` pointing at a destroyed scope's `deferred_` and re-install the counting handler with a dangling target (memory write through a stale pointer on next SIGINT); no production code violates the stack-scope pattern. (2) Registry cap 16: a 17th nested shield is a flag-holder; SIGINTs inside it count toward the innermost installed scope (graceful, silent). (3) sa_flags=0 (no SA_RESTART): blocking syscalls inside shielded sections can return EINTR — a spurious EINTR inside DuckDB/fsync surfaces as publication_failed (retryable by the finalize contract, finalize.cpp:10-19), not silent corruption. (4) The handler absorbs SIGINT during the scope — it is never forwarded to the previous disposition; honoring is entirely the owning thread's deferred() policy, so any scope that never checks deferred() swallows interrupts (the bike-ride post-loop window is exactly this). (5) `bike-ride --research`/research exit-130 also covers the post-finish close; plain bike-ride does not. (6) `finalize_guarded` (interrupt.cpp:6-15) exists as the self-shielding API but has no production caller — ride deliberately uses raw `finalize` under its own outer shield (interrupt.cpp:10-12 comment), so a deferred signal there yields no exception and no journal event. (7) `bike-sim`/`bike-export`/`bike-recording` are unshielded → default disposition kills by signal → shell observes 128+SIGINT; consistent with the 130 convention without literally returning it. (8) Batch children: cancelled-before-launch=130 (process_pool.cpp:56), signal-killed children → 128+sig (process.cpp:394-395).

## 4. Test Coverage & Gaps
COVERED: (a) platform/test_primitives.cpp:329-389 — main-thread install, double-raise count, sentinel-handler non-invocation, disposition restore on exit and on exception unwind, LIFO nesting (:363-374), off-main flag-holder + note(); duplicated at reference/test_recording_capture.cpp:369-418. (b) recording/test_publish.cpp:88-104 — fork child raises 2x SIGINT at manifest_link inside `finalize_guarded` → sealed + OperatorInterrupted; reference/test_research_recording_lifecycle.cpp:215-246 (real raise inside seal_episode via SealInterruptPolicy:89 → deferred journaled + published + OperatorInterrupted), :250-275 (InterruptingPublication:109 raise inside publish → manifest sealed, 'operator interrupt deferred' message, operator_stop_deferred correctly absent); research/test_lifecycle.cpp:227-238 (raise inside publish → OperatorInterrupted, single publication); batch/test_plan.cpp:159-163 (cancel → child exit_code=130). (c) test_native_recording_lifecycle.cpp:561-711,944-979 — journal composition: pre-finalize deferred interrupt journaled as operator_stop_deferred (asserted in RAM and sealed DB), inside-finalize not journaled + OperatorInterrupted, SIGINT during advance seals prefix as operator_stop with no control kinds. GAPS: (1) NO end-to-end test drives `cli::ride_command`/`physical_run` with a SIGINT deferred during seal/finalize/artifact-write and asserts the process exit code — the exact window where ride.cpp exits 0 is untested; the physical tests stop at RunCapture/finalize_guarded level and emit `operator_stop_deferred` manually, so the missing production emit is invisible to the suite (test file header even notes 'journal-level ports emit the oracle's kind vocabulary directly', test_native_recording_lifecycle.cpp:43-45). (2) NO test asserts a CLI-level return of 130: the SIGINT children exercise `ResearchRun::finish`/`finalize_guarded` directly and check OperatorInterrupted/manifest, never `run_research`'s return value; settle/replay/ride exit-130 lines are code-verifiable but signal-unexercised. (3) No test covers the >16-slot registry exhaustion path or non-LIFO destruction (out of contract, but also unguarded). (4) No test delivers SIGINT during `write_state_file` in settle or during preview/plot writes in ride.

## 5. Recommended Refinement
Split the property per subclause and per frontend rather than one conjunctive statement. (a) stands as written for stack-allocated main-thread scopes; add an explicit caveat that LIFO is convention-enforced only (out-of-order destruction dangles `g_current_target`) and that ≥17 nested scopes silently degrade to flag-holders. (b) should be narrowed or the code fixed: the guarantee 'exits 130 whenever SIGINT is deferred during seal/publication/artifact-writing' currently fails for plain `bike-ride` — add `interrupt.deferred() ? 130 : ...` (or fold `deferred()` into `reason`) ahead of the exit decision at ride.cpp:183-186 to match research.cpp:203, settle.cpp:59, replay.cpp:117, batch.cpp:78; optionally also journal the deferred interrupt. (c) should name the boundary explicitly ('deferred before the publication commit point is journaled; deferred inside publish is honored via OperatorInterrupted and not journaled') and either fix the physical frontend to emit `operator_stop_deferred` when `interrupt.deferred()` is observed while the EpisodeJournal is still open (matching RECORDING.md:179-180 and the Python `_journal_deferred` the tests emulate), or amend RECORDING.md to scope the kind to research journals only. Recommended test additions: a child-process probe that runs `ride_command` headless with a `before_stage`-style injection (or a raise inside `finalize`) and asserts exit 130 + a sealed manifest; a CLI-level assertion that `run_research` returns 130 on a deferred interrupt; and a test asserting presence of `operator_stop_deferred` in a published physical journal if the emit is added. Overall verdict: partially_violated — shield mechanics and the research frontend fully satisfy the property; the plain `bike-ride` physical path can silently exit 0 on a deferred SIGINT during seal/finalize/artifact writes and never journals `operator_stop_deferred`.

# Stage 3 (`R1`) Independent Research Report — `P18`: `ReplaySession` Checked-Replay Certificate Soundness, Mismatch Halt & Command-0 Verified-Frame Rollback

- **Inspected Commit:** `0502660dd14bd1021b3da90a61e20dcb1d5805a1`
- **Compliance Verdict (`R1`):** `partially_violated`
- **Product Justification Verdict (`R1`):** `relevant_engineering_contract`

## 1. Producers, Mutators, Consumers & Potential Breakers
PRODUCERS. ReplayInput is built by load_replay_input (native/src/replay/input.cpp:203-207) dispatching to database_input (input.cpp:109-200: replay_eligible gate :112-113, contract-3 recipe :119-126, validate_execution :126, summary/recipe execution agreement :128, changed-during-run flags :129-131, recorded error :132-133, policy-change :134-136, resolved-config equality :137-138, exact asset set :140-149, extents :150-152, manifest :153-156, four streams :157-166, transition wiring :167-173, command_shapes via input_detail.hpp:33-42, integration states :175-182, terrain sha :183-188, physical-prefix coverage :189-197, metrics :198) or load_legacy_input (native/src/replay/legacy.cpp:119-177, with validate_execution :88-118). Certificate/frame producers inside ReplaySession::Impl (native/src/replay/session.cpp:74-92): ctor :94-103 (check_execution :96, replay_executable_sha256 capture :97, FixedAssets :98 -> rebuild.cpp:101-112 mkdtemp materialization, start() :99 -> :106-111 request_from_input rebuild.cpp:114-178 + research::prepare_environment + verify_configuration rebuild.cpp:180-214 + runtime::make_research :110, initial require_state :100, initial observations[0] require_evidence :101, frame=environment->render_frame() :102); advance() :213-253 produces per-command verification (begin_control :228-229 feeding runtime::NativeResearchRuntime::begin_control research.cpp:172-197, advance_control :232 -> research.cpp:341-395, transition compare :234, observation compare :235, applied_prefix :236/:113-121 -> prepare_applied_since/acknowledge_applied research.cpp:529-555, rider_commands_prefix :237/:125-138 -> research.cpp:556-581 + journal writer research.cpp:233-241, acknowledge_control :238 -> research.cpp:396-406, finish() :140-183 emitting the certificate Value at :173-181); check_execution itself at rebuild.cpp:215-233 (native_source_sha256/execution_identity/mujoco_library_sha256/numeric_provider compare :220-224, model_source_sha256 :225, allocation_solver :227-229, drift flags :230-232) with platform::build_identity()/execution_identity() from platform/provenance.cpp:103-133. MUTATORS. frame: ctor :102, budget/target yield :233, capture_verified :139, rollback :251. verified: ONLY init-list verified(frame) :95 and capture_verified :139 (called :239 per completed command and :182 inside finish). mismatch/failure_step/first_mismatch/generation: catch :242-251. command/applied/rider_commands cursors: :238/:120/:137. completed :182; closed :262-264; result :173. restart() :254-261 swaps the whole Impl (generation+1 reserved :257, manifest equality gate :259, old env closed :260); close() :263.
