# Stage 3 (`R1`) Independent Research Report — `P22`: Configuration Mode Gates, Integer-Tick Quantization, Two-Phase Model Builders & Terrain/Kinematics Invariants

- **Inspected Commit:** `0502660dd14bd1021b3da90a61e20dcb1d5805a1`
- **Compliance Verdict (`R1`):** `partially_violated`
- **Product Justification Verdict (`R1`):** `mixed_subclauses`

## 1. Producers, Mutators, Consumers & Potential Breakers
(a) Mass sealing: producers of registry entries are the physical topology builders — legacy_topology.cpp:89 (battery), :90 (saddle_post), :95 (frame_structure over remaining frame geoms); linkage.cpp:60 (shock_damper), :99 (steer_assembly), :100 (stanchions), :101 (fork_lowers), :102 (front_wheel -> wheel.direct('inertial')), :122 (chainstay), :141 (seatstay), :152 (rocker), :162 (shock_yoke); drivetrain.cpp:43 (motor), :44 (crank_pedals), :67 (rear_wheel -> wheel.direct('inertial')) — exactly the 15 BikeMassSpecs budget ids (mass.hpp:144-162 'the fifteen physical budgets'). register_component (xml_writer.cpp:135-142) is a no-op when BuildContext::mass_registry() returns nullptr, i.e. in any non-physical mode (builder_internal.hpp:50-52). The consumer/seal is assign_budgets (xml_writer.cpp:143-177) invoked only when physical (builder.cpp:63), before finish_physical_topology (builder.cpp:67-68). Breakers: duplicate id (xml_writer.cpp:139), empty positive-mass part set (:140), expected!=actual name set (:155), a part in two components (:151), uncovered positive bike geom (:157-158), wheel-inertial set mismatch (:159-161), wheel part not a single inertial with mass==budget (:164-166), non-geom part in a non-wheel component (:170), nonpositive/zero-weight component via assign_component_mass (mass.cpp:417-437). Post-seal mutator: finish_physical_topology -> set_inertia (xml_writer.cpp:120-124) removes existing inertial children, re-adds via inertial() (:114-119), and zeroes direct geom mass attrs — used on crank/pedals/rear_wheel; total mass conserved (crank_pedals budget splits spindle .10/.85 + 2*arm .20/.85 + 2*pedal .175/.85 = 1.0). Write-once: add_body_inertial throws when carrier has an inertial (mass.cpp:392-394); Element-level inertial() appends unconditionally (xml_writer.cpp:114-119) — write-once holds on the XmlBodyElement carrier, not on the raw MJCF Element. (a2) JointEnvelope: producers load_joint_envelopes (validate.cpp:2069-2130) -> envelope_from_object (:1969-2028) and strength_coordinate (:2181-2222) deliberately emit neutral_anatomical_rad=NaN, direction=0 sentinels (:1983-1984, :2211-2212); binder with_pose_neutral_angles (rider_geometry.cpp:730-742) fills neutral from the build pose, runs detail::post_init (validate.cpp:1002-1006), and enforces neutral in [min,max]; consumers anatomical_angle (spindle_math.cpp:537-543), joint_q_range (:527 via validate_joint_envelope :513-518), reference_rom_check (validate.cpp:1931-1954), project_rider_controller envelope validation (project_config.cpp:311); invoker builder.cpp:71-77. Breakers: nonfinite min/max/direction not +-1 (:990-997), empty provenance (:997), nonfinite neutral (:1003), neutral outside declared range (rider_geometry.cpp:736-739), schema/key-set rejection (:1979-1980, :2083-2099, :2122-2127). (b) Suspension: producer BikeSuspensionSystem ctor (suspension_setup.cpp:1005-1018) sets config=click_config or damper_preset_config(base) (:611-644) and clamps clicks into both dampers via make_base_damper (:198-207) + hbo clamp (:839-841). Mutators apply_preset (:1020-1029 — writes current_preset+config+fork/shock clicks+lockout), cycle_preset (:1031-1047 over {base,plush,enduro,park}), set_clicks on each damper (:746-762, :858-883 — validate whole record then clamp to [0,max]), toggle_lockout (:885-889). Breakers: damper_preset_has_config false for custom (:607-609) making apply_preset(custom) a documented no-op; integer() rejects non-int clicks. Bypass: config and damper click fields are public, so direct writes desynchronize config from dampers silently, and current_preset stays base for a custom-injected click_config. (c) Config validation: producers — TOML load path -> cfg::post_init(SimulationPhysicsConfig) (validate.cpp; brake domain :666-678, quantization :1154-1248, Nyquist floors :1254-1282 + :1295-1299, seated-climb tick rules :884-902); wire path -> decode_drive (writer_config.hpp:1067-1113) mirroring brake checks :1090-1107; typed writer boundary drivetrain::validate(DriveConfig) (config_validation.hpp:207-220) invoked via checked_config (writers/drivetrain.cpp:19-22) — OMITS all brake_ceiling/brake_hold checks; wire schema layer cfg::wire::validate_config (schema.cpp:594-602 key sets; project_config.cpp:393) is key/type-only. Runtime consumer StaticBrake config built from drive.brake_hold_* (runtime.cpp:396-397). (d) Kinematics/terrain: producer HorstLinkageSolver ctor computes fixed links (solver.cpp:38-86); stroke_table_() lazy-builds a 101-pt linspace + wheel-travel solves into stroke_table_cache_ (solver.cpp:299-313, mutable cache declared solver.hpp:258-277); sole consumer solve_state_from_shock_stroke (:315-323). Terrain: HeightFieldSpec nrow=2 default (types.hpp:153), validate>=2 + engine sample bound (heightfield.cpp:8-14), normalize copies row 0 into all rows (:30-33) -> laterally-planar field; build_terrain emits nrow/ncol/size declaration (model/terrain.cpp:7-13); samples populated post-compile into the owned model (project_config.cpp:106-117; test_mjcf.cpp:646-652). Surface: 5-entry registry (surface.cpp:10-16), get_surface rejects unknown (:41-43), SurfaceMap sort+validate ordered/non-overlapping (:50-63); label twin SurfaceLabels identical semantics (senses_codec.cpp:217-247); projection surface_labels_of (project_config.cpp:51-67) rejects unmapped names; wire label decode rejects unknown (senses_codec.cpp:206-213). Model seal: require_request_model (project_config.cpp:89-119) revalidates field+track, asserts fit, recompiles expected model, injects samples, byte-compares immutable_model_image (:70-87, :118-119).

## 2. Sub-Clause Evidence Analysis
(a) HOLDS with two precise caveats. Unique registration is enforced by both registry flavors: ComponentRegistry (mass.cpp:443-461) and MassRegistry (xml_writer.cpp:135-142) reject duplicate ids and empty positive-mass sets; registration is physical-mode-only via mass_registry() (builder_internal.hpp:52). The bijective 15-component seal is real: BikeMassSpecs declares exactly 15 budgets (mass.hpp:144-162) and all 15 ids are registered (10 linkage.cpp + 3 legacy_topology.cpp + 3 drivetrain.cpp sites — count: battery, saddle_post, frame_structure, shock_damper, steer_assembly, stanchions, fork_lowers, chainstay, seatstay, rocker, shock_yoke, motor, crank_pedals, front_wheel, rear_wheel); assign_budgets enforces bijection over ids, no shared parts, full positive-geom coverage, wheel inertial==budget (xml_writer.cpp:143-177). Caveat 1: 'write-once inertial' holds on XmlBodyElement (mass.cpp:392-394) but the raw Element append path detail::inertial() is unguarded (xml_writer.cpp:114-119) — only caller discipline + set_inertia's remove-then-add (:120-124) keep it single; in-scope flow never double-adds. Caveat 2: the seal verifies coverage/budgets at builder.cpp:63, then finish_physical_topology (builder.cpp:67-68) redistributes crank/pedal geom masses into body inertials and rewrites rear_wheel's inertial — totals conserved, representation changed; registry Element* pointers dangling but unused afterwards. JointEnvelope pre-binding rejection holds: NaN-neutral/direction=0 sentinels are produced at load (validate.cpp:1983, :2211) and every evaluation path (anatomical_angle spindle_math.cpp:537-543, joint_q_range via validate_joint_envelope :513-518 + :527, detail::post_init validate.cpp:1002-1006, reference_rom_check :1948, runtime project_config.cpp:311) throws before reading a meaningful range; binding exists only via with_pose_neutral_angles (rider_geometry.cpp:730-742, invoked builder.cpp:71-77). Mode gates hold: builder.cpp:17 (standard|ride), :27 (physical=>ride), :28-29 (crank=>ride), :32-34 (articulated_planar=>physical), :36 (articulated_effort=>articulated), :39-43 (non-flat attachments=>physical+articulated). Two-phase holds: generate_mjcf produces XML text only (builder.cpp:11-83); compile_mjcf mounts into VFS and mj_loadXML's (:121-147), rejecting plugins (:145). (b) PARTIALLY HOLDS. Through the preset API the sync claim holds: ctor seeds config from preset/click_config and clamps into dampers (suspension_setup.cpp:1005-1018); apply_preset writes current_preset, config, and all damper clicks + hbo + lockout atomically (:1020-1029); cycle_preset rotates 4 presets (:1031-1047); custom is an intentional no-op (:607-609). But the invariant is convention-scoped, not enforced: (i) a custom click_config leaves current_preset==base while config describes a non-base tune — the preset label misreports state; (ii) config and damper click fields are public mutable members, so any direct set_clicks/field write silently desynchronizes config from damper state; (iii) out-of-range custom clicks are stored verbatim in config while dampers clamp — config!=damper state by design (asserted for the cfg twin at test_schema.cpp:1839-1844). (c) PARTIALLY VIOLATED as literally stated. Hysteresis/positivity bounds DO agree across layers for tires (validate.cpp:169-172, :239-242, :300-303, :389-392 vs config_validation.hpp DamperCore :40-54 and the tire validators ~:270-397) and the StaticBrake ordering brake_hold_release>=engage exists BOTH in post_init (validate.cpp:674-678) and in the wire decode boundary (writer_config.hpp:1102-1107) with identical positivity domains (:1090-1101). However: (1) drivetrain::validate(DriveConfig) — the typed validator actually guarding DrivetrainWriter via checked_config (writers/drivetrain.cpp:19-22) — contains NO brake_ceiling/brake_hold checks at all (config_validation.hpp:207-220), so a programmatically-built DriveConfig with inverted hold bounds reaches the writer unchecked; (2) integer-tick quantization (|rint(v/clock)*clock - v| <= 1e-9) and the Nyquist tau>=2*clock floors exist ONLY in post_init (validate.cpp:1154-1248, :1254-1282, :1295-1299, :884-902); the wire/typed validators check mere nonnegativity for the same fields (config_validation.hpp:120-121, :151-155, :182) — they carry no timestep context so parity is impossible as stated; (3) a small inverse divergence: drivetrain::validate requires stop_time_s strictly positive (:120) while post_init quantizes it allowing zero (:1238-1248). On the stated schema-validated-wire assumption the decode boundary does agree on brake bounds, but the property's claim that SimulationPhysicsConfig 'and wire config_validation.hpp agree on' quantization/Nyquist is false — those checks are single-sided. (d) HOLDS. Stroke-table memoization is real and safe: stroke_table_cache_ is a mutable optional pair (solver.hpp:258-277) lazily built once from immutable solver geometry (solver.cpp:299-313; geometry fixed in ctor :38-86 — no specs mutator exists, so no invalidation path is needed); only solve_state_from_shock_stroke consumes it (:315-323); table contents are bitwise-verified against the oracle (test_horst_link.cpp:794-814) though the caching behavior itself is untested. The compiled field is a 2-row planar heightfield: nrow defaults to 2 (types.hpp:153), validate() requires nrow,ncol>=2 (heightfield.cpp:9), normalize() writes the profile into row 0 then copies it to every remaining row (:30-33) making the field laterally constant (planar), build_terrain declares nrow/ncol/size (model/terrain.cpp:7-13) with samples injected post-compile, and require_request_model asserts compiled nrow/ncol==spec and byte-compares the whole compiled image (project_config.cpp:104-119). The surface domain is exactly the five labels: registry (surface.cpp:10-16), kSurfaces (validate.cpp:135-136, used :428-430), SurfaceLabel enum + bijective name map (senses.hpp:21-43), SurfaceLabels twin semantics (senses_codec.cpp:217-247), label_field wire rejection (:206-213), surface_labels_of rejection (project_config.cpp:52-57), per-surface effort caps (model_config.hpp:319-333) — all consistent.

## 3. Exceptions, Dependencies & Consequences
Dependencies: the seal's correctness depends on every physical builder calling register_component (registration is opt-in per site; a new positive-mass geom without registration trips 'mass registry does not cover every bike geom exactly once' xml_writer.cpp:158 — so the failure mode is fail-loud, good). The seal runs BEFORE finish_physical_topology, so post-seal set_inertia rewrites are outside the checked region — conserved totals rely on the constants in physical_topology.cpp, not on a re-check; a future profile change there would not retrigger assign_budgets. The registry stores raw Element* into a mutable tree: safe today because nothing consults the registry after assign_budgets, but set_inertia's remove of the rear_wheel inertial (physical_topology.cpp) leaves the registry pointing at a removed element — an ordering hazard if a second seal pass were ever added. JointEnvelope depends on the NaN-neutral sentinel convention: any producer that fills neutral at decode (rather than leaving NaN) silently disables the pre-binding rejection — the sentinel is contract, not a type-state. Suspension sync depends on callers using apply_preset/cycle_preset exclusively; public field access is an unguarded bypass. The (c) asymmetries: wire decode_drive enforces brake ordering but the typed drivetrain::validate(DriveConfig) does not — consequence: only programmatic construction of DriveConfig (tests, embedded callers) can inject inverted hold bounds into DrivetrainWriter, since every wire document passes decode_drive; severity is therefore a latent contract hole rather than a live ingress hole. Quantization/Nyquist are necessarily physics-side because the writer wire document lacks timestep_s — the property's parity phrasing should be re-scoped rather than 'fixed'. stop_time_s: wire positive vs physics quantized-nonnegative divergence is benign (wire stricter). Stroke-table memoization depends on solver immutability — specs_ is never mutated post-ctor (solver.cpp:38-86); introducing a specs mutator would invalidate the cache silently. Surface consistency depends on three hand-maintained copies of the five-name domain (surface.cpp:10-16, senses.hpp:21-43, validate.cpp:135-136) — name-set drift compiles fine and only fails at runtime get_surface/label_field rejection; the effort-cap field names in model_config.hpp:319-333 are a fourth copy by naming convention.

## 4. Test Coverage & Gaps
Covered: (a) registry/inertial/assign via oracle-driven tables — add_body_inertial incl. duplicate_inertial rejection (test_inertia.cpp:412-483), assign_component_mass + register_component cases (:488-569), mass_specs count/order (:573+); JointEnvelope end-to-end — pose-bound neutrals vs compiled jnt_range to 1e-12 AND runtime controller envelope parity (test_mjcf.cpp:548-695), loader schema cases (test_schema.cpp:1683-1741), seated-topology envelope usage (test_seated_topology.cpp:130-168); builder gates indirectly via physical() fixture builds (test_mjcf.cpp:549,630-641). (b) preset table labels/has_config/configs (test_inertia.cpp:1430-1462; test_schema.cpp:1790-1820), click clamps ctor+set_clicks, lockout toggle, travel/stroke rejections (test_inertia.cpp:1612-1690), apply_preset + cycle incl. custom->index-0 restart (:1693-1781), cfg-side verbatim-vs-clamped split (test_schema.cpp:1835-1845). (c) quantization rejections for monitors holds via staged TOML (test_resolution.cpp:482-520), nonfinite timestep (:524-549), static-brake behavior (test_native_static_brake.cpp:65; test_static_brake_latch.cpp). (d) stroke-table contents bitwise (test_horst_link.cpp:794-814), surface registry order (test_generators.cpp:75), senses labels (test_rider_senses.cpp:48-153), hfield extent contract incl. corrupt nrow/ncol/adr rejections (test_contracts.cpp:1445-1499), two-phase sample injection path exercised (test_mjcf.cpp:641-652). Gaps: no test trips the assign_budgets seal failures end-to-end (registry mismatch, unregistered geom, wheel inertial budget mismatch are unreachable-negative paths — they only fire on a broken refactor); no test asserts Element-level inertial() can't double-add (only carrier-level duplicate is exercised); no test covers BikeSuspensionSystem with a custom click_config asserting current_preset semantics or config/damper divergence; no test exercises drivetrain::validate(DriveConfig) on inverted brake_hold bounds — the very gap reported above, meaning the missing check has no failing-test pressure; Nyquist-floor rejections (assist.tau<2dt, closure_tau<2dt, pneumatic rate_relaxation<2dt) and the remaining quantization domains (research.*, reposition_*, balance_*) are not evidenced by located tests — only the monitors quantization is explicitly staged; memoization itself (single build, cache reuse, determinism across calls) is untested — only table contents; SurfaceLabels ctor rejection paths are untested at the unit level; require_request_model's image-compare seal lacks a dedicated negative test.

## 5. Recommended Refinement
Refine the property statement to match the audited reality: (1) split subclause (c) into two clauses — 'post_init(SimulationPhysicsConfig) enforces StaticBrake ordering, 1e-9 integer-tick quantization of every physics-clock deadline, and tau>=2*timestep_s Nyquist floors' (holds; cite validate.cpp:666-678,1154-1299,884-902) and 'the wire decode boundary re-validates the brake domain identically' (holds; writer_config.hpp:1090-1107) — and drop the claim that config_validation.hpp itself enforces quantization/Nyquist, which is impossible without a timestep context; (2) record the real defect found: drivetrain::validate(DriveConfig) (config_validation.hpp:207-220) omits brake_ceiling_nm/brake_hold_* positivity and the engage<=release ordering that both post_init and decode_drive enforce — either mirror the checks there or document that DrivetrainWriter's typed path intentionally trusts decode-time validation; (3) qualify 'write-once inertial assignment' as XmlBodyElement-scoped, noting Element-level inertial() (xml_writer.cpp:114-119) is unguarded and set_inertia (:120-124) is the sanctioned replace path; (4) qualify suspension sync as apply_preset-scoped: current_preset does not reflect custom ctor configs and config/damper fields are publicly writable — suggest documenting the verbatim-vs-clamped divergence (test_schema.cpp:1839-1844 semantics) or adding a sync accessor; (5) note stroke-table memoization is verified for content, not cache behavior, and depends on solver immutability; (6) add a negative-path test hitting assign_budgets seal failures and a test that require_request_model rejects a mismatched model image.

# Stage 3 (`R1`) Independent Research Report — `P23`: Tyre Carcass/Bristle Lifecycles, Shared Writer Clock/Restore Protocol, `CruiseWriter` & GPU Batch Parity

- **Inspected Commit:** `0502660dd14bd1021b3da90a61e20dcb1d5805a1`
- **Compliance Verdict (`R1`):** `partially_violated`
- **Product Justification Verdict (`R1`):** `mixed_subclauses`

## 1. Producers, Mutators, Consumers & Potential Breakers
PRODUCERS/MUTATORS. All three tyre writers own per-writer clocks: TireWriter::last_time_s_ (native/src/writers/tire.cpp:620-621 try_compute gate, :657-659 advance gate throwing 'tire state can advance only once per increasing timestamp', :863 publishes last_time_s_=time after both wheels commit, :845-864 accumulate_validated two-side staging, :566-570 commit_prepared_state resets clock, :388 reset, :146-148 restart_clock decl tire.hpp); DistributedTireWriter mirrors it (:535-536 try gate, :596-598 throw gate, :898 publish, :399-405 commit swaps staged banks + clock reset, :316 reset, distributed_tire.hpp:109-111); PneumaticTireWriter (:830-831 try gate, :868-870 throw gate, :889 publish inside commit, :670-702 commit_prepared_state copies dec_delta_/dec_elastic_/dec_maxwell_ + rim fields + restore_patch_states from patch_stage_, :405 reset, :702 clock reset, pneumatic_tire.hpp:107-121). CruiseWriter mutates via state_ = next commit (native/src/writers/cruise.cpp:48-83 compute, :53 engaged gate, :67-69 pushing_further, :70-79 conditional integral + clamp, :81-83 commit, :86-90 reset, :114-123 set_state validation). Pneumatic internals: CarcassState rim FSM (native/src/pneumatic/carcass.cpp:221-241 restore_fields, :244-251 clear_rim_event, :257-303 update_rim_event FSM with np.argmax first-ray semantics, :200 exp(-dt/tau) Maxwell relaxation decay, :497-499 loss_factor hysteresis on carcass + rim elastic), bristle tangential law with dashpot term (native/src/pneumatic/brush.cpp:371-390 kBristleDampingS*(-filtered_v_s) element damping, :114-138 finite guards), patch slot pools (native/src/pneumatic/model.cpp:36-52 slot bank comment, :241-251 reserve patch_slots to bound, :300-311 slot key on unused slot, :448-453 evaluate bound check, :604-682 migrate/step/write slots, :773-775 evaluate_into bound check, :785-807 restore_patch_states bounded), patch-run extraction (native/src/pneumatic/geometry.cpp:309-339 patches_into output-capacity throw, :342-347 patches allocates). Radial laws: compliant hysteresis (native/src/contact/laws.hpp:48-62 normal_contact_hysteresis), distributed station hysteresis (native/src/tyre/station.cpp:77-100), tabulated (native/src/contact/tabulated_radial.cpp:38-49), GPU parity (native/src/gpu/tire_kernel.hpp:23-97). GPU runners: cpu_batch_runner.cpp:329-330 atomics, :334-493 worker_fn, :495-502 spawn+join before :515-519 delete; metal_batch_runner.mm:1791-1902 epoch/done_count pool, :1772-1778 gpu_ok warmup, :1906-1943 pipelined/serial loops, :1950-1955 stop_pool+epoch+join, :1959-2005 gated output writes, :2006-2007 suppress + :2017 return -7; JSON depth (native/src/gpu/model_assets.hpp:125 kMaxJsonDepth=64, :260-367 iterative scan_value, :272 frames array, :284-286 depth bound). CONSUMERS: stepper.cpp:461-504 TireWriterView std::visit dispatch incl. prepare/commit; runtime/step.cpp:1399-1404 compute vs probe fork, :1419-1426 committed-snapshot resistance feed, :1572-1582 cruise compute call (traction_limited hardcoded false), :2612 stored_energy wire, :2747-2748 loss accounting; initialization/equilibrium_cache.cpp:149-162 install_material ordering prepare->validate->drive restore->commit->commit->assign->restart clocks; initial_state.cpp:190-199 restore + clock restarts. BREAKERS: interval_clock::interval_id (native/src/interval_clock.hpp:21-39) throws on nonfinite/negative time, nonpositive dt, int64 overflow; validation::derived/nonnegative/positive throw on nonfinite (contact/laws.hpp:13-22 finite_fail overflow_error, :92-96 brush passivity violation throw); writer_config.hpp:453 + numerics/density_fit.cpp:19-20 reject damping_ns_m!=0 under Ban 39; pneumatic_tire.cpp:660-667 NaN-bristle-on-lumped-tier rejection.

## 2. Sub-Clause Evidence Analysis
(a) CLOCK: all three writers reject time <= *last_time_s_ before any mutation (tire.cpp:657-659, distributed_tire.cpp:596-598, pneumatic_tire.cpp:868-870) — strictly increasing, so the property's 'non-decreasing' holds a fortiori (equal timestamps are also rejected; double-advance is atomic). Publication happens only inside the post-both-wheels commit (tire.cpp:845-864, distributed :884-898, pneumatic :889). RESTORE: two-phase prepare_state(committing only via noexcept commit_prepared_state) exists on all three (tire.cpp:417-565 stage+validate, :566-570 noexcept commit; distributed :329-397, :399-405; pneumatic :489-668 dec_* staging + patch_stage_, :670-702). install_material (equilibrium_cache.cpp:149-162) sequences tire prepare BEFORE drive restore and tire commit AFTER, with a comment (:150-152) that all commits after the throwing stages are no-throw — all-or-nothing across owners provided callers follow the protocol; a caller invoking commit without successful prepare could commit stale/partial stage content for distributed/pneumatic scratch, but the protocol is contractual and all in-tree callers honour it. WARM ALLOC: warm try_compute_into paths are noexcept, construction-sized (probe_ third model for pneumatic), with BIKE_NONBLOCKING on stepper paths (rtsan.hpp:1-37); one defensive regrowth escape exists (step.cpp:1608-1615) which is documented unreachable on valid snapshots. Source-level evidence only — no runtime allocation counts observed in this audit. BAN #39: every radial law is rate-independent hysteresis F_el+eta*F_el*tanh(rate/eps), clamped at zero (laws.hpp:48-62, station.cpp:94-100, tabulated_radial.cpp:38-49, carcass.cpp:497-499, gpu/tire_kernel.hpp:97); stored energy = 1/2*k*delta^2 independent of rate. The legacy viscous normal_contact(delta,delta_dot,k,c) survives (laws.hpp:27-38) but only for rider pedal/rig uses (rider_contacts.cpp:526, legacy_controller.cpp:237, validation/rigs.cpp) — not tyre radial. damping_ns_m is a required config key pinned to 0 (density_fit.cpp:19-20, tire_kernel.hpp:23-25 comment). HOLDS. (b) PNEUMATIC LIFECYCLE: carcass Maxwell relaxation uses exp(-dt/tau) decay (carcass.cpp:200) with dt/dtheta/tau validated positive (:186-188); rim-strike FSM tracks first-engaged ray (np.argmax semantics :265-279), running peaks (:282-292), emits RimStrikeEvent on full release (:293-303), serialized via restore_fields (:230-241); bristle migration runs over bound-sized slot pools with pre-reserved rings (model.cpp:241-251, :448-453), wire restore bounded by prepare (pneumatic_tire.cpp:670-702, model.cpp:785-807); NaN wire encoding for inactive cells + lumped-tier NaN enforcement (pneumatic_tire.cpp:660-667); finite guards throughout brush.cpp (:114-138, :181-211, :300-306). HOLDS. (c) CRUISE: engaged = controller_grounded.value_or(rear_in_contact) (cruise.cpp:53), disengage zeroes torque + commits (:57-60). Integral updates iff !pushing_further && !traction_limited (:70-79), where pushing_further = |demand|>=ceiling && same_sign(demand,error) (:67-69). This is conditional-integration anti-windup: it freezes the integrator only when saturation pushes along the error. The property's literal 'iff engaged && !traction_limited && !saturated' is too strong — under opposite-sign saturation (demand clipped but opposing the error) the implementation still integrates. Matches the Python oracle and test test_exact_saturation_freezes_nonzero_error. Atomicity: all mutations target `next`; validation::derived on error*timestep/integral/demand throws on nonfinite BEFORE state_=next (:81-83); integral clamped to ceiling/ki (:77). Atomic on overflow/validation failure. PARTIAL literal deviation. (d) GPU: kMaxJsonDepth=64 bounds a fixed-size iterative frame stack (model_assets.hpp:125,272,284-286) — deterministic, allocation-free scan; each scan_value descent restarts the budget so a member value may nest a further 64 within a fixed 3-level find_member chain — bounded in total. Metal pool: stop_pool+epoch notify + join (:1950-1955) strictly before world_data/model deletion (:2010-2016) and before stack teardown — pool lambdas capture locals by reference and no return path exists between pool creation (:1853) and join. CPU runner joins all threads (:500-502) before deletes (:515-518). Output suppression: every output write sits inside if(gpu_ok) (:1959-2005), else branch only logs and returns -7 (:2006-2007, :2017). HOLDS.

## 3. Exceptions, Dependencies & Consequences
DEPENDENCIES: clock gates depend on d->time monotonicity supplied by the engine and on dt>0 (interval_clock.hpp:22-25 throws otherwise); the property's wheel-above-road and in-range-x assumptions are NOT enforced inside writers (wheel centers penetrating terrain are handled by the contact kernels' delta<=0 short-circuit, not rejected). Restore atomicity across owners depends on caller ordering (install_material sequence) — prepare/commit is a protocol, not enforced by the writer (commit_prepared_state after a failed prepare would publish partially-staged scratch for distributed/pneumatic). Cruise: production call site passes traction_limited=false unconditionally (step.cpp:1573) so the !traction_limited gate is dead in the shipped path — it is exercised only by unit tests; 'saturated' must be read as oracle anti-windup (pushing_further) not literal clip-anywhere. CONSEQUENCES: the literal-iff deviation means cruise integrator may grow while demand is clipped opposite to error — defensible (anti-windup semantics) and oracle-parity, but the property text overclaims. Brush tangential dashpot (kBristleDampingS, brush.cpp:377) is a bristle-shear dashpot, not a radial tyre damper — inside Ban 39's radial scope but is a rate-dependent loss term worth noting for energy accounting. geometry.cpp:342-347 patches() allocates a vector — cold convenience path only; warm path uses bounded patch_slots spans. GPU JSON depth is bounded per-descent (each member value restarts the 64-frame budget) rather than an absolute document bound — fine for the fixed find_member chains (state->model_dims->key, state->integration_state) but a hostile document could reach ~3*64 effective depth. cpu_batch_runner always writes outputs after joins (no gpu_ok concept) — a non-finite world is reported via out_all_finite flag, not suppressed; by design. No test runs performed — all findings are source-only evidence.

## 4. Test Coverage & Gaps
COVERED: test_native_tire.cpp:217-228 'once per increasing timestamp' rejection + :297-349 nonfinite/inf/max segment fuzz on clock; test_native_atomicity.cpp:241-248 duplicate-timestamp atomicity, :225-237 invalid-restore atomicity, :363-373 duplicate prepare timestamp; test_native_clock_contracts.cpp:88-159 interval_id half-even/fenv-independence, :277-307 pinned snapshot interval ids incl. int64 boundary (:329), :187-224 cruise timestep-only-when-integrating oracle check; test_native_cruise.cpp:276-295 exact_saturation freezes integrator both signs, :541 limited/engaged Case coverage, :513 support underflow preserves state; test_native_state_restore.cpp prepare/commit + rejected-restore cases incl. drive-side (geometric Jacobian :689-705); test_native_pneumatic_{carcass,brush,geometry,model,tire,wire}.cpp + test_pneumatic_tire_{forces,oracle,config}.cpp + pneumatic_reference_support.hpp + golden fixtures (pneumatic_tire_fixture.hpp) — oracle-parity suites for carcass/rim/brush/wire; test_gpu_model_assets.cpp:347-490 captured_errors incl. nesting_too_deep (100 nested '[' -> kParseError, outputs untouched), truncation, control-char, trailing garbage; test_gpu_batch_runners.cpp:346+ cpu_and_metal_hybrid_rollouts + argument validation (:290). GAPS: no test found asserting zero heap allocation on warm try_compute_into/RTSan paths — only rtsan_control.cpp as a deliberate-violation control for sanitizer qualification (the lane exists but allocation-freedom evidence here is source-level); no test observed suppressing partial outputs under an injected gpu_ok=false GPU failure (metal path untested for the failure branch); cruise traction_limited=true path has unit coverage but zero end-to-end coverage (production call hardcodes false); no test asserting the literal property form (integrator frozen under opposite-sign saturation) — tests assert the implemented oracle semantics; pneumatic NaN/bounds rejection covered for wire restore (lumped-tier NaN check) but runtime NaN-injection through brush migration paths is only guarded, not directly fuzz-tested per source review.

## 5. Recommended Refinement
1) Reword subclause (c) to match the implemented+oracle semantics: 'integrates iff engaged && !traction_limited && !(saturated && same_sign(demand,error))' — i.e. conditional-integration anti-windup — or, if literal !saturated is intended, change code and oracle; as written the property overclaims. 2) Either plumb a real traction_limited producer into runtime/step.cpp:1573 or mark the parameter intentionally-reserved — currently the gate is dead code in production. 3) Add an explicit zero-allocation assertion on the warm tyre paths: a test that counts allocations (matching allocator / RTSan lane run) across try_compute_into/probe_compute_into for all three backends, converting the step.cpp:1608-1615 regrowth guard from a silent escape into a fail-loud invariant. 4) Add a fault-injection test for the metal runner GPU-failure branch (gpu_ok=false -> no partial output writes + -7 + clean teardown) and a CPU-runner test pinning the all_finite-vs-output contract. 5) Consider making the JSON depth bound absolute per document (carry remaining budget across find_member/scan_value) or document the per-descent semantics, and add a test asserting effective worst-case depth. 6) Strengthen prepare/commit protocol safety: assert/reset staged buffers on commit_prepared_state entry (or a prepared_ flag) so commit-without-prepare cannot publish stale scratch, rather than relying on caller discipline. 7) Document in brush.cpp that kBristleDampingS is a bristle-shear dashpot outside Ban 39's radial scope, and confirm its dissipation lands in the energy ledger (brush_loss_step_j accounting).

# Stage 3 (`R1`) Independent Research Report — `P24`: Qualification Sweep Fail-Closed Verdicts, Summary Crash-Consistency & Container/Dependency Staging

- **Inspected Commit:** `0502660dd14bd1021b3da90a61e20dcb1d5805a1`
- **Compliance Verdict (`R1`):** `partially_violated`
- **Product Justification Verdict (`R1`):** `mixed_subclauses`

## 1. Producers, Mutators, Consumers & Potential Breakers
PRODUCERS of sweep summary JSON: run_sweep (TU kinds tidy/cppcheck/frontends/odr) native/src/tools/check_frontends.cpp:361-720, run_header_sweep :905-1244, run_scripts :1246-1393, run_diagnostic_controls native/src/tools/check_contracts.cpp:106-471, run_context_checks :517-1466 — all write <build>/native_check_summaries/<kind>.json via write_summary native/src/tools/check_reports.cpp:297-313 (direct ofstream+trunc, NOT staged); run_contracts :2465-2692 writes contracts.json via write_summary_atomic check_reports.cpp:315-334 (tmp+fs::rename). MUTATORS: each runner builds the summary Value incrementally via set_member; every summary is born fail-closed (tool_health="failed", finding_status="none", status="failed": check_frontends.cpp:384-386, 929-931, 1262-1264; check_contracts.cpp:126-128, 538-540) and only upgraded after evidence; count_suppression check_frontends.cpp:292-304. WORKER MERGE: for_each_index check_pool.cpp:31-58 (atomic index handout, first-exception stop, rethrow after join); workers fill per-index slots outcomes[]/slots[] merged in index order check_frontends.cpp:581-621 (TU), 1135-1173 (headers) — deterministic record order. CONSUMERS/WIRE: tools/run_native_checks.sh:56-77 drives the binary in fixed kind order, exits on first failure, appends per-kind timings to BIKE_GATE_TIMINGS_FILE; tools/run_tests.sh:117-120, 258-281 owns stage order + --from skipping + journal; tools/check_change.sh:48-50 produces --only-sources runs (partial-<kind>.json); check_<kind> CMake targets native/cmake/Checks.cmake:51-71; readers = tooling expect_json probes + humans. RESTORE/BREAKER boundaries: run_process check_reports.cpp:799-892 records launch_error/timed_out/returncode instead of throwing (process_health="ok" iff rc==0 && !timed_out && !launch_error :874-885); exceptions escaping runner bodies (check_jobs CheckError at check_frontends.cpp:578, for_each_index rethrow, odr link lambda :623-642, write_summary throws) bypass write_summary entirely -> no summary written for the run (main catch checks_main.cpp:250-255); write_summary itself truncates destination in place check_reports.cpp:304; write_summary_atomic never checks the staged stream's write before rename :322-334; container.sh is dead-coded at tools/linux/container.sh:19-24 (exit 3 before dispatch) so pull_paths_from_container rollback :134-154,223-245 is unreachable; link_deps.sh writes .complete AFTER the atomic mv publish tools/link_deps.sh:59-65 (ordering inverted vs claim); run_tests.sh --from exits between validation (line 135) and trap install (line 241) skip the PARTIAL marker and journal (e.g. mktemp/git/mkdir failures at :155-206). bt::run scoreboard: thread_local require_count fixture_test.cpp:42-46; require() counts before evaluating :77-81; run() returns 1 on executed==0 :57-75.

## 2. Sub-Clause Evidence Analysis
(a) PARTIALLY HOLDS. For the per-file sweep kinds the biconditional is exact: status=passed iff !tool_health_failed && !blocking_findings and finding_status=blocking iff blocking_findings else none (check_frontends.cpp:645-651; identical shape for headers :1176-1182 and scripts :1369-1373); run_sweep returns 0 iff status==passed :680,717-719 and every setup/validation failure returns 1 with a failed summary. --only-sources IS isolated: summary_kind = "partial-"+kind :372-373, selection=partial :427, restricted to cppcheck/frontends/tidy checks_main.cpp:215-217, empty selection throws CheckError :420-421 (fail-closed, no zero-unit green). Deterministic merge holds (per-index slots + index-order merge). BUT the bare iff does NOT describe every kind: diagnostic-controls leaves finding_status="none" forever (init check_contracts.cpp:127, never re-set) and adds control-pass conjuncts (status=passed iff failures.empty() && !tool_health_failed :429-433) — a failed control with healthy tools gives status=failed while tool_health=ok AND finding_status=none, breaking the stated iff; context likewise (init :539; status requires all sdk_controls in {passed,rejected,not-applicable} :1436-1442); contracts has a different schema entirely — no tool_health/finding_status members at all (:2663-2668,2671-2676), so the claim is schema-inapplicable there. 'tool_health orthogonal to negative diagnostic-control findings' is MOSTLY true but asymmetric: the libcpp negative control's EXPECTED nonzero rc + #error does not mark health (:399-402 — health only on !launch_ok||!timeout_clear||!guard_status), while positive controls' nonzero rc does (:222-223); however a FAILED negative expectation (!guard_status, incl. missing marker or clean compile) DOES poison tool_health (:401) whereas a positive-control marker miss with rc==0 does not (:219-223) — tool health is folded with control outcome for the guard only, not purely orthogonal. (b) VIOLATED for 8 of 9 kinds. write_summary check_reports.cpp:297-313 opens the destination itself with ios::trunc — a crash or concurrent reader mid-write sees a truncated/corrupt <kind>.json; only contracts.json is staged tmp+rename via write_summary_atomic :315-334, and even that path does not verify the staging stream before rename (:323-330: only the open is checked; a short write still publishes). 'Never left missing on mid-sweep error' holds for setup-phase errors (summaries written with status=failed+errors before return 1: check_frontends.cpp:470-488,549-560,1306-1346; check_contracts.cpp:149-156,574-581) but is violated for post-setup escapes: malformed BIKE_CHECK_JOBS throws at check_frontends.cpp:578 outside any catch, worker-lambda exceptions rethrown by for_each_index :581/:1135, and write_summary's own throw all reach main :250-255 with NO summary for the run (stale file remains); missing build dir returns 1 with no summary at all :365-368. (c) MIXED. run_tests.sh --from DOES print PARTIAL — the finish() EXIT trap prints 'PARTIAL run (--from %s): not a final verification' whenever from is set :229-231 — and journals the from-stage into timings.tsv field 6 (${from:--}) :234-237; run_native_checks.sh --from prints 'PARTIAL run' :78-80. Narrow gap: exits between --from validation (:135) and `trap finish EXIT` (:241) — mktemp/git/mkdir/getconf paths :155-206 — produce neither marker nor journal row. extract_deps.sh DOES verify before publishing: archive SHA-256 before extraction (tools/linux/extract_deps.sh:81-89 via acquire_deps.sh and :81), every required_files member's SHA-256 inside the staged root :153-157, license_files must exist under the dependency root :158-162, publish is a same-fs mv only after all checks :163, destination must be absent :76-77, traversal guards :33-37,72-75,129-131,154,159-161; acquire_deps.sh verifies the download's SHA before mv into _archives :82-89 and enforces https-only :72,83. link_deps.sh: claim is factually INVERTED — the atomic mv publish happens at :59/:62 and .complete is written INTO the published target at :65 (marker-after-rename, which is what makes it meaningful); the invariant is fail-closed (target lacking .complete is wiped and reacquired :46-51) and the only consumer of .complete is link_deps.sh itself :46. container.sh pull DOES implement staged-destination rollback (stage inside repo :159, all operands validated before publish :161-222, per-path existence recheck + rename publish :225-244, EXIT-trap reverse-order restore-from-backup or removal :134-154, pull_committed gate :245) — BUT the script is dead-coded: 'PLAN-CHANGE GUARD' echoes a disabled message and exits 3 before the command dispatch :19-24, so at this commit no pull (and no rollback) can run; the rollback test tools/linux/test_container_pull.sh (314 lines, covering cp-failure, staged-revalidation, publish-failure rollback of both operands, late-destination nesting, alias/hardlink/symlink/special rejections) is wired to NOTHING — no CMake, mise, or workflow references it. docs/development/linux.md:124-125 documents it is 'not a crash-atomic snapshot'. (d) HOLDS. bt::run returns 1 with 'FAIL test body ran zero checks' when the body executes zero bt::require calls (fixture_test.cpp:68-72); require() increments the thread_local scoreboard before checking :77-81 so even a failing check counts; exceptions -> FAIL 1 :61-67. In record mode golden compares return without counting :163-165,184-190, so record-mode golden-only tests also fail closed.

## 3. Exceptions, Dependencies & Consequences
EXCEPTION CHANNELS: CheckError (context/manifest/selection violations) and ContractError (contracts kind) — declared checks_internal.hpp:51-70; runner catches map them to failed summary + exit 1, main's catch-all prints 'native <kind> failed: <what>' and returns 1 checks_main.cpp:250-255; argparse errors exit 2 :52-56,207-217 (argparse parity); --check-child exec failure _exit(127) :69-75; process-level failures are recorded not thrown (run_process :840-861 catches invalid_argument/system_error/runtime_error into launch_error). DEPENDENCIES: sweeps depend on native_check_context.json + native_sources.json + compile_commands.json emitted at configure (Checks.cmake:44-45, BikeCheckContext.cmake) — load_context/load_source_manifest/load_entries fail closed on missing/malformed/stale/duplicate/unexpected selections (check_context.cpp:129-435+; load_entries requires manifest<->database TU-set agreement checks_internal.hpp:255-264); tool resolution env override -> context tool_overrides -> tool_defaults -> PATH (resolve_tool), wrong-family tool = selection error into errors[]+tool_health=failed (inspect_sweep_tool check_frontends.cpp:336-358, tested tooling_checks.cmake:2513-2523); BIKE_CHECK_JOBS malformed -> CheckError (check_pool.cpp:18-29) — note this escapes WITHOUT a summary (post-setup throw check_frontends.cpp:578). CONSEQUENCES OF VIOLATIONS: (i) a kill/crash during write_summary leaves a truncated summary the gate can mistake for the previous verdict's artifact — fail-open surface vs the claim; (ii) exceptions past setup silently leave the previous run's summary of record in place — a stale 'passed' survives a crashed run until the next complete write; (iii) write_summary_atomic's unchecked staging stream can publish a truncated contracts.json on ENOSPC-class failures; (iv) the timings.tsv journal marks --from runs by stage name but a --from run that exits before trap install leaves no row at all; (v) link_deps .complete gap (kill between mv :62 and marker write :65) is handled safely — target is wiped and reacquired next run :46-49; (vi) container.sh dead code makes the (correct, tested) rollback unreachable — the property holds in source but is runtime-vacuous at this commit; (vii) BIKE_GATE_TIMINGS_FILE rows are appended per kind only for kinds that ran (run_native_checks.sh:72-75), consistent with PARTIAL marking.

## 4. Test Coverage & Gaps
COVERED: verdict classification matrix on mock tools — rc127/rc2/rc139/warn/clean cases assert tool_health and finding_status independently (tooling_checks.cmake:946-1001); wrong-family tool -> health failed (:2513-2523); missing override -> failed status + zero TUs (:2494-2510); parallel deterministic record order + BIKE_CHECK_JOBS validation + blocking finding (tooling_sweep_engine.cmake:57-102); --only-sources partial-<kind>.json isolation, header-reaching selection, empty-selection error, kind restriction (tooling_sweep_engine.cmake:104-131); headers dedup/context-merge determinism (:144-244); real-build headers/diagnostic-controls/context sweeps assert ok health + expected controls incl. the negative libcpp control (tooling_checks.cmake:1522-1588); gate launchers: kind order, --from skip+PARTIAL, timings file, journal fields incl. the from column, quick/bogus/slow --from rejection, concurrent-gate share, check_change --only-sources routing (tooling_gate.cmake:86-288); harness fail-closed require proven via --require-failure (test_contracts.cpp:4308-4314 driven by test_native_loader_contracts.cmake:10-19, registered Phase3Ports.cmake:209); contracts-kind checks executed per CTest case via validate_ctest_contracts (check_contracts.cpp:2116-2168); offline lane pinned to extract_deps.sh + lock (tooling_platform.cmake:1506-1514), extract_deps.sh presence in source package (package_smoke.cmake:100); container.sh pull rollback covered ONLY by the orphaned shell fixture test_container_pull.sh (publication-failure rollback :200-206,289-305, cp-failure :171-179, nesting :299-305) — comprehensive but unwired (no CTest/mise/workflow reference) and currently non-runnable since the driver exits 3 at :23-24. GAPS: (1) no test asserts summary ATOMICITY — nothing kills a sweep mid-write_summary or pre-creates a torn file; the truncating write is untested because it is the mechanism; (2) no test for the post-setup escape paths that skip write_summary (malformed BIKE_CHECK_JOBS asserts stderr only — tooling_sweep_engine.cmake:85-89 — never checks whether a summary file was left/stale); (3) write_summary_atomic's unchecked staging write untested (no ENOSPC injection); (4) diagnostic-controls/context: tests assert the happy-path ok/none but no fixture forces a control failure with healthy tools to expose that status=failed can coexist with tool_health=ok AND finding_status=none (the iff gap); (5) --from pre-trap exit window (mktemp/git failure) untested; (6) bt::run zero-check branch (fixture_test.cpp:70-72) has NO dedicated test — nothing runs bt::run([] {}) expecting exit 1; (7) link_deps.sh has no test at all (ordering/backup/adoption paths unverified in-tree); (8) extract_deps.sh negative cases (hash mismatch, traversal, missing license) untested — only presence/usage contracts exist; (9) colab_gpu/*.py in scope has no property-relevant tests for staging/atomicity (test_model_assets.py exists for model_assets only).

## 5. Recommended Refinement
Split P24 into separate verifiable claims and tighten wording: (a) scope the iff to the per-file sweep + headers + scripts kinds ('exits 0 iff tool_health==ok AND finding_status==none for kind in {tidy,cppcheck,frontends,odr,headers,scripts}'); for diagnostic-controls/context state the real rule (status==passed iff tool_health==ok AND all controls passed; finding_status is constant 'none'), and exclude contracts (different schema, no health/finding fields). (b) as stated is FALSE: only contracts.json is written via staging+rename (write_summary_atomic); the sweep writers use write_summary which truncates the destination in place (check_reports.cpp:304). Either reword the property to 'every sweep summary is written before returning, with failed summaries recorded on setup errors' (what the code does) or, if crash-consistency is the real requirement, change write_summary to stage+rename like write_summary_atomic — and add a post-write stream check before rename to write_summary_atomic itself (:323-330). Also decide whether post-setup escapes must still write a summary (currently they do not: check_jobs throw at check_frontends.cpp:578, for_each_index rethrow) — recommend wrapping the post-setup body so an internal error still emits a failed summary. (c) fix the link_deps claim ordering: the code publishes the staged target by rename THEN writes .complete inside it (:62,:65); phrase as 'a target is consumable only when .complete exists, written last; incomplete targets are wiped and reacquired'. Note container.sh pull's rollback is unreachable at this commit (dead-code guard :19-24) — the claim should be conditioned on the Linux lane being re-enabled, and its orphaned test should either be wired (e.g. a scripts-level harness) or noted as disabled-with-reason per the repo's red-test policy. (d) add a self-test: a case that runs bt::run with a no-op body and asserts exit 1 + 'ran zero checks' (currently untested). Additional test targets: forced control-failure-with-healthy-tools for diagnostic-controls/context to pin the real verdict rule; ENOSPC injection for write_summary_atomic; stale-summary detection when a sweep crashes post-setup.
